Yelemiya 1

1 Citabu cici ca Yelemiya, cikulolosa zinghani zono zalongwa na Yelemiya u mwana wa Hilikiya, u muyagwe ne wakohani we cikaya ca Anatoti, ca mu yisi ya Benyamini.
2 Muwaha Mulungu yalonga na Yelemiya mu mwaka we likumi ne 'datu, wa mu wuteme wa Yosiya, u mwana wa Amoni, lono yali mutemi wa Yuda.
3 Muwaha Mulungu yalonga kanyhi na Yelemiya mu vilo vya Yehoyakimu mwana wa Yosiya lono yali mutemi. Na ku wulawa mu vilo vivyo, Muwaha Mulungu yakajendelela du kulongana na Yelemiya kenji na kenji, sunga mu mwaka we likumi na monga wa mu wutemi wa Zedekiya u mwana wa Yosiya. Mu mwezi we 'hano wa mu mwaka wono i wanhu wa Yelusalemi wahamizwa no kutegulwa ku Babiloni.

U lucemo lwa Yelemiya yawe muwizi

4 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya nhavi,

5 “Ane nali nakondya kukuhagula,

na lono nali nghali hamba kukuwumifya kose.

Na lono wali wukali hamba nalelwa,

ane nali nakondya kukusiwanula wuwe muwizi kuli makungugo.”

6 Nane nghamupitula nghatya,

“Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, ane nha si manyile lu kulonga,

nha nghali nili mudodo muno.”

7 Ninga nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakamhitula yakatya,

“Gwe Yelemiya, wulece kutya vyono agwe wukali mudodo,

langa wubite du kuli wanhu kono nghukutuma,

wukawalonjele zinghani zose zono nghukulajiliza wukazilonje.

8 Holeka wulece kuwogopa i wanhu wawo,

hambi ane nha nowa hamonga nagwe ho nghudime.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

9 Hanyuma ya zizo, u Muwaha Mulungu yakawugolola muwoko wakwe, yakamhamiza mu mulomo wangu, yakanyitya,

“Langa ta, alu, nghukutajila zinghani zangu mu mulomo wako zono kosuma kuzilonga.

10 Nane i zuwali, nghukupela wulajilize kuli makungugo,

hamonga ne matemi gose.

Ho wusume kukula, no kumwagula,

no kongomeza, hamonga no kuhodanga,

ho wuzenje no kuhadika.”

U wugubulilwe we hejete

11 Hanyuma ya zizo, u Muwaha Mulungu yakambuza yakutya, “Gwe Yelemiya, wukuwona cici?” Nane nghamupitula nghatya, “Ane, nghuwona cinhambi co mubici wo mulozi.”
12 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanyitya, “Heya kulonga mbeka, holeka nane nindilize sunga nghani yangu yifisilizwe.”
13 Hanyuma ya zizi, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe Yelemiya, wule, wukuwona cici kanyhi?” Nane nghamupitula nghatya, “Gwe Muwaha Mulungu, ane, nghuwona nyungu ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, yono yina malenga gono gakuhemula, no mulomo wayo, wulanjile kukuno.”

14 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanyitya, “Ale, wongomele wolawilila ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, wuwongomeze i wanhu wose, wono wekaye mu yisi yiyi.
15 Hambi 'ne, nha nogacema i makungugo gose, gono gekalaga ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, gaze. Ne watemi wawo, wologasimya i magoda gawo go wutemi, mu malango ga Yelusalemi, no kwidoma ne majinji gayo gose, mbavu zose. Hamonga no kugapilima i makaya gose ga Yuda.
16 Nane, nowalipiciza i wanhu wangu, ku soko yo wubi wawo wono wetuma. Hambi nha wandeka 'ne, wakawa kuyilavizya makumbiko, i milungu yinji, kanyhi wakenozeza ne vihwani viseswe, no kuvihemba.
17 Holeka, aluno wiwikanizaje 'gwe Yelemiya, wubite wukawalonjele zinghani zose, zono ane nghukulajiliza wukazilonje. Ninga luno, wulecele lukulu kuwogopa. Na hono wuwogope, nane nokunoza wucile kuwogopa lono wuli hali wene.
18 Ninga wuhulicize Yelemiya! I wanhu wose wa mono yisi yiyi, nha cinji, i watemi, ne wawaha we wanhu, ne wakohani, hamonga ne wanhu wose wokugaluka. Ninga nane izuwali, nghukupela i vilungo vyo kulewana nawo. Nagwe kokwihwana ne likaya lyono lina magomawakali, ne lisumbili lye cuma, ne lijinji lya shaba.
19 Holeka wene wolewana nagwe, ninga si wokusumila lukulu. Hambi ane nha nowa nili hamonga nagwe ho nghudime. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 2

Lidimo lyo Mulungu kuli wanhu wa Isilayeli

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela,
2 niyiganule nghani yiyi kuli wanhu wose wono wekaye mu Yelusalemi niwatye, Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ane nghumbuciye vyono, lono wali wukali mudodo,

wali wuli mwihuwilwa muno,

na vyono lono cepogozeza ane na agwe, wali wunyendile muno.

Hambi nha wanhyatya,

cikakola wose mu nyika mu yisi yono yali yikali hamba nahadikwa cinji.

3 Gwe Isilayeli, yo gwegwe du wuli wene

mono wali wuli wangu,

yo gwegwe mono wali yo sawo yangu siwanulwe.

Nane nghawaletela magazo no wongomezwe,

i wanhu wose wono wakutamisaga.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

U wubi we walezi we Waisilayeli

4 Nye nyo lulelo lwa Yakobo, ale muhulicize ta, zono Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga, anye nyo we mhuga yose ya Isilayeli.
5 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Alu i walezi wenyu wambona nina lisutyaci?

Hamba nghani yono yikaleka wene wakundeka 'ne?

Hambi wene nha wakahemba vihwani viseswe vyono visina cambuzi,

na weneco wakawa wasina cambuzi.

6 Hamba si wenda no kunyhelelela yaye,

hamba yiwe yo nene mono nawaponya ku wulawa ku yisi ya Misili

nghawatonga mu mbago,

mu yisi ye nyika ne manhimbwisa,

mu yisi nghavu, yono yina mbali mbaha.

Yisi yono si yikalagwa na munhu wo wose

hamba hasina munhu wo wose mono kolo

kola mu yisi yiyo.

7 Nane nghawaleta sunga ku yisi yono yina moso muno,

ho wayisangalalile mikalo yayo,

hamonga ne vinhu vinji viswanu vyono vili mumo.

Ninga wene wakayihodanga yisi yiyo,

wakayavufya i yisi yono nawapela.

8 Ne wakohani nawo, hamba si wawuza watye,

‘Alu u Muwaha Mulungu yali hayi lo?’

Heya si wasuma kuwuza,

hambi i wejizi wangu wo wulajilize nha si wali wamanyile 'ne.

Nawo i walongola wakambedegala 'ne,

i wawizi nawo, wakawa wakuwilila mwi'tagwa lya Baali,

wakawa wakuvihemba vihwani,

vyono si vikwiliye cinji co cose.

U Muwaha Mulungu yakuwasitaka wanhu wakwe

9 “Ale, ku soko ya zizo,

holeka nane no Muwaha Mulungu cokwimana ne wanhu wangu,

cilongane calo nawo.

Kanyhi niwahose hamonga ne wana wawo.

10 Aluno, wubite ku lubavu lwa kumwezi ku vizingwa

vya Kupulo, wumutume yunji yabite ku cilima ku yisi ya Kedali.

Nagwe kowona nha vyono,

kusina nghani yinji yono yihwanile yiyi, yono yejela kuko.

11 Vyono hasina hamba likungugo linji lyo lyose,

lyono lyakondya kuyikawaniza milungu yalyo muli lyene,

hamba yiwe milungu yiyo si ya mbeka yaye.

Ninga i wanhu wangu wakondya kundeka'ne,

no Mulungu mono nawaletela lilumbo,

wakayityatya i milungu yono si yosuma hamba kuwataza cinji yaye.

12 Holeka nanye nyo wulanga mucicime so woga, kanyhi mwizine,

mwinjilwe no wucelewale so zinghani zizi.

13 Hambi i wanhu wangu nha wetuma wubi wuli hejete,

wandeka 'ne mono nili muvwilo we malenga go wumi.

Wene wehimbila visima vyono vikung'welela mbela mbela,

vyono si vyosuma kwikala na malenga.

Cambuzi ce Waisilayeli, so wu wula wihuwilwa wawo

14 “Ninga Isilayeli, si muwanda yaye,

hamba si yalelelwa mu wuwanda yaye.

Alu ku sokoci i walugu wakwe wakumupelemba?

15 Alu lanje, wakumukolomela wenha simba.

Ne yisi yakwe, wayinoza yawa nyika,

ne vikaya vyayo, vyaweza matongo du,

gono gagwiye hasi no kulekwa.

16 Heya, sunga ne wanhu wa ku Memufisi,

ne wanhu wa ku Tapenesi,

wakondya kulitula lindosi lyakwe.

17 Gwe Isilayeli, alu zizi,

nha kwiletela gwegwe go mweneco!

Hambi nha kundeka 'ne no Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wako, mono nakulongoza agwe mu nzila.

18 Alu mwene 'gwe, wukwihwesa kotuga cambuzici,

hono wubite ku Misili,

wukang'we i malenga ga mwi'kolongo lya Nile?

Hamba wukwihwesa kotuga cambuzici lo,

hono wubite ku Ashuli,

wukang'we i malenga ga mwi'kolongo lya Efulata?

19 Ninga u wubi wako, wo wokulipiciza, no wibavye

wako, nawo wo wokuling'aniza 'gwe.

Nagwe baho ho kokwifundila u wubi we nghani yiyi,

hamonga no wusungu wo kundeka 'ne, ane no Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wako,

no wuwe wako wo kuwa wusina cihuliko ca kuli'ne kanyhi.

Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga.”

Waisilayeli wakulema kumuhemba u Muwaha Mulungu

20 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Agwe Isilayeli, wulawa katali walema wutonji wangu,

wukalema kuhulika 'ne no kuhemba nene.

Langa wukawa kubita mwi'tunda litali,

na mu masina ge mabici gono gadedewaye,

wukawa kuhemba i milungu yinji.

21 Ninga ane ho nakuhadika 'gwe nha muzabibu muhagulwa,

mu wulawa mu mbeyu swanu muno.

Ninga lu lanje vyono weza winha!

Weza winha muzabibu wono wudodile

wono wusina kanyhi cambuzi co cose.

22 Holeka alu, hamba wikweme ne sabuni nghali muno,

ninga u wubi wako vyo wowa wijeye du ha meso gangu.

23 Alu mwene, ho kosuma nhawule kutya,

vyono agwe si wiyavufye yaye,

hamba wukali nahembela lukulu Baali?

Ninga wulanje vyono wetumila wubi mwi'lolo,

wuziwone ta zono wetuma kuko!

Agwe wehwana ngamiya, yono yilecigwe si mbope,

yikumangha caka du ako na ako!

24 Wihwanile nyhenje yono yizoweye nyika,

yono yinusaga mbeho ako na ako so masulumizi gayo,

alo nani mwene mono kosuma kuyimila u wusulumizi wayo?

Kanyhi nha hasina nume yono yogazika lono yikuyilondola

hambi yene nha yijelaga du nhondo zose mu madaha gayo.

25 Gwe Isilayeli, wulece kuyisakaza migulu yako bwete,

hamba kwipela nyhilu bwete, so wulondolela milungu yinji.

Hambi agwe nha wukutya, ‘Ane nalema kukuwuyila,

hambi nha niyendile i milungu mijenzi,

holeka nane noyityatya du.’ ”

Isilayeli wanojeye walipicizwe

26 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ninga nha vyono u muhizi kakolwaga cevu lono yebatwa,

ale na nyenye nhavivyo nyo wanhu wa

Isilayeli, nanye molokolwa cevu,

hamonga ne watemi wenyu,

ne wawaha wenyu, ne wakohani wenyu, hamonga ne wawizi.

27 Nanye wose molokozwa cevu,

anye wose mono mutezaga u mubici awulya,

yo sogo yenyu, ne libwe alilya, yo nyina yenyu.

Na zizi zolomwijelelenyi vyono anye mwakondya kundeka 'ne,

hamba si mukusaka no kumbuyila yaye.

Ninga kolo mwenjila mu wugaye,

hobaho mukuwa kunondola ho nize nimuponyenyi.

28 “Alu i milungu yono mweyinozeza nyenye nyo weneco, yili hayi lu?

Nanye kolo mwenjila mu magayo,

mweze yileka le yimuponyenyi,

hono mbeka yosuma kumuponyenyi!

Nagwe Yuda, wina milungu minji muno,

hambi liwalo lye milungu yako,

nha liling'aniye ne liwalo lye zikaya zako.

29 Alo mwina nghanici nane?

Hamba nhawule mweza musina cihuliko kuli'ne?

30 Nghamulipicizenyi,

ninga wulipicize wangu wono nghamulipicizenyi,

hamba si wakwila cinji yaye,

nanye hamba si mwenda ho nimugomolenyi.

Mukawawulaga i wawizi wenyu,

mukehwana nhe simba yono yisagahaye muno.

31 Nye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli,

muhulicize ta zono nghulonga ye.

Mwene ane, nha cinji nehwana nhe nyika hongo kuli'nye,

hamba naweza nha wutitu kuli'nye,

ne yisi ye zimbali?

Alu nhawulenyi mukutya mokwituma nha vyono mwendile nyenye,

si mowuyila lukulu kuli'ne kanyhi ng'o?

32 Alu mwene mukwihwesa

nha vyono muhinza kosuma kuwusemwa wihambo wakwe hongo?

Hamba bibi halusi kocisemwa i civwalo cakwe ce halusi hongo?

Ninga anye nyo wanhu wangu,

mbeka mwakondya kusemwa'ne vilo vinji, vyono visina liwalo.

33 Ninga 'nye langa muli wasugu du,

ku wulondola zo wusenyha.

Sunga ne wacekulu wabi,

wosuma kwiyijiza ku wulawa kuli'nye.

34 Sunga ne vivwalo vyenyu vikwilile sakami ye waciwa,

wono wasina masutya,

hamba si sakami ye wahizi yaye,

i wabena zikaya za wanhu. ‘Ninga hamonga na zizi,’

35 anye mukutya nhavi,

‘Ane sina masutya go gose, holeka u Muwaha Mulungu,

si kongholelwa likuwo yaye.’

Ninga mumanye vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu,

nomulipicizenyi du,

hamba yiwe nyenye nyo weneco,

si mukulumba yaye vyono mwitumile wubi.

36 Hambi nha mweyibedegala nyo weneco,

mukawa kuyipitucila i milungu ye makungugo ganji.

Ninga mumanye vyono molokozwa cevu ne yisi ya Misili,

nha vyono mwakozwa cevu ne yisi ya Ashuli.

37 Molosumwa ne Wamisili, mukolwe cevu.

Ninga ane no Muwaha Mulungu,

nane nawalema wawo wono muwetumbiye,

hambi nha si motuga cambuzi co cose kuli wene.”


Yelemiya 3

Isilayeli mono si mwihuwilwa

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Hono munhu yamulece mugolece wakwe no kumupela talaka,

nayo mucekulu yuyo ho yabite yakatolwe na munhu yunji,

hodu u munhu yuyo si kosuma kumuwuyila kanyhi u mucekulu yuyo.

Ku soko hono yanoze nhavivyo,

nghani yiyo, yoyavufya yisi muno muno.

Gwe Isilayeli, alu nha watuga wendece wenji muno muno,

kanyhi kunji ho wukusaka wumbuyile'ne!

2 Alu wugalamile ta wulanje mu matunda mucanyha, wule,

ho hali hanhu hanji hono agwe si wetuma nha munhu musenyha?

Hambi agwe nha wekalaga ha mhelo ye zinzila wuwalindiye wendece wako,

wukawa wehwana u mwalabu mono kehundikaga mu nyika,

ho yawatendele vibi i wakola nzila.

Nagwe Isilayeli wakondya kuyavufya yisi,

ku soko yo wusenyha wako.

3 Na ku soko yiyo,

holeka nayo mvula si yikutonya,

sunga ne mahalavu gose hamba si gakuza yaye.

Hambi nha wehwana i wanhu wasenyha,

akuno wusina hamba cevu yaye.

4 “Na kanyhi vyo wukali du kunyitya,

‘Agwe wuli Baba,

hambi nha wali wunyendile 'ne ku wulawa wudodo wangu.

5 Holeka si kosuma kwikala ne likuwo lyako nhondo zose,

hamba kungholelwa likuwo cibitilila.’

Heya 'gwe Isilayeli, nhavivi vyo kulongaga,

ninga akuno vyo wukwituma ne makani mabi du,

nha vyono wukusaka gwegwe.”

Isilayeli na Yuda yiwanojeye wanene

6 Kanyhi mu vilo vyo mutemi Yosiya, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Alu wule, waziwona zono yetumile Isilayeli, mono si mwihuwilwa? Hambi nha yakondya kundeka 'ne, kawa kubita mu matunda matali gose, na hasi ye mabici gose gono gena mahamha gadedewaye, kawa kwituma zo wusenyha wakwe.
7 Nane nghawa kwihwesa nha vyono, kolo yakukondya kwituma zizo zose, nhondo ho kosuma kumbuyila'ne. Ninga mwene hamba si yasuma kumbuyila 'ne hamba nhavi, sunga no lumbulye Yuda mono si mwihuwilwa, nayo yakaziwona zizi zose.
8 Nayo Yuda, yaziwona, nha vyono nakondya kumuleka Isilayeli, namupela talaka, na vyono namuwinga, ku soko yandeka 'ne, no kuzityatya zo wusenyha. Ninga nayo Yuda munghalo lumbulye Isilayeli mono si mwihuwilwa, nayo hamba si yatuga woga yaye. Langa nayo yakawa musenyha,
9 akuno yasina ne cevu yaye. Yakayavufya yisi, yakasenyha mu nzila yo wuhemba mabwe ne mibici.
10 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, mwene Yuda u lumbulye lya Isilayeli mono si mwihuwilwa, nayo yakenoza nha yakumbuyila 'ne, ninga hamba si yali mbeka, langa ko wulambi du. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

11 Hanyuma kanyhi, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela, nha vyono, hamba yawe Isilayeli yali yakondya kumuleka, ninga hamba nhavivyo, kelajila nha vyono yali ling'ani si yenha Yuda mono si mwihuwilwa.
12 Holeka yakawa kunyitya nha vyono mbite nghamulonjele Isilayeli nimutye nhavi,

“Agwe Isilayeli, mono si wuli mwihuwilwa,

ale wumbuyile 'ne.

Hambi ane nha nili lusungu,

hamba si nokolwa ne likuwo,

hamba kukukolelwa likuwo cibitilila.

13 Langa ane yono nghusaka kuli 'gwe,

wuvumile masutya gako gose nha vyono wali wamubedegala u Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu weko.

Kanyhi wunene,

nha vyono wali kusenyha ne milungu mijenzi,

hasi ye mibici yose yono yina mahamha gono gadedewaye,

hamba kugebata malajilizo gangu yaye.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

14 “Nye nyo wanhu mono si muli wehuwilwa,

ale mumbuyilaje 'ne, hambi nha muli wangu.

Nane nomusolenyi munhu yumonga yumonga,

mu wulawa mu makaya gose.

Kanyhi nosola wanhu wejete wejete,

mu wulawa mu milango yenyu yose.

Nane nomugomolenyi sunga kwi'tunda lya Sayuni.

15 “Nane nomuwicilenyi watemi wono wowa wena cihuliko kuli'ne. Nawo womutemenyi mu nzila ye mahala no wizuce.
16 Nanye kolo mweza kuwa mwenjipa, no kuwa wenji muno mu yisi yiyo, ne wanhu hamba si wololonga kanyhi ze Lisanduku lyangu lye Cilagane yaye, hamba si wolowa kulihwesa kanyhi hamba kulikumbucila kose, hamba kulisaka kanyhi, hamba kuwa no wuhile wo kunoza Lisanduku linji kanyhi lye Cilagane.
17 Ne vilo vivyo kolo vyakondya kuza, Yelusalemi nhondo, yolocemwa, ‘I ligoda lyo wutemi lyo Muwaha Mulungu.’ Ne makungugo gose golokwikongholela baho gawe kuhemba nene du. Nawo lulo, hamba si wolokwitumila lukulu kanyhi za mu cibede cawo, hamba i zimbi za mu zinhumbula zawo.
18 Mu vilo vivyo, i wanhu wa Isilayeli ne wanhu wa Yuda, wolokwilunza hamonga. Nawo wolowukana hamonga ku wulawa ku wuhamywe, mu yisi yo lubavu lwa kusumuka, wawe kuwuya kanyhi ku yisi yono ane nawapela i walezi wenyu yiwe wuhazi wawo.”

Wanhu wo Mulungu wakuhemba vihwani

19 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Gwe Isilayeli,

ane nasakaga nghubocele 'gwe kotya wuli mwanangu,

nghupele ne yisi swanu muno,

i hanhu haswanu hono hacizize yinze yose.

Ane nasakaga agwe wunyheme'ne, baba,

ho wulece kundeka 'ne kanyhi.

20 Ninga kumbe nagwe,

wukawa si wuli mwihuwilwa kuli'ne yaye,

wukehwana nho mucekulu musenyha.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

21 Ne lizi likuhulikwa ku wulawa mu matunda mucanyha.

Lizi lye wanhu wa Isilayeli, so wulile wawo,

no wulombe wawo.

Hambi nha wekalaga mu mikalile yo wubi du,

wakondya kumusemwa u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wawo.

22 Holeka anye wose,

mono mwali mwakondya kumuleka u Muwaha Mulungu wenyu,

alu mumuwuyilaje nhondo.

Nayo komuponyenyi,

no kumunozenyi muwe wehuwilwa kuli mwene.

Nanye mukutya,

“Heya cikuza kuli'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi 'gwe nha yo gwegwe go Muwaha Mulungu wetu.

23 Hambi nha si casuma kutuga wutazi wo wose

kuli wuhembi we wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu

wono cahembaga mucanyha ye vizuli vye matunda.

Hambi u wutazi kuli Isilayeli,

nha wulawaga kuli Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu du.

24 “Ninga u wuhembi wo kumuhemba Baali, u mulungu we cevu, wakondya kucinoza ciwe matewa gono gajiliye. Hamonga ne wana wetu we cilume na we cicekulu, cakondya kuyajiliza sunga ne milimo ye walezi wetu yono wetuma, ku wulawa vilo vye katali.
25 Holeka alu yicinojeye cigone hasi so cevu cetu, cilece zisoni zetu zicifunice. Hambi ase hamonga ne walezi wetu, nha cikalaga cikwituma wubi du hali Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu, kanyhi hamba si cigebate ne malajilizo gakwe.”


Yelemiya 4

I wanhu wakucemwa wanene

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Nye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli,

hono muwe mukusaka kumbuyila,

ale heya mumbuyilaje.

Na hono musace kuwa wehuwilwa kuli'ne,

muwe kuviwusa ne vihwani vyenyu viseswe,

vyono nane si nivyendile,

2 ale, yowa viswanu kuli'nye mwilahizize mwi'tagwa lyangu.

Na mu nzila yiyo i makungugo gose wosaka'ne,

niwapele wulugutwe, nawo womhela lukumyo.”

3 U Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela i wanhu wa Yuda na wa Yelusalemi,

yakutya nhavi, “Ale, mulime luno i migunda yenyu yono yikali si nalimwa,

mulece kuhadika mbeyu zenyu mu mamivya.

4 Mukamise i cilagane cenyu cono calagana nanye,

ane no Muwaha Mulungu wenyu.

Kanyhi mwiyilavye kuli'ne nyenye nyo weneco,

anye nyo wanhu wa Yuda na Yelusalemi.

Ninga hono mwijelele du,

mulece kunoza nhavivyo,

likuwo lyangu lyokwaka nha moto,

ku soko ye metumo mabi gono mwetuma.

Na kolo likuwo lyangu lyaka,

hasina hamba yunji wo wose mono kosuma kulituza yaye.”

Wa Yuda wakogofywa no wulugu

5 Ale, mutowe zindulele mu yisi yose ya Yuda,

muzoze muno muno.

Muwalonjele i wanhu wa Yuda na Yelusalemi,

watizize mu makaya gono gena magomawakali.

6 Mutyatye nzila yono yidomile ku Sayuni!

Mutize, ho mwiponye, mulece kucela!

Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu nha koleta magazo

hamonga no wongomele wuwaha ku wulawa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka.

7 Hambi mono yapejigwe u mulimo wuwo wo kongomeza,

nha yakondya kuwikanizwa,

nayo yehwanile simba yono yikuza yikulawilila ha wihundiko wayo.

Yakuza yayongomeze Yuda.

Ne makaya gose ga Yuda gololekwa matongo,

hasina hamba munhu monga mono kolokwikala mumo.

8 Holeka luno, muvwale magunila,

mulile ko wusungu wuwaha.

Ku soko vyono likuwo liwaha lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

likali si nawaleka i wa Yuda.

9 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Mwi'zuwa lilyo, i watemi, hamonga ne wetumami wawo, wolowula vilungo, i wakohani nawo, wolotinuswa, i wawizi nawo, wolocelewala.”
10 Nane nghamupitula nghatya, “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, alu wawakonga i wanhu wawa wa Yelusalemi! Hambi nha wawatya vyono holowa ho wuli mulamu du, ninga mumaje wolowa muwicilize ha mimelo yawo.”

11 Na mu vilo vivyo, i wanhu wa Yelusalemi, wololonjelwa nha vyono, i mbeho yo moto yikuputa, yikulawa ku nyika yikuwaziza wene. Kanyhi hamba si mbeho du, yono yinha civulavumbi cono ciputaga du no kweza, aa ng'o.
12 Langa yowa mbeho yono yikuziza mu wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu. Nayo yowa mbeho yono yili vilungo muno si nhe civulavumbi! Nayo yo Muwaha Mulungu du, mono kolavya ze ning'aniza kuli wanhu wakwe.

Yuda yikupilimwa ne walugu

13 Ale, wulanje ta,

i walugu wakuza wenji muno wenha mavunde.

Ne magali gawo go wulugu,

gehwanile libeho lyono likupilima-pilima,

sunga ne zifwalasi zawo,

zikumangha musunho muno muno kuciza 'ndi.

Woyi hee!

Alu langa cajilila!

Alu ho cawona wusungu!

14 Gwe Yelusalemi,

kweme mabi gose gono gali mu nhumbula yako,

ho wupone.

Alu ho kojendelela kwihwesa makani mabi sunga nili?

15 Hambi lizi lye wanhu nha likuganula zinghani zimbi ku wulawa kwi 'kaya lya Dani,

na mu matunda ga Efulayimu.

16 I wanhu wawo, waza kuwasugula we makungugo,

no kuwalonjela wono wekaye mu Yelusalemi,

nha vyono i walugu wakuza,

wakulawa ku yisi yo kutali.

Ne walugu wawo kolo wakuza,

wologazozeza i makaya ga Yuda.

17 Woloyipilima Yelusalemi nha wanhu wono wawulindilize mugunda,

hambi i wanhu wayo,

nha wakondya kumbedegala ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

18 Nagwe Yuda, yo gwegwe mono wileteye zizi go mweneco,

ku soko ye mikalile yako mibi,

na ku soko ye makani gono wetuma.

No wubi wako wo wukuleteye magazo gaga,

nago gokucinya sunga mo mu nhumbula yako.

U wukalale wa Yelemiya, ku soko ye wanhu wakwe

19 Nye nghuwona wusungu si!

Hamba si nosuma kuwukangalila yaye!

Nye nhumbula yangu si!

Nhumbula yangu, nha yatowa muno!

Nane hamba si nosuma kunyamala yaye.

Hambi nha nghuhulika mazi ge zindulele hamonga ne mazozo go wulugu.

20 Ligazo ali likuza, hanyuma linji nalyo likutyatya,

sunga ne yisi yose nhondo, yalekwa matongo du.

Ne mahema getu gongomezwa musunho,

ne mapaziya getu gademulwanga vitema vitema.

21 Alu, nojendelela kuwuwona wulugu wukali nhavivi sunga nili?

Hamba nojendelela kuhulika mulilo we zindulele sunga nili lo!

22 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Wanhu wangu walele muno muno,

hambi nha si wamanyile 'ne.

Wehwanile i wana wadodo wono wasina mahala,

kanyhi wasina hamba wizuce bwete.

Langa wene wasugu du ku wituma zimbi,

ninga ku wituma ziswanu, kwawafutila.”

Yelemiya yakugubulilwa zo wuze wo wongomele

23 Nane nali neza kuyilanga yisi,

nghayiwona yina wambuce du,

nako ku wulanga kwali kusina wuzelu.

24 Nali neza kulanga mu matunda,

nghagawona matunda gakucicima,

navyo vigongo vyazumhazumhaga du ako na ako.

25 Nghawona vyono hali hasina wanhu,

sunga ne zindeje zose zali zaguluka zatiza.

26 Nayo yisi yono yina moso, yakondya kuwa nyika,

ne makaya gayo gose gakondya kuwa matongo.

Ku soko ye likuwo liwaha lyo Muwaha Mulungu.

27 (U Muwaha Mulungu yali yalonga nha vyono yisi yose,

yolowa mbago yose, ninga hamba nhavivyo,

mwene hamba si koloyongomezeza lukulu yaye.)

28 Yisi yololila,

no wulanga wolotukuwala U Muwaha Mulungu vyo yakutya.

Nayo hamba si kolowukawaniziza lukulu u wuhile wakwe.

Mwene vyono yakondya kuzilonga, hodu yazilonga du,

hamba si kokawaniza kanyhi wulonje wakwe.

29 Ne wanhu wose wolotiza no kwiyagala ako na ako,

ku soko ye lizozo lye wapanda fwalasi,

ne waguma wupinde wanji wolotiziza ku mbago,

na wanji wolobita mucanyha ye mabwe mawaha.

Ne vikaya vyose vyololekwa visina wanhu,

na holowa hasina hamba munhu monga

mono kolokwinjila kanyhi yekale mumo.

30 Woyi he kuli'gwe, 'gwe Yelusalemi!

Alo nhawule wukwivwika vivwalo vye liwala lidunghu?

Hamba nhawule wukwihamba no wihambo we zahabu

no kwiyola mu meso gako ne lanji?

Ninga wumanye nha vyono winozelezi wako wuwo,

wowa nghani bwete du.

Hambi i weyendi wako,

nha wakondya kukubedegala, nalu wakusaka wakuwulaje.

31 Nane hulika cililo,

kotya nha cililo ca munhu wa cicekulu,

mono yali mu nghunga yo lusawo.

Cililo cinha co mucekulu mono yakulela mwanagwe mulongozi.

Kumbe cililo cico ca Yelusalemi,

mono yakukapilicila ha wehele,

akuno yalaze ne miwoko yakwe yakutya,

“Woyi nye, aga magayo gangu!

Hambi nhumbula yangu nha yikuzilika,

hambi nha weza kuza wambulaje.”


Yelemiya 5

U wubi wa Yelusalemi

1 Nye nyo wanhu wa Yelusalemi,

ale ta mumanyhe ako na ako mu zinzila zenyu!

Mulanje-lanje ako na ako! Mwiwonelele nyo weneco!

Mulondole mu sokoni zose!

Wule, mosuma kumuwona mbeka

u munhu mono yakwituma ze ling'ani

mono yakuhidimbala kuwa mwihuwilwa hali Mulungu?

Na hono mbeka mumuwone u munhu mono yenha nhavivyo,

nayo Muwaha Mulungu koyinenela Yelusalemi.

2 Hamba yiwe mukwiyitya mukumuhemba Muwaha Mulungu,

ninga mukwilonjela caka du,

hamba si mukulonga mbeka.

3 Mwene u Muwaha Mulungu kalangaga wihuwilwa,

hambi nha yamutowenyi, nanye hamba kuhulika ng'o,

yakamukunhulanjenyi,

ninga anye mukalema ku wiyijize.

Hambi nha mwali musina cihuliko,

hamba si mwasaka kuwuleka wubi wenyu.

4 Hobaho nane nghawa kutya,

“I wanhu awa mbeka waciwa kanyhi walele.

Hambi nha wakwituma zo wulele du,

siwamanyile yaye,

zono u Mulungu wawo yakuzisaka

hamba zono u Muwaha Mulungu yakusaka wene wetume.

5 Ane, nane nowabitila wanhu,

nghalongane nawo kwe vilungo,

hambi awo nha wazimanyile zono u Mulungu wawo yakuzisaka,

na zono u Muwaha Mulungu,

yakuwasaka wene wetume.”

Ninga wose wakawulema u wutemi wo Muwaha Mulungu,

wakalema sunga no kumuhulika kose.

6 Na yiyi yo soko yono yoleka simba zilawe

mu mbago zize ziwawulaje,

nago maminzi gololawa mu nyika gaze gawahawanje

no kuwalubutula mhonje mhonje.

Nago masuwi golokwiwendya-wendya mu zikaya zawo.

Nawo wanhu hono waze walawile kudeha,

gaze gawahawanje nhugu nhugu,

hambi u wubi wawo nha winji muno.

Nawo wakacila kumuleka Mulungu yizuwa ne'zuwa.

7 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakatya,

“Alu mwene,

ho nosuma wule kuwanenela wanhu wangu u wubi wawo lo?

Hambi wene nha wakondya kundeka'ne

nalu wakuyihemba i milungu yo wulambi.

Ninga ane ho nawalisa wanhu wangu sunga wakeguta,

ninga hamba nhavivyo, wene wakeyinjiza mu wusenyha,

wakawa kwikala mu zinyumba hamonga ne wasenyha.

8 Wali wenha kotya fwalasi zinume zono zigute

so masulumizi, mu madaha ga hakali mitondo.

Na muli wene wose,

munhu yakawa kumulilila mugolece wo muyagwe so masulumizi.

9 Alu nane hongo, ho ndece kuwalipiciza ku soko ye zinghani zizo?

Hamba ma!

I nhumbula yangu yilece kwilipiciza simbili mu nghungugo yono yinha yiyi?

10 “Alu nane nowatuma walugu,

waze wayidumulanje mizabibu ye wanhu wangu,

ninga si woyongomezeza lukulu yaye.

Nowalonjela vyono wayidumulilaje manhambi du.

Hambi i manhambi gago, nha si sawo yangu yaye.

11 Hambi anye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli na Yuda,

nha mwakondya kungalucila lukulu.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalema wanhu wa Isilayeli

12 I wanhu wo Muwaha Mulungu wakondya kumulema Mulungu wawo,

akuno wakutya,

“Hambi nha si kosuma kwituma cinji.

Nase hamba si cosuma kutuga magaziko yaye,

hamba si cosuma kuwa no wihomi, hamba kutuga nzala yaye.”

13 Kanyhi wakulonga wakutya nha vyono,

i wawizi wasina nghani yo yose,

langa wenha mbeho du,

wasina nghani yo yose yono yikulawa kuli Muwaha Mulungu.

14 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,

“Gwe Yelemiya, alu vyono i wanhu wawa wakulonga nha vivyo,

alu nane noyinoza inghani yangu yiwe nha moto mu mulomo wako.

Nawo wanhu wawa, wawe nha ngwagu,

nawo moto, wowatakamiza wose.

15 Nye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli,

u Muwaha Mulungu koleta likungugo ku wulawa kutali,

lize limuhomenyi.

Ne likungugo lilyo lyowa lili vilungo muno,

kanyhi lya katali.

Likungugo lyono ne milonjele yalyo,

anye si muyimanyile yaye.

16 Ne wasilikale walyo i waguma no wupinde,

nha vitung'ati muno, wono wawulagaga wasina lusungu.

17 Nawo wololya vya mu nhwanga yenyu,

hamonga ne zindigwa zenyu.

Wolowawulaga i wana wenyu we cilume hamonga na we cicekulu.

Wololya matewa ge zing'ombe zenyu,

ne zingholo zenyu.

Woloyihodanga ne mizabibu yenyu,

hamonga ne mibici yenyu ye mikuyu.

Ne makaya genyu gono mwitumbiye,

gono gena magomawakali, gose gago,

golokongomezezwa lukulu ne wasilikale wo wulugu.

18 “U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, ‘Ninga hamba nhavivyo mu vilo vivyo, si nolowongomezeza lukulu wanhu wangu.’
19 Ninga nawo kolo wakukuwuza wakutya. ‘Alu socici u Muwaha Mulungu kacinozeza zizi zose.’ Nagwe Yelemiya, wuwalonjele nha vyono, niwatendela zizo vyono wene wandeka, wakeyitumamila i milungu mijenzi mu yisi yawo weneco. Holeka luno nawo, wolowetumamila wajenzi mu yisi yono si yawo weneco.”

U Mulungu yakuwasugula wanhu wakwe

20 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

Ale, wuwalonjelaje wo lulelo lwa Yakobo,

hamonga ne wanhu wa Yuda, wutye,

21 “Ale muhulicize anye nyo wanhu walele,

mono musina nhumbula yo wizuce,

nyo wanhu mono mwina meso,

ninga si mukuwona, mwina makutu,

ninga si mukuhulika yaye.

22 Hambi ane nha yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

alu so cici si mukunyogopa yaye?

Hamba socici si mukucicima hali'ne ng'o?

Ane nha nawuwika musawawa wuwe wo mupaka we lilenga,

wuwe wo mupaka we vilo vyose,

wono lilenga si lyosuma kuwuponghola yaye.

Heya hamba mawingo galyo gatunhume muno muno,

ninga si gosuma kuponghola yaye.

Ne mawingo, heya gokoloma muno muno,

ninga hamba nhavivyo si gosuma kulapucila ku nhumbi yo mupaka yaye.

23 Ninga anye nyo wanhu, mbeka muli zeze,

kanyhi muliwabezi,

hambi nha mwakondya kundeka 'ne, no kupatucila kunji.

24 Musina ne lihweso hamba hadodo,

lyo kumhela cogopo,

ane mono nghumuletelenyi mvula ye mhinga

na ye mahwela mu vipango vyayo.

No kumupelenyi vilo vye nhwanga mu myaka yose.

25 Ninga 'lu, u wubi wenyu,

wakondya kumwimilenyi zizi zose iziswanu,

zono zali zinojeye muzituje.

26 Ne wanhu wabi nawo wekalane du ne wanhu wangu,

wekalaga wawatejeze,

nhe wanhu wono watandazaga zinyavu zawo ho wawagwize nhe zindeje,

ninga kumbe watandaze zinyavu zawo ho wagwize wanhu.

27 Kanyhi, wehwanile u mupelembi,

mono kamemyaga zindeje mwi'tundu lyakwe,

ale nhavivyo nawo vyo wamevizye mu zikaya zawo,

i vinhu vyono wavitecile.

Na yiyo yo soko yono yikuleka weza wali vilungo, kanyhi wagoli.

28 Nalu lanjeta, vyono wajinile,

wakung'alala nhavivi ku soko wasina wiyimile mu wituma zimbi.

Kanyhi hamba si wakusuma no kuwanozeza ling'ani

i wana wono wasina walezi wawo,

hamba kulajila ling'ani yaye, kuli wono wakwiwonelwa.

29 “Ninga nane, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, mbeka,

nowalipiciza du ku soko ye zinghani zawo zizo.

Na mbeka, nowalipiciza du likungugo lili.

30 Nalu mezino ne mawifyo gejela mu'si.

31 Hambi i wawizi nha weza wasina nghani zo kulonga,

langa wakuwila zo wulambi du.

I wakohani nawo, wakulondola zawo du weneco.

I wanhu wangu nawo, wasina

hamba zo kulonga muli zizo wene wakuwona zose swanu du.

Ninga lu magu ta,

hamba wolotendaci nhondo,

kolo zeza kuza baho ku ndumo yayo?”


Yelemiya 6

Yelusalemi yapilimwa ne walugu

1 Ale, mutize ho mupone,

anye nyo wanhu wa Benyamini!

Muwuce mu Yelusalemi.

Mutowe ndulele mu Tekowa,

mwinule citanjilo co moto mu Beti-hakelemu.

Hambi i mabi, hamonga no wongomezwe,

nha vikuza vikulawilila ku lubavu lwa kusumuka.

2 Ne likaya lya Sayuni,

hamba liwe yiswanu muno,

ninga lyolokongomezwa du.

3 Ne watemi wolokuza wasite nghambi baho he 'kaya,

hamonga ne wasilikale wawo wo wulugu.

Wolokoma ne mahema gawo walipilime likaya.

Na muli wene wose,

munhu wo wose kolosita nghambi yakwe hono kowa yakusaka mweneco.

4 Hanyuma yayo wolokwilonjela weneco wose wawe kutya,

“Ale, mwiwikanizaje luno,

cibite ciyihome Yelusalemi!

Nhondo cibite mu madaha ge cinyamihe.”

Ninga hanyuma kanyhi wolotya,

“Nye, ninga cacela muno, mulanje luno,

ne lizuwa lu lyeza kuswa,

sunga ne mibeho ye cinyamihe yeza kutalimha.

5 Holeka yowa ciba hono cilihome likaya mu madaha ge cilo,

cigongomeze ne magomawakali galyo.”

6 U Muwaha Mulungu yali yawalonjela

i watemi wawo wateme mabici,

wanozeze wunanucilo,

wo kulihomela likaya lya Yelusalemi.

Kanyhi yali yawatya nha vyono,

“Ane no Muwaha Mulungu,

nolilipiciza likaya lili,

ku soko limemile nghani zo wiwonela wanhu du.

7 Nha vyono muwonaga lisima liwikaga malenga maswanu,

ale nalyo likaya lya Yelusalemi,

vyo liwikaga i makani galyo mabi.

Nane nha nghuhulika zo wuhizi wo wuhodanjice du mwi'kaya,

kanyhi nghuwona du matamwa,

hamonga ne zinghomele mumo mwi'kaya.

8 Nye nyo wanhu wa Yelusalemi,

alu mwali mulece ta i magayo gaga,

gawe suguzi kuli nye ye, na hono si nhavivyo,

hodu nane nomulecenyi, nilinoze likaya lyenyu,

liwe nyika, hawe hanhu hono u munhu wo wose si kosuma kwikala mumo yaye.”

Isilayeli wabezi

9 U Muwaha Mulungu Mudaha Vyose yakanonjela yakatya,

“Hambi Isilayeli nha wolowusizwa lukulu nhe mahalalizwa

ge zabibu mu mubici wo muzabibu,

wono wali wakondya kuhepwa zi zabibu zayo.

Holeka aluno, yikunojeye wuwaponye wanhu wose wono kosuma,

lono i cipango cico cikali si naza.”

10 Ninga nane nghamupitula nghatya,

“Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

alo nani mono kokwenda kuhulika 'ne waco,

kolo neza kuwalonjela, nghuwasugula?

Hambi nagwe nha wuwamanyile,

vyono wo wanhu wono wasina cihuliko hamba hadodo,

holeka hamba si wovumila yaye kuzihuliciza ne zinghani zako,

woziwona nha nzwaji du,

muli zose zono wukunhuma mbite nghawalonjele.

11 Hambi nane nha memile likuwo lyako kuli wene,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu.

Nane hamba si nosuma kwiyimila yaye.”

Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,

“Alu wulimwagaje likuwo lyangu mu wana,

wono wali mu zinzila,

na mu wikongholelo we wazelelo.

Nawo i walume hamonga ne wagolece zawo,

wolosolwa no kutegulwa kutali,

hamonga ne wakombi wose,

nawo hamba si wolosigazwa yaye.

12 Ne zikaya zawo, wolopegwa wanhu wanji,

hamonga ne migunda yawo,

ne wagolece zawo.

Nane nhondo nowalipiciza i wanhu wose wa mu yisi yiyi.

13 Hambi wanhu wose i wadodo sunga ne wawaha,

nha wahidimbaliye mu wutujila sawo,

mu zinzila zo wuhizi du.

Sunga ne wawizi, hamonga ne wakohani,

nawo wose wakwituma metumo go wukoci du kuli wanhu.

14 Na kanyhi wakuziwona zinghomele ze wanhu wangu,

kotya nha wikubule-kubule du wudodo.

Hanyuma wawa kutya, ‘Aa! Zose swanu du.’

Na kumbe, hasina swanu hamba monga.

15 Wule, wukwihwesa wene wakuwona cevu,

ku soko ye makani gago ge mawifyo, gono wetumile?

Waco!

Wayago hamba si waziceleleye yaye,

holeka hamba ne cevu, wene si wacimanyile yaye.

Holeka ku soko yiyo, nawo wologwa,

nhe wayawo wanji vyono wagwa,

kolo neza kuza nghuwalipiciza.

Na wuwo wo wolowa wo mudumo wawo.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

Isilayeli yakuyilema i nzila yo Mulungu

16 U Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela wanhu wakwe yakutya nhavi,

“Nye nyo wanhu wangu,

ale mwime mu zinzila zimbaha, mulanje.

Mukawuze zinghani zo wukolo we katali,

muyiwone nzila yo mugoloka.

Nanye muyikole nzila yiyo, holeka nanye mokwikala mupolile.”

Ninga wene wakatya,

“Ase calema, hamba si cokola mu nzila yiyo ng'o.”

17 Hanyuma u Muwaha Mulungu yakawika walindilizi,

ho wahulicize i lizi lye ndulele lyono likuwasugula.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo, wene wakatya,

“Ase si cikusaka hamba no kuhuliciza ng'o.”

18 Na ku soko yiyo, nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Ale, muhulicize ta anye nyo makungugo,

muwone cono cowaziza i wanhu wangu.

19 Nagwe go 'si, wuhulicize! Wuwone vyono neza kugalipiciza i mahweso gawo,

no kuwongomeza i wanhu wawa,

hambi nha wagalema i mejizo gangu,

hamba kuzibocela yaye zinghani zangu.

20 Alu nane nowa nina cambuzici,

hono niwubocele wuvumba wono wakundetela ku wulawa Sheba,

hamba no wudi wa mhunjile swanu wono wukulawa ku zi'si zo kutali?

Nane hamba si novibocelela lukulu i vilavywa vyawo,

hamba si nokwendezwa yaye ne makumbiko gawo.

21 Nane nowanoza wanhu wawa,

wekobaze, wagwe.

I wasogo zo munhu, ne wana wawo we cilume,

ne weyendi wayawo,

hamonga ne wekalani wayawo, wose wolo kongomezwa.”

U wulugu wukulawilila ku lubavu lwa kusumuka

22 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ale, i wanhu wakuza wakulawa ku yisi yo lubavu lwa kusumuka,

we likungugo lyono lili vilungo, wakwiwikaniza so wulugu.

23 Wakondya kwibata no wupinde wawo mu miwoko,

hamonga ne mimaje yawo.

Kanyhi wene, wo wanhu wakali muno,

kanyhi wasina hamba lusungu yaye.

Nawo wakukoloma wenha i mawingo ge lilenga lono wapandile mu zifwalasi zawo.

Nawo wakondizye kwiwikaniza waze wayihome Sayuni.”

24 Nawo i wanhu wa Isilayeli wakutya nhavi,

“Heya, nase cakondya kuzihulika zizo,

ne miwoko yetu yawula vilungo,

nase luno cakondya kwibatwa no wugaye,

hamonga no wusungu,

ceza cinha u mucekulu mono yali mu nghunga yo lusawo.

25 Sunga nase nhondo,

ceza kogopa muno muno ku wubita mu mawimbi,

hamba kujenda-jenda du mu zinzila,

hambi i walugu wetu nha wewikanize,

kanyhi wacipilime mbavu zose ho wacihome.”

26 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakuwatya nhavi,

i wanhu wakwe,

“Ale, le, muvwale magunila mwivutuse mu mavu.

Mulile ko wusungu wuwaha,

mulavye ne mahozi nha vyono munojeye,

nho mwana we cilume, we ciwene.

Ku soko yuyo mono kokuza no wuhile wo kumongomezenyi,

nha kosumwa musunho du.

27 Alu 'gwe Yelemiya, ale, wuwajele wanhu wangu,

nha vyono kucijelaga cuma,

nagwe wuwamanye nha vyono wenha.

28 Hambi wose nha wasina cihuliko hamba nhavi,

webavyi, kanyhi wakamu na ku wuhulice,

wenha shaba, hamba cuma.

Kanyhi wose wahizi,

wendile kubita ako na ako wena ze maheho go kuheha wayawo.

29 Mivuwo yikufugutiza moto muwaha muno,

ninga vyuma vye lisasi du vyo vyenjedukaga no kulawila mu moto.

Holeka hasina cambuzi co kuweza i wanhu wangu,

hambi i wabi nha wakali nawuswa yaye muli wene.

30 Holeka i wanhu wowa kucemwa,

‘Makwe ga fweza.’

Hambi ane no Muwaha Mulungu nha nakondya kuwalema wene.”


Yelemiya 7

Yelemiya yakuganula mu Nyumba yo Mulungu

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanhegula
2 sunga ho ha mulango we Nyumba yo Mulungu, hono i wanhu wa Yuda wenjililaga, ho wamuhembe Mulungu. Yakanonjela nime baho ha mulango, niwalonjele i wanhu wa Yuda, zono u Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha vyose, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli yakusaka yawalonjele. Nane nghawalonjela nghatya, “Hulicizenyi Nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu anye nyo wanhu wa Yuda, mono mwinjilaga mumu ho mumu hembe Muwaha Mulungu.
3 U Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yakutya nhavi. Muzipitule zinzila ze mikalile yenyu, hamonga ne makani mabi gono mwitumaga, holeka nayo komulecenyi mwikale hanhu baha.
4 Mulecele lukulu kwitumbila zinghani zizo zo wulambi, zo wutya ‘Ase cipolile, hambi ayi nha yo Nyumba yo Mulungu! Nha yo Nyumba yo Mulungu! Nha yo Nyumba yo Mulungu!’

5 “Ninga langa muyipitule nzila yenyu ye mikalile yono mwikalilaga, mulecele lukulu kwituma i zimbi zono mwitumaga. Muwe kwitendela ze ling'ani munhu na muyagwe.
6 Mulece kuwewonela wajenzi, hamba i wana wono wasina walezi wawo, hamba i wacekulu wafwile. Mulece kuwawulaga i wanhu wono wasina masutya mono yisi yiyi. Mulece kuyihemba milungu yinji, hambi zizo nha zo mongomezenyi bwete.
7 Na hono mbeka muyipitule mikalile yenyu, nane nomulecenyi mwikale hanhu baha, mu yisi yono nawapela i walezi wenyu, wuwe wo wuhazi wawo.

8 “Alu lanje! Mukwitumbila zinghani zo wulambi.
9 Mukuhiza, mukuwulaga, mukusenyha, mukwilahiza ko wulambi, kanyhi mukumulavizya Baali makumbiko, no kuyihemba i milungu yono si mwali muyimanyile hamba hadodo.
10 Mukwituma zinghani zono si nizendile hamba hadodo. Hanyuma kanyhi ti, ho muwa kuza kwima hali 'ne, mugati ye Nyumba yangu, no kutya, ‘Ase cipolile!’
11 Anye lu, mukwihwesa nha vyono Nyumba yangu, heza hanhu ho wihundiko we wahizi lo? Ninga nane nakondya kuziwona zose zono mukuzituma.
12 Ale, mubite ku Shilo, i hanhu halongozi, hono nahahagula, ho hawe i hanhu ho wuhembelo. Muwone na zono nayinozeza ku soko yo wubi we wanhu wangu Waisilayeli.
13 Nanye mwetuma u wubi wuwu wose, hamba niwe namulonjelenyi zizi zose kali kenji na kenji, ninga nye mukalema kuhulika 'ne. Yali yiwe yili ciba le, hono mwali mulece kwidika, lono nali kumucemenyi.
14 Hamba nhavivyo, zono netuma ku Shilo, zo noyitendela ne Nyumba yangu yiyi, yono anye muyitumbiye. Nane nohatendela i hanhu baha hono nawapela i walezi wenyu hamonga na nyenye, nha vyono nayitendela Shilo.
15 Nomuwinjenyi ha meso gangu, nha vyono nawawinga i wandugu zenyu, i wanhu wa Isilayeli. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

U wu wula cihuliko we wanhu

16 Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Gwe Yelemiya, wulece kuwalombela wanhu wawa. Wulece kulila hamba kulomba ku soko yawo. Hamba wulece kuneneleza 'ne, ku soko yawo, hambi 'ne nha si nokuhuliciza yaye.
17 Alu 'gwe si wukuziwona yaye, zinghani zono wakwituma mu makaya ga Yuda, na mu zinzila za mu Yelusalemi lo?
18 I wana wawo, wo watenyi we zingwagu, i wasogo zawo, wo wapembi wo moto, i wanyina zawo, wo wavuwi wo wusaje ho wamunozeze mikate, u mulungu we cicekulu mono wamucemaga, mulungu we cicekulu wa ku wulanga. No kuyijidila milungu yinji, i vilavywa vye divayi, ho wanghoze likuwo'ne.
19 Wene wakwihwesa nha vyono wakunhamisa nene, kumbe ho wakwitamisa weneco, hamonga no kwiyiletela cevu ca bwete.
20 Holeka lu, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nane nolijidila likuwo lyangu mumu mu Nyumba yangu. Hamonga na zizo, nolijidila sunga na muli wanhu, na muli vilimu, na mu mabici gose, sunga na mu vilimwa. Ne likuwo lyangu, lyowa lihwanile moto mukali, wono howa hasina munhu wo wose, mono kosuma kuwuzimya yaye.

21 “Nye nyo wanhu wangu, manyile'ne vyono anye, muzoweye kusola i makumbiko genyu ganji, muwa kugakongoga mu moto, na ganji go muwa kulya. Ninga ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghumutyenyi, vyono munojeye mweze kulya vyose du hamonga.
22 Hambi ane nha si nawapela malajilizo go gose i walezi wenyu, kuli ze vilavywa vinyanywe, hamba i makumbiko ge liwala linji lyo lyose, lono nawawusiza mu yisi ya Misili.
23 Langa nawalajiliza nghawatya vyono, weze wali cihuliko du kuli'ne, nane niwe Mulungu wawo, nawo wawe wanhu wangu. Kanyhi nghawalajiliza, nha vyono, yiwanojeye wekale mu mikalile nha vyono nawalajiliza, holeka womoteswa mu milimo yawo yose.
24 Ninga wene si wahulika 'ne, hamba kuzicelelela bwete, zono nawalonjela. Wene waketuma zimbi vyono wasakaga weneco mu zinhumbula zawo. Wakacila kuwa wabi muno, wakawuka ku wuswanu.
25 Na ku wulawa lono i walezi wenyu wawucila mu yisi ya Misili, nghali niwatumaga kuli'nye wetumami wangu i wawizi sunga nalu.
26 Ninga hasina hamba munhu monga, mono yawahulika, hamba kuzicelelela bwete zono wazilongaga. Ninga 'nye mukajendelela kuwa musina cihuliko kuli'ne, no kuwa wabezi muno si nhe walezi wenyu.

27 “Holeka agwe Yelemiya, wuwalonjelaje inghani zizi zose, i wanhu wangu, ninga wene hamba si wokuhulika yaye. Agwe kowacema, ninga wene hamba si wokwidika ng'o.
28 Wuwalonjele nha vyono, likungugo lyawo lisina cihuliko kuli'ne, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wawo, hamba si wakusuma kwifundila mu wulipicizwe wawo. No wihuwilwa wawo wakondya kuwucila lukulu muli wene, hamba no kuwuduwila du kose, si wawuduwilaga yaye.

I metumo go wubi ga mwi'lolo lya Hinomu

29 “Ale, mulile, anye nyo wanhu wa Yelusalemi,

mucete zimvwili zenyu muzitaje kutali.

Mwimbe zinyimbo ze maziko mu vizuli vye matunda,

hambi ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nha ngholigwe ne likuwo,

nakondya kuwalema ne wanhu wangu.

30 “Nawo i wanhu wa Yuda, mbeka wetuma nghani 'mbi muno muno. Wene wawika i vihwani vyawo viseswe mu Nyumba yangu, wakayavufya.
31 Kanyhi wakazenga cikumbicilo cono cacemwaga Tofeti, mwi'lolo lya Hinomu, ho wawalavye i wana wawo we cilume na we cicekulu, wawakongojeze mu moto. Ninga nane ho si nawalajiliza wetume zizo. Kanyhi ne nghani yiyo, yali yili musina muli'ne.
32 Holeka ku soko ya zizo, ninga vilo vyolokuza, lono si holocemwa kanyhi Tofeti, hamba Lilolo lyo Mwana wa Hinomu. Langa holocemwa Lilolo lye Mawulaji. Nawo wolowa kuwazika mwi'lolo lilyo, i wanhu wose wono wasina i hanhu ho kuzikwa.
33 Ne mivimba yiyo, yolowa ndigwa ye zindeje, hamonga ne malimu ga mu mbago. Na holowa hasina hamba munhu wo wose mono koziwinga yaye.
34 Nayo yisi yololekwa nyhiwa. Nane noloyidumya nyemo yose no wusangalale, mu makaya ga Yuda, na mu zinzila zose za Yelusalemi, hamonga ne zinyemo zose za mu zindigwa ze zi halusi.


Yelemiya 8

1 “Na mu madaha gago i mafupa gose ge watemi na ge wawaha wa Yuda, na ge wakohani, na ge wawizi, hamonga ne mafupa ge wanhu wose wono wekalaga mumo mu Yelusalemi, mafupa gose gago golofukulwa mu vitenjelo vyayo.
2 Hanyuma geyagazwe caka mwi 'hala, gehwane mboleya ya mu migunda. Golokwanzalikwa he'zuwa, no mwezi, hamonga ne zinyelezi, vyono wene wali wavyendile no kuvitumamila, hamonga no kuvitumbila no kuvihemba.
3 Ne wanhu wawa wabi, we likungugo lili, wono wowa wakali wasigaye womi, wono wowa wakali wekaye mu zi'si, mono naweyagaziza, wolokwenda wafwe si nho kwikala womi. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha Vyose.”

U wubi no Wulipicizwe

4 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela mbite nghawalonjele wanhu wakwe niwatye nhavi,

“Wulenyi, hono munhu yekobale yagwe hasi,

kokwikalilila baho hasi yagwiye du,

hamba si kokwinuka kanyhi lo?

Hamba hono munhu yajililwe nzila,

si kowuya ne nyuma yayilondole kanyhi ng'o?

5 Ninga anye 'lu,

nhawule vyo mukali du muvidindimiye i vihwani vyenyu viseswe,

hamba si mukusaka kumbuyila ng'o.

6 Ane nghuwa hulicize du,

ninga hamba si mulongaga ze mbeka yaye.

Kanyhi muli nyenye,

hasina hamba monga mono yakulajila vyono yakunena ku soko yo wubi wakwe ng'o.

Hamba hasina mono yewuza mweneco yatye,

‘Mwene wubi wangu cicici?’

Ninga wanhu wose,

munhu yatyatizye na nzila yakwe du,

wehwanile nhe zifwalasi zono zikumanyhila ku wulugu.

7 Sunga ne yobwa ya mu wulanga nha yigamanyile ne madaha gayo go kuwuyila kwi nhuli lyayo,

sunga ne zingungulima zose,

ne zisamamba,

hamonga ne zinghweli nghweli,

zose zigamanyila madaha gayo go kuhamila,

no kubita kwikala hanhu hanji.

Ninga i wanhu wangu, woyi he!

Hamba si mugamanyile ne malajilizo gono ane namulajilizenyi.

8 Alu hono muli nhavivyo,

ho mosuma wule kulonga mutye, mwina mahala,

kanyhi mugamanyile ne malajilizo gangu?

Ninga mulanje vyono i wejizi wo wulajilize wawukawaniza wulajilize so wu wula cihuliko wawo.

9 Ne wanhu wenyu we mahala wakozwa cevu,

nalu watimanjila, wegwiza weneco mu mutego.

Hambi nha wayilema nghani yangu,

alu zili hayi 'mbi, zono wakwiyitya 'ti wena mahala?

10 Holeka nane lu, noyisola migunda yawo,

niwapele wanhu wanji, sole ne wacekulu zawo nawo,

mhele walume wanji.

Alu lanje, vyono wanhu wose i wawaha ne wadodo,

wakujela kwitujila pesa mu zinzila zo wukoci.

Sunga ne wawizi, hamonga ne wakohani,

nawo nhavivyo du, wekalaga wakukonga-konga wanhu.

11 Wakuziwona zinghomele ze wanhu wangu nha wikubule kubule du wudodo.

Hanyuma wawa kutya,

‘Aa! Zose swanu du,’ na kumbe hasina swanu hamba monga.

12 Ninga wule nyo wanhu wangu,

mwakolwa cevu so makani gago ge cevu gono mwagetuma?

Waco nye, hamba si mwakolwa cevu yaye,

hamba si mwapuwa ng'o!

Holeka luno nanye kolo nghumukundujizenyi,

mogwa nha vyono wanji wagwa.

Nanye nhondo, wuwo wowa wo mudumo wenyu.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

13 “Nali nina wuhile wo kuwakongholela hamonga wanhu wangu,

nha vyono munhu kakongholezaga nhwanga yakwe,

ninga wanhu wangu wehwanile no mubici wo muzabibu,

wono wusina zabibu, hamba mukuyu,

wono wusina zinghuyu,

wono sunga ne mahamha gayo gakondya kunyala.

Holeka nane nghawaleka i wanhu wanji wayisole yisi.

14 “Nawo i wanhu wo Mulungu wakwiwuza wakutya,

‘Alu mwene nye!’

Nhawulenyi lo cikaye cinyamaye twii.

Alenyi cimanyhile mu makaya gono gena magomawakali cikafwile mumo.

Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wetu, nha yakondya kuciling'aniziza wufwe.

Hambi nha kacipela 'se sumu, ciying'we,

vyono nase cituma wubi hali mwene.

15 Hambi ase nha calanjililaga mulamu,

ne madaha go kuponywa,

ninga ziswanu hamba si zejela yaye,

langa wogofywe wo wukacila muno.

16 Ne walugu wetu nha wakondya kwinjila mwi'kaya lya Dani,

nase sunga nalu,

cikugahulika i mazi ge zifwalasi zawo vyono zikuzoza.

Sunga ne yisi yose yikucicima ku soko ye mililo ye zifwalasi zawo.

Ne walugu wetu wawo,

nha wakuza ho wayongomeze yisi yetu,

hamonga ne vinhu vyose vyono vili mumo,

i makaya hamonga ne wanhu wose wono wekaye mumo.

17 “U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

Alu mwilanjililaje!

Hambi 'ne nha nghumutumilenyi mazoka gono gena sumu nghali muno muno,

gono gasina wuganga wo wose wono wosuma kugemila yaye,

ho galece gamulumenyi.”

U wukalale wa Yelemiya kuli wanhu wakwe

18 U wukalale wangu, hasina hamba yunji mono kosuma

kuwuwusa yaye,

hambi nha nhamigwe mu nhumbula yangu.

19 Ale, hulicizenyi anye mono muli mu yisi yose.

Hambi 'ne nha nghucihulika cililo ce wanhu wangu

wakulila wakutya,

“Alo mwene u Muwaha Mulungu yeza yali kusina

kuko ku Sayuni?

Hamba u mutemi we Sayuni, yeza yali kusina kuko?”

Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu, u mutemi wawo yakupitula

yakutya,

“Nhawulenyi nanye mwanghoza likuwo,

ku soko yo wuhemba vihwani wenyu,

hamonga no winamila i milungu yenyu ye cijenzi,

yono yisina wutazi wo wose?”

20 Nawo i wanhu wo Mulungu wakulila wakutya,

“Alu nhwanga yakondya kupuluta,

ne cibahu naco capuluta,

ninga ase hamba kupona yaye.”

21 Mbeka, ane Yelemiya, nhumbula yangu yikuzizidika muno,

ku soko ye wanhu wangu wono wena wusungu

mu zinhumbula zawo.

Nane nghulila, no wucelewale wunyibate muno.

22 Alo mwene kuko ku Gileyadi, hongo kusina

miti yo yose?

Hamba kuko ku Gileyadi, kusina muganga wo wose hongo?

Alu socici i wanhu wangu wakali sunga nalu

si wanaponywa yaye?


Yelemiya 9

1 Na mbeka,

nali nyende u mutwe wangu wanda wuwe yisima lya malenga,

ne meso gangu galavye mivwilo ya mahozi,

ho sume kulila misi ne cilo,

ku soko ye wanhu wangu wono wakondya kuwulagwa.

2 Na mbeka, nali nyende nhuje hanhu ho kwikala ku nyika,

hono nosuma kuwa kutali ne wanhu wangu.

Hambi wene, wose nha si wehuwilwa,

lipuga lye wagaluci.

3 Wene wewikanize kulonga zo wulambi du nhondo zose,

wanhu wono wetujila vilungo vyo kuyitema yisi

mu nzila zo wulambi.

U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“I wanhu wangu nha wetuma wubi wityatiliye,

hamba si wakuwa kumanya 'ne,

vyono yo nene Mulungu wawo.”

4 Holeka musuguce,

mweze kwiyidima munhu no mwiyendi muyagwe,

no munhu wo wose,

yalecele lukulu kumwitumbila u nduguye,

hambi i wandugu za munhu,

nha wowa walambi wose,

hambi nha wowa wasonjezi,

wakuwasonjeza wayawo.

5 Na wose wowa walambi, wakuwajiliza wayawo.

Ne zinimi zawo, wazizoweleze kulamba du nhondo zose,

hamba si wasumaga no kuwuleka wubi wawo.

6 Wajendelelaga du kwituma zo wubi awu na awo,

ne metumo gawo go wulambi, nago vyo getyatiliye du.

Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

vyono i wanhu wakwe wakondya kumulema.

7 Ku soko ya zizo, holeka nayo u Muwaha Mulungu

we mihanga yakutya nhavi,

“Noweza wanhu wangu nha shaba, niwajele.

Hambi i wanhu wangu nha wetuma zinghani zimbi,

alu ho niwanoze nhawule?

8 Zinimi zawo nha zihwanile ne zisonga zono zina sumu,

wekalaga wakulonga zo wulambi du nhondo zose.

Nawo wose, welonjelaga nghani zo wukoci munhu na muyagwe,

ninga mu nhumbula yakwe ho yakumusepa yamutamise.

9 Alu'ne ho ndece kuwalipiciza so makani gago hongo?

Hamba nolecelaci kwilipiciza ne likungugo lyono linha nhavivi?

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

10 Nane Yelemiya nghatya, “Ale, nane nogalilila matunda,

no kugalavya mahozi gangu ku soko yo wudimilo.

Hambi wudimilo wose nha wanyala hasina

hamba yunji kanyhi mono yakukolela mumo.

Ne mazi ge zimitugo,

gapela no kuhulikwa mumo.

Sunga ne malimu gose hamonga ne zindeje,

vyose vyakondya kuwuka mumo.”

11 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Nane noyinoza Yelusalemi

yiwe malundo ge matongo,

hawe hanhu hono macewe gowa kwikala,

nago makaya ga Yuda, gowa nyika,

hawe hanhu hono yunji si kosuma kwikala.”

12 Ninga nane Yelemiya, nghamuwuza nghatya, “Alu Muwaha Mulungu, i nhunizo ye yisi yiyi, holeka yilekwe yinyale yiwe nha nyika cicici, sunga hamba no munhu wo wose yalece kukolela mu ciligati yayo? Alo we mahala mono kosuma kuzizuka zizi yo nani 'mbi? Hamba gwegwe go mweneco go Muwaha Mulungu, kumuloloseza nani lo, nayo ho yasume kuwalonjela na wanji?”
13 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakamhitula yakatya, “Zizi zijela, vyono i wanhu wangu wakondya kugaleka i mejizo gangu gono nawejiza. Weza wasina hamba cihuliko kuli'ne, hamba kwituma zono nawalonjela wetume.
14 Wakawa wajendeleye ne lizeze lyawo du lyo kuhemba vihwani vya Baali, na vyono wafundwa ne wasogo zawo.
15 Alu agwe wuhulicize zono ane nane no Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga u Mulungu we Waisilayeli, vyono notenda. Nowalisa wanhu wangu mabici makali, niwang'wese ne sumu wang'we.
16 Noweyagaziza mu makungugo ganji, gono weneco, hamba ne walezi wawo, wali si wagamanyile yaye. Nowatumila walugu, nghawongomezeze lukulu.”

I wanhu wa mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi wakulila, wakulondola wutazi

17 U Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga yakutya nhavi,

“Alu muzihwesaje ta zizi zono zikuza!

Muceme wanya kulila waze walile,

muwaceme wacekulu wono wembaga zinyimbo ze maziko.”

18 Nawo wanhu wakutya,

“Ale, muwalonjele weyohe,

waze wacimbile'se lwimbo lwe maziko, wembe,

sunga ne meso getu gameme mahozi,

ne zinghope zetu ze meso zisuluze malenga so cililo cetu.”

19 Ale, ta muhulicize cililo cono cikulawa ku Sayuni cono wakutya,

“Alu langa congomela! Cakozwa cevu muno muno!

Nalu yicinojeye ciyilece yisi yetu hambi zikaya zetu nha zamwagulwanga.”

20 Nane Yelemiya nghatya,

“Nye nyo wacekulu,

ale muhulicize zono u Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga,

muteje makutu genyu muzikamise zinghani zakwe

nanye muwejize ne wana wenyu we cicekulu vyono walilaga,

ne weyendi wayenyu, vyono wembaga u lwimbo lwe maziko.

21 Hambi i lifwa nha lyaciziza likolile mu madilisha getu,

lyenjila mu zikaya zetu zo wutemi,

ho liwawulajile lukulu i wana wetu mu zinzila,

ne wazelelo wetu mu zi sokoni.

22 Nalu, i mivimba yokwiyagala caka hanhu hose

yihwane nhe malundo ge mboleya yono yili mu migunda.

Hamba maso go wuhemba gono gadudulwa,

hanyuma kanyhi gawa kulekwa no mududuzi gagwiye hasi.

I maso gono gowa gasina mono yakugalundika hamonga.

Ale, zizi zo Muwaha Mulungu kanhuma nimulonjelenyi.”

23 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“I wanhu we mahala, walece kwikumya so mahala gawo,

hamba wono wena vilungo, ku soko ye vilungo vyawo.

Nawo i wagoli nhavivyo,

walece kwikumya ku soko yo wugoli wawo.

24 Langa mono yakwikumya,

yekumyaje ku soko vyono yakondya kumanya'ne,

hamonga no kunyizuka.

Hambi u wendo wangu nha si wupitukaga yaye,

nane nitumaga ze ling'ani na zo mugoloka.

Na zizi zo nghani du zono zinyendezaga.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

25 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “I vilo vikuza, lono nolowalipiciza i wanhu wose wono walame mu miwili du,
26 Wamisili, Wayuda, Waedomu, Waamoni, na Wamowabi, hamonga ne wanhu wono wekalaga mu nyika, wono wekalaga na mvwili fupi.” Hambi wanhu wawa wose, hamonga ne Waisilayeli wose, nha walame, ninga si wacikamise i cilagane cono wene walajiye citanjilo cayo. Holeka wakali nalama mu zinhumbula.


Yelemiya 10

U wuhemba vihwani, no Wuhembi we mbeka

1 Nye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli,

alu muhulicize ta zono u Muwaha Mulungu yakusaka kumulonjelenyi,

2 Yakutya nhavi,

“Mulecele lukulu kuzityatya zinzila ze makungugo ganji,

hamba kogofywa ne vitanjilo vya kucanyha,

hamba wawe wene we makungugo vikuwogofya muno.

3 No wuhembi we wanhu wawo,

nha wusina cambuzi hamba hadodo,

hambi u mubici, nha wukutemwa mu mbago,

hanyuma wuwa kuseswa ne wasesi ko wunghula,

4 no kuhambwa kwa fweza na zahabu,

na hanyuma wawa kuwukomelela misumali,

ho wulece kudegandega.

5 Ne vihwani vivyo, vihwanile nhe cihwani ciseswe co munhu,

cono ciwicigwe mu mugunda we matanga,

ho cogofye zindeje.

Ne vihwani vivyo, si vikusuma kulonga yaye,

hamba kujenda, langa sunga wuvidinghule du.

Holeka anye, mulecele lukulu kuvyogopa,

hambi nha si vyosuma kumutamisenyi yaye,

hamba kumunozezenyi nghani yo yose yono swanu.”

6 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, nha hasina yunji wo wose kanyhi,

mono yehwanile gwegwe, agwe wuli vilungo.

Ne litagwa lyako likumucile muno, na mu wusume.

7 Hamba nani 'mbi kanyhi,

mono si kakogopaga gwegwe go mutemi we makungugo gose?

Yo gwegwe du wuli wene mono wunojeye kogopwa,

hambi nha hasina yunji wo wose,

mono yehwanile gwegwe,

muli we mahala wose wa mu makungugo,

hamba muli watemi wawo.

8 Wose nha walele du, kanyhi wasina mahala.

Ale wene,

wakwihwesa hamba wosuma kwiyijiza cicici,

mumo mu vihwani lo vye mabici?

9 Hambi i vihwani vivyo,

nha vihiliwe du fweza yono yikulawa ku Sipeyini,

ne zahabu yono yikulawa ku Ufazi,

nayo yo milimo ye watyani wanghula,

yivwalile myenda ye liwala lyo lugandu,

hamonga na ye liwala lidunghu,

i myenda mihone ne wanghula.

10 Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yo gwegwe du, go Mulungu we mbeka,

yo gwegwe du go Mulungu mumi,

kanyhi yo gwegwe du go mutemi we cibitilila.

Na lono wuwe wakolwa ne likuwo,

i yinze yicicimaga,

sunga ne makungugo, si gakuwa kusuma

yaye kulikangalila likuwo lyako.

11 (Holeka anye nyo wanhu, munojeye muwalonjele we makungugo, vyono i milungu yiyo yono yeneco si yayiwumba yinze, hamba wulanga, yokongomezwa yose. Hamba si yolokwijela kanyhi hanhu ho hose mono yinze.)

U lwimbo lo kumukumya Mulungu

12 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yayiwumba yinze kwe vilungo vyakwe mweneco,

yakayiwumba yinze kwe mahala gakwe mweneco,

yakawutandalika sunga no wulanga.

13 Yakagalajiliza i malenga ga ku wulanga gakolome,

kanyhi yo mweneco mono kagaletaga mavunde,

ku wulawilila ku ndumo ye yinze.

Kanyhi yo mweneco mono kaganozaga mamuli,

gawe kumulika mu madaha ge mvula,

kanyhi kayiletaga mbeho mbaha mu wulawa mu viwutwa vyakwe.

14 Na muli zizi zose, i wanhu wakwijela walele,

kanyhi wasina mahala,

na wawo wono wakuvisesa vivyo vihwani,

wakondya kukozwa cevu, ku soko ye vihwani vyawo,

hambi i milungu yawo yono wakuyisesa,

nha ya wulambi du, kanyhi yisina wehele muli yene.

15 Holeka yisina cambuzi, langa co cinhu ca caka du.

Ninga i lizuwa lyono Muwaha Mulungu yalaziza,

kolokuza yayongomezeze lukulu, i milungu yiyo yose.

16 Hambi u Mulungu wa Yakobo, nha si yehwanile na wene,

hambi mwene, nha yo yavinoza vinhu vyose,

kanyhi yo mweneco mono yawahagula i wanhu wa Isilayeli,

wawe wo wanhu wakwe wahagulwa.

Ne litagwa lyakwe yakucemwa,

U Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga.

I mazizo go wuhamywe

17 Nye nyo wanhu wa Yelusalemi,

alu mwakondya kupilimwa ne walugu!

Holeka alu, munhu wo ose,

yakongholeze vinhu vyakwe.

18 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yakuza yamuwusenyi mu muno mono yisi yiyi,

yamugumilenyi kutali,

komutajilenyi mu magayo mawaha,

hamba holowa hasina kanyhi yunji mono kolosigala mu yisi yiyi.

Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

19 Nawo i wanhu wa Yelusalemi wakalila wakutya,

“Woyi hee, catamiswa nye,

ne zinghomele zetu, hamba si zolopona yaye.

Nase cehwesaga nha vyono cosuma kuwukangalila

u wugaye wono winha vivi!

20 Alu lanje vyono, sunga ne mahema getu gasakala,

ne milejehe yono yali yigawopile,

nayo yakondya kudumukanjika.

Ne wana wetu nawo, wose wakondya kubita,

alu wali hasina.

Holeka heza hasina,

hamba munhu wo wose kanyhi,

mono kocikomela mahema getu,

hamba mono kosuma kucidumulila mabupa ne magayo pazigeya.”

21 Nane nghapitula nghatya, “Nye! I watonji wetu,

nha weza wasina mahala,

hamba si wasakaga no kuwuza wutonji yaye

kuli Muwaha Mulungu.

Na yiyi yo soko yono yikuleka sunga ne milimo yawalemela,

sunga ne wanhu wetu luno, wakondya kwiyagala ako na ako.

22 Ninga luno ta muhulicize! Nghani yakondya kuza!

Lanje, kowuli musindo muwaha muno,

wukulawa ku yisi yo lubavu lwa kusumuka,

ne lipuga lyayo lye wasilikale,

lyogongomeza i makaya ga Yuda gawe nha nyika,

hawe hanhu hono gowa kwikala macewe du.”

23 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ane manyile nha vyono hasina munhu hamba yumonga yaye,

mono kewumipilaga mweneco.

Hamba hasina munhu mono kawibatilizaga wumi wakwe mweneco.

24 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ale wuwakongholezaje wanhu wako,

wulece kucileka yaye,

hamba wulece kucilipiciziza mwi'kuwo lyako,

hambi nha ho kocongomezeza lukulu.

25 Langa wite likuwo lyako kuli we makungugo

wono si wakumanyile,

hamba kukuhemba gwegwe,

na muli wanhu wono si wakwendile 'gwe.

Hambi nha wawawulaganga i wanhu wako,

kanyhi wakondya kucongomezeza lukulu,

wakondya kuyinoza ne yisi yetu yeza matongo du.


Yelemiya 11

Yelemiya ne Cilagane

1 Hanyuma kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, nhavi,
2 “Alu Yelemiya, wuhulicize ta nghuloloseze i zinghani ze Cilagane cici. Nagwe wuwalonjele i wanhu wa Yuda na wa Yelusalemi,
3 nha vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, nakondya kumulijita munhu wo wose, mono si yakuzikamisa zinghani ze cilagane cici.
4 Hambi nha co cilagane cono nacinoza ne walezi wawo, lono nawawusiza mu yisi ya Misili, mu yisi yono yali yinha nha moto mukali kuli wene. Nane nghawalonjela nha vyono wahulice 'ne no kwituma zinghani zose, zono nawalajiliza. Nane nghawaloloseza nha vyono, hono wazibate, wowa wanhu wangu, nane nowa Mulungu wawo.
5 Ale, nane ho nowufisiliza wihuwizo wangu, wono nawehuwiziza i walezi wawo, nha vyono nowapela yisi yono yina wugoli, hamonga no moso mwinji, yono wena nayo luno.” Nane nghapitula nghatya, “Heya vyo yiweze, gwe go Muwaha Mulungu.”

6 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Alu, wubite mu makaya gose ge yisi ya Yuda, hamonga na mu zinzila zose za mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi. Wubite wukayiganule nghani yangu kuko, wuwalonjele wanhu vyono, yiwanojeye wazihulice zinghani ze cilagane no kuzitumama.
7 Hambi nha nawahomelela no kuwasugula i walezi wawo, lono nawawusiza mu yisi ya Misili, vyono yiwanojeye wahulice'ne. Kanyhi hamba si naduma baho du, langa najendelela kuwasugula wanhu sunga nalu.
8 Ninga wene hamba si wahulika yaye, hamba kwituma zono nghulonga. Langa wene wakajendelela du kuwa wasina cihuliko, no kwituma zimbi nha vyono wali wazoweye. Ninga nane, nghacila muno kuwalajiliza wetume ze cilagane cici, ninga wene wakalemela lukulu kwituma. Holeka nane nghawalipiciziza mu wulipicize wono wukuloloswa mu cilagane cici.”

9 Hanyuma kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “I wanhu wa Yuda, hamonga ne wanhu wa Yelusalemi, nha wamumbiye wuhile wubi.
10 Alu nha wawuwuyila u wubi we walezi wawo, wono walemaga kwituma zono nawalonjelaga, wene wakayihemba milungu yinji. Holeka ku soko yiyo, wawa wose, Isilayeli na Yuda, wakondya kucibena cilagane cangu cono nacinoza ne walezi wawo.
11 Holeka sunga nalu, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghali kuwasugula du, nha vyono nowaletela wongomele wuwaha muno, wono si wosuma hamba kuwilega yaye. Nawo kolo wakundilila wakusaka wutazi, nane hamba si nowahuliciza yaye.
12 Hanyuma yayo wawo wanhu wa Yuda na Yelusalemi, wawe kuyibitila i milungu yiyo, yono wayilavizyaga makumbiko, wakayililile yiwapele wutazi. Ninga i milungu yiyo, si yosuma hamba hadodo kuwaponya, kolo weza kuza u wongomezwe wuwo.
13 Kanyhi, wumanye nha vyono i wanhu wa Yuda, wena milungu minji muno muno, nha vyono winha u winji we makaya gawo. Nawo i wanhu wa Yelusalemi, wazenjile makumbicilo menji muno, gehwanile no winji we zinzila zawo za mwi'kaya go kumufusiza wuvumba mulungu we cevu Baali.
14 Holeka ku soko yiyo, 'gwe Yelemiya, wulece kuwalombela wanhu wawa kuli'ne, hamba kuneneleza 'ne ku soko yawo. Hambi lono wali mu magayo, na kolo wakundilila ho niwapele wutazi, nane si nowahuliciza hamba hadodo ng'o.”

15 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Alu lanje ta! I wanhu wono niwendile, wakwituma luno zinghani zimbi. Alu, ho wowa wena ling'anici kanyhi mu Nyumba yangu? Alo wene wakwihwesa nha vyono wosuma kuwimila wongomezwe wuwo hongo, mu nzila yo kwihuwiza du, na mu nzila yo wulavya mitugo so makumbiko? Wule, wukwihwesa zizo zowanoza wawe wena nyemo hongo?
16 Nane halulongozi, nawacemaga wene, u mubici wo muzeyituni wono wina mahamha gono gadedewaye, wono wulelile mikalo miswanu muno. Ninga aluno, ane nogakung'unhila mu moto mahamha gayo ne nyhinyinho mbaha, ne manhambi gayo gose gobeneka.”

17 “Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu we mihanga, yo nene mono nawahadika Isilayeli hamonga na Yuda. Ninga lu lanje, nakondya kuwogofya ne magazo. Ninga zizi zose, nha weletela weneco du, so wituma zimbi wawo. Hambi nha wanghoza likuwo so wulavizya Baali makumbiko.”

Yelemiya yakuwumbilwa wuhile wubi

18 U Muwaha Mulungu,

yakamanyiza zo wuhile wubi wono wakumumbila i walugu wangu.

19 Ninga ane nali ninha nyana ye ngholo,

yono mholo, yono yikutegulwa ku wuwulagwe,

hamba si nali manyile nha vyono wakondya kumumbila wuhile wubi.

Wakutya, “Nye!

Alenyi ciwudumule u mubici wuwu,

lono wukali wukamile.

Alenyi cimuwulaje,

ho halece kuwa na munhu wo wose mono kolomukumbucila kanyhi.”

20 Hobaho nane nghawa kumulomba Mulungu nghatya,

“Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu u Mulungu we mihanga,

agwe yo gwegwe mono wuli muling'anizi we ling'ani,

kanyhi yo gwegwe du mono kuwajelaga i wanhu mu zinhumbula zawo,

sunga na mu mitima yawo mugati.

Holeka nane luno,

nghuziwika zinghani zangu mu miwoko yako,

nane nghulanjiliye wuwalipicize wanhu wawa.”

21 Ne wanhu wa ku Anatoti, wali wendile ane mbulagwe. Wakanonjela nha vyono wosuma kumbulaga, hono njendelele kuyiganula nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu.
22 Ale! Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga yakatya, “Ale, heya, nowalipiciza! I wazelelo wawo, wowulajilwa ku wulugu, nawo i wana wawo, i wahinza ne wazelelo, wofwa ne nzala.
23 Nane nha nakondya kuliwikaniza lizuwa lyono nolowuletela wongomele muli wanhu wa Anatoti. Ne lizuwa lilyo kolo lyakondya kuza, holowa hasina hamba munhu monga wa muli wene mono kolopona.”


Yelemiya 12

Yelemiya yakumuwuza Muwaha Mulungu

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

hamba ciwangane nagwe muli zangu,

agwe ninga vyo kojendelela kuwa mu mbeka du.

Holeka nane luno,

nosuma kukuwuza zinghani zono zinojeye wiling'ano,

nha vyono nhavi,

Alu mwene,

nhawule i wanhu wabi nawo wamoteswe muno?

Hamba nhawule i wanhu wono si wehuwilwa nawo wekaye wacehile?

2 Alu wawahadika, nawo wahimbiza milela,

weza kuwiluka, wakulavya mikalo.

Na nhondo zose, walongaga zako,

ninga walongaga kwe milomo yawo du,

mu zinhumbula zawo, hamba si wakuceleleye bwete.

3 Ninga go Muwaha Mulungu, agwe wuzimanyile zangu,

kanyhi kuziwonaga na zono nitumaga zose

kanyhi wumanyile nha vyono nghwendile 'gwe.

Ale, wuwakulunhye wanhu wawa i wabi,

nha ngholo zono zikutegulwa ku wuwulagwe,

holeka wuwawikanizaje, sunga lizuwa lyawo

lyo kuwulagwa liwaficile.

4 Alo yisi yetu yijendelele kuwa yinyalile,

hamonga ne mahanze ga mu mawimbi gose sunga nili lo?

Sunga nalu, i vilimu, hamonga ne zindeje zose

nha zakondya kongomezwa,

ku soko yo wubi we wanhu wetu,

i wanhu wono watezaga “Hambi u Mulungu nha

si yakuziwona yaye zono citumaga.”

5 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakatya nhavi,

“Alu mwene 'gwe Yelemiya,

hono i wanhu du wono wajendaga kwe migulu,

wakukukataza no lumangho, alu ho kokatalila hayi

waco, hono mwitije lumangho ne zifwalasi?

Na hono wuwe si wukusuma kwima wukamile

mu yisi yono yipolile,

alu ho kosuma wule mu yisi ya Yoludani

yono yina widunhumuzi?

6 Alu, lanje, vyono, sunga ne wanduguzo wose

mono muli mhuga monga, nawo wakugaluka,

nalu wakondya kwilunza mwi'puga lya

wono wakukuhoma 'gwe.

Holeka nagwe, hamba wakulonjele nghani swanu muno,

ninga wulecele lukulu kuwetumbila hamba hadodo.”

U wukalale wo Muwaha Mulungu, ku soko ye wanhu wakwe

7 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Holeka nane luno, nakondya kuwaleka wanhu wa Isilayeli,

nakondya kuwalema i wanhu wangu we nghungugo hagulwa.

Nalu i wanhu wono niwendile,

nakondya kuwakombola mu miwoko ye walugu wawo.

8 Hambi wene i wanhu wangu, nha wakondya kundeka,

nalu wakungholomela, wehwana nhe

simba ya mu mbago, holeka nane nawasula.

9 Nalu i wanhu wangu, luno wehwana nhe ndeje,

yono yipilimwe ne zindeje zinghali mbavu zose,

zono zikucemeleza i malimu gose ga mu mbago,

gaze vihanje kulya sikukuu yiyo!

10 Ne wadimi wenji

wakondya kuwuhodanga u mugunda

wangu we mizabibu

wayikanyanjiza migunda yangu

no kuyinoza yisi yangu yono niyendile yiwe nyika.

11 Wayinoza yawa mbago, nalu yawa wambuce du hali'ne.

Ne yisi yose yakondya kuwa nyika

na hasina hamba yunji mono yakuyitondowoza yaye.

12 Ne wanhu wakulawa mu yisi yose

yono yili mu matunda, mu nyika,

wakuza kuyiteka yisi yangu.

Holeka nane luno,

nayiletela wulugu wuyongomeze yisi yose.

Na howa hasina hamba monga mono kokwikala yapolile.

13 Hambi i wanhu wangu,

nha wahadika wuhemba,

ninga wakadudula mabeleje du.

Na mbeka,

wetuma mulimo mukamu muno hamba kutuga cambuzi yaye.

Heya, si watuga nhwanga yaye ku soko ye likuwo lyangu likali.”

U wihuwizo wo Muwaha Mulungu kuli wono wekalane ne Waisilayeli

14 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Ane nina nghani yono nghusaka niwalonjele wono wekalane ne wanhu wangu Waisilayeli, wono wakondya kuyinoza matongo, i yisi yono nawapela i wanhu wangu Waisilayeli. Nha vyono nane, nowawusa wawo i wanhu wabi, mu zi'si zawo, niwakule, niwawuse ne milela nho mubici, nimuponye Yuda muli wene.
15 Nane kolo nakondya kuwawusiza lukulu, ale, baho langa nane ho nolowawonelela nawo lusungu, niwagomole kanyhi mu zi'si zawo, wanhu na yisi yawo.
16 Nawo hono wawuvumile mu zinhumbula zawo u wuhembi we wanhu wangu, kanyhi welahize wawe kutya, ‘Nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu kekalaga mumi,’ nha vyono nawo wali wawafunda i wanhu wangu, kwilahiza kuli Baali, ale, nawo baho, nhondo ho wowalilizwa hamonga ne wanhu wangu, no kumoteswa.
17 Ninga likungugo lyo lyose lyono lyowa lisina cihuliko, nane noliwusiza lukulu likungugo lilyo, nilyongomeze. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 13

Kaputula ya bafuta

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela mbite nghegulile kaputula ya bafuta, niyivwale. Ninga yakatya vyono, ndece kuyiloweka mu malenga yaye.
2 Holeka nghagula kaputula ya bafuta nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu yanonjela, nghayivwala.
3 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
4 “Alu wubite sunga ko kwi'kolongo lya Efulata, wukayihundice kuko kaputula yiyo, mu mhalika ye libwe.”
5 Ale, du, nane nghawa kubita, nghawa kuyihundika kuko, behi ne likolongo lya Efulata.

6 Vyali vyeza kupuluta vilo vinji, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela vyono mbite kwi'kolongo lya Efulata, nghayisole kanyhi kaputula yi yilya.
7 Ale, nane nghawa kubita, nali neza kuhawona i hanhu hono nayihundika kaputula yiyo, nghayagana yakondya kuhodanjika, hamba si yokwila kanyhi cinji co cose.

8 Hanyuma u Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya nhavi,
9 “Ale, nane vyo nowuhodanga nhavivi, u wiwoni no widunhumuzi wa Yuda, na wa Yelusalemi.
10 Hambi i wanhu wawa, nha wakulema kuzihulika zinghani zangu. Weza wanhu wabi ku wulawa na katali, wakuhemba no kuyitumamila i milungu yinji. Holeka nawo wokwihwana ne kaputula yiyino, yono yeza yisina kanyhi cambuzi co cose.
11 Ale, nha vyono kaputula yikamaga nzii mu ciwino, nane nali nitumbiye nha vyono i wanhu wose wa Isilayeli na Yuda, wowa wenamatiye nane hamba kundeka yaye. Nane nazinoza zizi, i wene ho wawe wanhu wangu, ho wasume kulikumya no kulipela lilumbo litagwa lyangu. Ninga wene si wakasaka kuhulika 'ne hamba nhavi.”

Njilo ye divayi

12 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya nhavi, “Gwe Yelemiya, ale wuwalonjelaje wanhu wa Isilayeli nha vyono, njilo zose zomema divayi. Ninga wene wokupitula watye, nawo heya wamanyile nha vyono njilo zose zomema divayi.
13 Nagwe wuwalonjele nha vyono, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nowang'wesa divayi, i wanhu wose we yisi yiyi, nawo woying'wa sunga wagale muno muno, hamonga ne watemi wawo, wo lulelo lwa Dawudi, sunga ne wakohani wawo, ne wawizi, hamonga ne wanhu wose wa Yelusalemi.
14 Hanyuma ho niwe kuwatulanjiza nhe zinjilo zono zikutowezwa njilo na njilo nyagwe. Nawo vyo wotowezwa munhu na muyagwe, i wasogo zo munhu ne wana wawo. Nane si nowawonela lusungu yaye, hamba lusungu, si lonyimila yaye, ho ndece kuwawulaga ng'o.”

Yelemiya yakuwasugula wanhu kuli zo wiwoni

15 Nye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli, u Muwaha Mulungu yakondya kulonga. Mweze wenenenyhezi, kanyhi muli cihuliko kuli mwene.

16 Mogopenyi u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wenyu,

lono yakali si naleta wutitu,

wono wosuma kumwambuzenyi mwikobale mu matunda,

mu madaha go wutitu,

ne madaha gono mukulanjilila wuzelu,

u wuzelu wono mwali muwitumbiye.

17 Holeka hono mwikale si mumuhulice,

nane nolila cinyele ku soko yo wiwoni wenyu,

nolila ko wusungu muno, ne mahozi gangu gosulula,

ku soko ye wanhu wo Muwaha Mulungu,

wono wakondya kusolwa mateka.

18 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,

“Wubite wumulonjele mutemi, hamonga no nyina,

wawuce mu magoda gawo go wutemi.

Hambi i vipombelo viswanu mu mitwe yawo,

nha vyakondya kuwopozwa mu mitwe yawo.

19 Hambi i makaya ga Yuda,

gono gali ku lubavu lwa kutakama ge yisi ya Yuda,

nha gakondya kupilimwa no wulugu,

hamba hasina munhu wo wose mono kosuma kwinjila mu makaya gago kanyhi.

Ne wanhu wose wa Yuda nha wakondya kusolwa,

wategulwa ku wuhamywe.”

20 Ale lu Yelusalemi, winule meso gako wulanje!

I walugu wako nha weza kukuziza wakulawilila ku lubavu lwa kusumuka!

Ku lubavu lwe wanhu wono wali wakwitumbiye muno u wudimi wako,

i wanhu wono nagwe wedunhumulilaga muno.

21 Alu ho kotya wule, lono i wanhu kanyhi wawo,

wono wehwesaga vyono wo wayago,

alu wakusuma no kukutema?

Na mbeka kohulika wusungu muno,

nho mucekulu mono yali mu nghunga yo lusawo.

22 Na kolo wukwiwuza go mweneco wukutya,

alo nhawule zizi zinyijelela lo?

Heya!

Myenda yako yidemulwa,

nagwe wukebatwa ne walume,

no kugona nagwe kwe vilungo.

Ale, wumanye nha vyono,

zizi zikwagana ku soko yo wubi wako wono wukogofya muno.

23 Alo wukwihwesa nha vyono u munhu mutitu,

kosuma kuwukawaniza wutitu we nghuli yakwe lo,

hamba suwi kugapata i mawalawala gayo?

Na hono yiwe yisumice, ale,

na gwegwe mono wuzoweye kwituma zimbi du,

kumbe kosuma kwiyijiza ku wituma ziswanu.

24 Ninga luno u Muwaha Mulungu,

kokwiyagaza ako na ako nhe mahanze,

gono gakuwa kupemheluswa ne mbeho ya mu nyika.

25 Hambi nha yalonga na katali,

nha vyono lili lyo lyowa lye 'fungu lyako.

Hambi nha yadumula nghani,

nha vyono kokutendela zizi.

Ku soko vyono 'gwe wamusemwa mwene,

wakondya kuyitumbila milungu yo wulambi.

26 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu kokupenula myenda yako yakukoze cevu.

27 Hambi nha yakondya kukuwona vyono,

wukwituma zinghani zono mwene si yazendile yaye.

Kakondya kukuwona na vyono wukuyityatya

i milungu ye wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu mu vigongo,

sunga na mu mawimbi,

nha munhu mono yakumusulumila u mucekulu wo muyagwe,

hamba nha fwalasi nume yono yikuyisulumila fwalasi nyhekulu.

Woyi hee!

Nye nyo wanhu wa Yelusalemi!

Hambi nha mukulema kwezwa!

Alu ho mojendelela ne zinghani zizo sunga nili lo?


Yelemiya 14

Wu wula mvula kwa vilo vinji

1 Ku soko yo wu wula mvula mu'si kwa vilo vinji,

u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,

2 “Alu Yuda yeza yili mu cililo,

hambi i makaya gayo nha geza kufwa,

ne wanhu wayo, wagonile hasi du so wukalale,

nayo Yelusalemi, yikulilila wutazi.

3 I wagoli nawo, wakuwatuma i wetumami wawo ku lulenga,

wetumami wawa kubita ku visima,

hamba kutuga malenga yaye.

Hodu wawa kuwuya wali bwete wasina malenga.

Akuno wakoligwe ne zisoni, wakuwona cevu,

wakwihundika sunga no wusu wawo.

4 Hambi nha heza hasina mvula,

no muhangha wababala,

ne walimi wose, watuga wusungu mu zinhumbula zawo

nawo weza kwihundika sunga no wusu wawo.

5 Na mu mbago namo, sunga ne sawada nayo,

yikuyileka nyanagwe caka du

hambi nha mweza musina mahanze.

6 Nazo zinyhenje, zimile mu vizuli vye matunda,

zikutwetucila kotya maminzi,

sunga ne meso gazo, hamba si gakuwona viswanu yaye.

Hambi nha heza hasina mahanze.

7 Nawo i wanhu wangu, wakundilila wakutya,

‘Hamba wuwe u wubi wetu wukucihomelela,

ninga 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wucitazaje du,

nha vyono gwegwe wehuwiza.

Na mbeka, ase cikulecile kali kenji du,

na kanyhi cituma wubi hali'gwe.

8 Ninga agwe yo gwegwe du wuli wene

mono wo witumbilo wa Isilayeli.

Kanyhi yo gwegwe du mono kuciponyaga mu magayo.

Alu nhawule winha munhu mujenzi mu yisi yetu,

hamba nho munhu mono yali mu muhinzo,

mono yakugona lizuwa limonga du?

9 Hamba nhawule winha munhu mono yatinuswe,

nhe citung'ati mono si kosuma kuponya?

Ninga mbeka, agwe go Muwaha Mulungu wuli hamonga nase!

Nase cili wanhu wako du,

holeka dedede, wulece kucileka.’ ”

10 Kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu,

yakulonga kuli ze wanhu wawa, yakutya nhavi,

“Ninga wene wendile kunhiza'ne,

akuno hamba si wakusuma no kwiyimila kose.

Holeka nane si nghwendezwa nawo.

Alu nane nogakumbucila i makani gawo mabi,

niwalipicize ku soko yo wubi wawo.”

11 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela yakatya nhavi, “Alu, wulece kuwalombela kuli'ne wanhu wawa, ho niwaponye.
12 Nawo hamba walece kulya, ninga 'ne hamba si nowahuliciziza lukulu cililo cawo ho niwaponye, ng'o. Hamba wandavizye ne cilavywa cinyanywe, hamonga ne cilavywa co wusaje, ane si nokwendezwa nawo yaye. Langa 'ne nowawulajila wose ku wulugu na mu nzala, na mu matamwa.”

13 Ninga nane, ane Yelemiya, nghamupitula u Muwaha Mulungu nghamutya, “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, agwe wumanyile manyi manyi, nha vyono i wawizi wakuwalonjela wanhu, vyono holowa hasina kanyhi wulugu, hamba nzala, hambi nha vyo wehuwiza gwegwe, nha vyono, holowa mulamu du mu yisi yetu.”

14 U Muwaha Mulungu nayo yakapitula yakatya, “I wawizi walonjelaga mwi'tagwa lyangu, zinghani zo wulambi. Ninga 'ne hamba si niwatumile yaye, hamba kuwalajiliza nghani yinji yaye, hamba kuwalonjela nghani monga du. Holeka wene wakulonga zo wugubulilwe wono si wulawile kuli'ne yaye. Ku soko yiyo, u wuwile wawo, mbeka wusina cambuzi hamba cidodo bwete, hambi gago nha mahweso gawo du, gono wehwesize weneco.
15 Ninga ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nokulonjela luno zono nowanoza, i wawizi wawa wono wakwisolela zinghani weneco, wono ane si niwatumile yaye, wawa kulonjela mwi'tagwa lyangu, wakutya vyono ati wulugu ne nzala si vyosuma kwinjila mu yisi yiyi. Ane nowawulaga i wawizi wawa ko wulugu ne nzala.
16 Ne wanhu wose wono wali kulonjelwa zizo, nawo wowulajilwa mu nzila yiyo du. Ne mivimba yawo yogumilwa caka caka mu zinzila za mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi. Na holowa hasina munhu wo wose mono koloyizika i mivimba yiyo. Ne nghani yiyi, yolowaziza wene wose, hamonga ne wagolece zawo, ne wana wawo we cilume na we cicekulu. Nane nolowalipiciza mu nghango ye metumo gawo mabi vyono genha.”

17 U Muwaha Mulungu yakandajiza niwalonjele wanhu

zo wukalale wangu niwatye nhavi,

“I meso gangu gasuluze mahozi misi ne cilo,

galece kuduma yaye,

hambi i wanhu wangu nha watuga nghomele

kanyhi watamiswe muno muno.

18 Nane lono nghubita mu mawimbi,

nghuyiwona i miwili ye wanhu wono wawulagwe mu wulugu,

na kolo nghubita mu makaya,

namo nghuwawona i wanhu wono wakugaya no kufwa so nzala.

Ninga nawo i wawizi hamonga ne wakohani,

wene wajendeleye du ne milimo yawo,

hamba kumanya yaye na cono wakwituma.”

I wanhu wakumuneneleza u Muwaha Mulungu

19 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

alu mwene hodu wamulemela lukulu Yuda?

Alo wawasulila lukulu i wanhu wa ku Sayuni lo?

Alu nha wacisuliliza muno,

sunga ceza si cikusuma hamba no kuponywa yaye?

Ase cali cilanjiliye kutuga mulamu,

ninga hamba kuziwona ziswanu yaye.

Cali citumbiye nha vyono coponywa,

ninga cikatuga wogofywe wuwaha muno.

20 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, ase cetuma wubi muno hali 'gwe,

holeka aluno, cikuwuvumila wubi wetu,

hamonga no wubi we walezi wetu.

21 Ninga wukumbucile wihuwizo wako,

holeka wulece kucisuliliza,

hamba kulikoza cevu likaya lyako Yelusalemi,

i hanhu he ligoda lyako lyo wutemi, lye nhojo.

Kanyhi wulece kucibena cilagane cono walagana nase.

22 Hambi mu makungugo gose, nha musina cihwani co cose cono

cosuma kutonyeza mvula, sunga ne mavunde,

nha sigosuma geneco du kunyunyutya mvula.

Holeka ase, witumbilo wetu wose,

wuli kuli 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu go Mulungu wetu.

Hambi nha yo gwegwe du mono kwitumaga zizi zose.


Yelemiya 15

Woyi hee kuli wanhu wa Yuda

1 Hanyuma u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya nhavi, “Gwe Yelemiya, hamba yali yaze Musa na Samwili, wawe wemile bahano izuwali, wakuneneleza 'ne, nane hamba si nali niwawonele lusungu wanhu wawa ng'o. Holeka wuwawuse wabite, wawuce ha meso gangu.
2 Nawo kolo wakukuwuza vyono wabite hayi lo, nagwe wuwalonjele zono ane nonjile, nitya,

Woyi hee, hambi wanji nha wofwa ne matamwa,

na kuko ko wobita!

Woyi hee, wanji wofwila ku wulugu,

na kuko ko wobita!

Woyi hee, wanji wofwa ne lizala,

na kuko ko wobita!

Woyi hee, nawo wanji wosolwa wategulwe

kutali nha wafungwa,

nawo kuko ko wobita!

3 Nane no Muwaha Mulungu, nakondya kudumula nghani, nha vyono zizo nghani mbaha yine, zo zowagana. Wowulagwa no wulugu, ne miwili yawo yokwegwa ne mabwa, ne zindeje zoyilya, i malimu ga mu mbago, nago golya cono cowa cikali cisigaye.
4 Nane nowanoza i wanhu, wawe nghani ya wogofye, kuli wanhu wa mono'nze yose, ku soko ye nghani yono Manase u mwana wa Hezekiya, yetuma mu Yelusalemi, lono yali mutemi wa Yuda.”

5 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Alu nye nyo wanhu wa Yelusalemi,

alo nani mbi mono komuwonelenyi lusungu,

hamba nani lo, mono komulililenyi?

Hamba nani lo mono komuzizenyi yamuwuzenyi

zo wuwe wenyu?
