6 Hambi anye nyo wanhu, nha mwakondya kundeka 'ne,

nalu mwamhitucila migongo yenyu.

Ku soko yiyo, holeka nane nghawugolola

muwoko wangu, nghamongomezenyi,

hambi nha nali nalemwa kulimila likuwo lyangu.

7 Nane nghamupemhelusizenyi ne mbeho mu makaya gose

ga mu'si, kotya nha muli mahanze,

nghamongomezenyi, anye nyo wanhu wangu,

nghawawulaga ne wana wenyu,

vyono si mwaleka kwituma zinghani zenyu zimbi.

8 Sunga nalu mu yisi yenyu, mowali wacekulu wenji

muno i wafwile, no winji wawo winha nho

musawawa wa mwi'lenga.

Kanyhi nghawawulaga i wazelelo wenyu mu wuzelelo wawo,

nghawatajila mu wusungu wuwaha i wanyina zawo.

Nghawapela wusungu musunho, hamonga no wogofywe.

9 No nyina yo munhu mono yawajilize i wanagwe mupunghati,

yakondya kuzilika, yakuhangawa no wehele.

No wuzelu wakwe we lizuwa, alu wapituka wawa wutitu,

nalu yakolwa cevu no kuwa no wusungu wuwaha

mu nhumbula yakwe.

Nane nowaleta walugu wenyu,

wawawulaje wose wa muli nyenye wono wakali womi.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

Yelemiya yakumung'ung'ulicila u Muwaha Mulungu

10 Alu nhondo, neza nili munhuci waco, mono sina hamba nyemo yose! Hamba socici lo yaya yandela? Hambi nha neza nili munhu wa wugombane no wisumaniza na wanhu wanji wa mono'si. Ane ninga ho nghali si namukopesha yunji pesa zangu, hamba kukopa kwa yunji yaye. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, i wanhu vyo wakali wakundijita du.
11 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, nane nghutya, hono niwe ane si nakwitumamila viswanu, ale malijito gawo lece ganyizize, na hono niwe si nakunena, ku soko ye walugu wangu, lono wali wali mu wugaye, hamonga no wusungu wuwaha.
12 (Na hasina munhu wo wose mono kosuma kucibena cuma, na ko muno, cuma cono cikulawa kusumuka, hamba shaba.)

13 Kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Kanyhi nolowaleta walugu, waze wazisole zisawo ze wanhu wangu hamonga no wugoli wawo wose, wawalipicize ku soko yo wubi wono wetumile mu yisi yose.
14 Kanyhi nolowanoza wawetumamile i walugu wawo, mu yisi yono si wayimanyile yaye. Hambi likuwo lyangu nha linha moto, nalyo lyojendelela kwaka cibitilila.”

15 Hanyuma nane Yelemiya, nghapitula nghatya,

“Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe go mweneco wuzimanyile.

Ninga langa wunghumbucilaje,

wumhele wutazi.

Nghukunena wundipicizize simbili yangu kuli wose wono wakumhela magayo.

Wulece kuwakangalila wawo,

ho walece kusuma kumbulaga.

Kanyhi wukumbucile vyono ane nghutovulwa ku soko yako gwegwe.

16 Agwe wanonjela zinghani zako,

nane nghazihuliciza muno.

Ane nili wako,

agwe go Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga vyose.

Holeka ne zinghani zako zimemile

mu nhumbula yangu na nyemo hamonga no wusangalale.

17 Nane hamba si nali nendile kuvitaga vilejeho vyangu,

mu wuduwa ne wanhu wanji,

kwiyendeza bwete du mu luseko,

na mu wiyendeza ne zangu nene no mweneco.

Na ku soko ye cihuliko cangu ca kuli zo wulajilize wako,

holeka hanji, nekalaga nyamaye nili wene,

akuno memigwe ne likuwo.

18 Alu nhawule njendelele mu wusungu du?

Hamba alu socici ne zinghomele zangu si zikuponywa yaye?

Hamba nhawule lu si zikupona ng'o?

Gwe go Mulungu,

hamba wukusaka wunhukuwice mutima wangu,

nhe cikolongo cono cinyalaga mwibahu?”

19 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakamhitula yakatya,

“Hono wuwuye, nane nokugomola,

nagwe kowa wuli mwitumami wangu kanyhi.

Na hono wuzilece zinghani ze cibwete-bwete,

wuwe kuganula zinghani zangu,

baho langa ho kowa wuli muwizi wangu kanyhi.

Ne wanhu wokuziza gwegwe,

ninga agwe kowa wusina soko yo kuwabitila wene.

20 Nane nokunoza wuwe nhe lijinji lya shaba muli wanhu wawa.

Nawo wokwihoma nagwe,

ninga si wokusuma yaye.

Nane nowa hamonga nagwe ho nghudime no kukuponya.

21 Nane nokuponya mu miwoko ye wanhu wabi,

ne wanhu wagazi.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 16

U wuhile wo Muwaha Mulungu mu mikalile ya Yelemiya

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe Yelemiya, wulece kutola, hamba kulela wana, i hanhu hono henha bahano.
3 Ninga nane nokulonjela zono zowagana i wana wono wolelelwa baha, hamonga ne walezi wawo.
4 Hambi wose nha wofwa ku soko ye matamwa mabi, na kanyhi holowa hasina hamba yunji wo wose mono kolowalilila hamba kuwazika yaye. Ne miwili yawo, yolowa yilundile caka du ako na ako mu muhangha, kotya malundo ga suji. Nawo wolofwila mu wulugu, hamba mu nzala, ne miwili yawo yolowa ndigwa ye zindeje hamonga ne vilimu vya mu mbago.

5 “Nagwe, yikunojeye wulecele lukulu kwinjila mu kaya yo yose yono yina cililo. Kanyhi nagwe wulece kumulilila hamba munhu monga. Nane hamba si nowamotya wanhu wangu no mulamu yaye, hamba si nowalajila zo wendo wangu, hamba si nowawonela lusungu yaye.
6 Nawo i wagoli ne waciwa, wose wofwila mono yisi yiyi du, na holowa hasina hamba munhu monga mono kolowalilila, hamba kuwazika yaye. Kanyhi holowa hasina hamba munhu monga mono kolokwisalanga-salanga mu muwili wakwe, hamba kwiceta mu mutwe wakwe, ho yalajile wukalale wono yena nawo mu nhumbula yakwe ku soko yawo.
7 Kanyhi holowa hasina hamba munhu monga mono kololya ndigwa, hamba kung'wezana na yunji mono yafwiliywe no nduguye, ho yiwe nha yo nzila yo kumusindamika. Hamba hasina munhu monga, mono kolomuwonela lusungu, u munhu wo wose mono yafwiliywe no sogwe hamba nyina.

8 “Nagwe Yelemiya, nghukulonjela nha vyono kanyhi, wulecele lukulu kwinjila mu kaya yo yose yono yina zindigwa ze sikukuu, no kuhanga nawo mu wulye na mu wung'we.
9 Alu agwe wuhulicize zono nowanoza, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli. Ane nogadumya ha meso genyu, i mazi gose ge nyemo no wusangalale, hamonga ne mazozo gose ge nyemo, ga mu zindigwa zi nyinji ze zi halusi.”

10 “Nagwe kolo wukukondya kuwalonjela zizi zose, nawo wokuwuza i soko yono yikuleka ane ndumula nghani, nha vyono nowalipiciza vibi muno muno nhavivyo. Wokuwuza kanyhi wamanye i nghani 'mbi yono wetumile, hamonga no wubi wono wamutendeye u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wawo.
11 Nagwe wuwapitule, wuwalonjele zono u Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga yakutya nhavi, ‘I walezi wenyu, wandeka ane no Mulungu wawo, wakawa kuyihemba milungu yinji, no kuyitumamila. Wakalemela lukulu no kugahulika i mejizo gangu.
12 Anye nanye luno mwetuma vibi muno kuciza ne walezi wenyu. Nanye wose, mweza musina hamba cihuliko, kanyhi mweza muli wanhu wabi muno, hamba si mukusaka kuwa na cihuliko kuli'ne yaye.
13 Ku soko yiyo, nane nomuwusenyi mono yisi yiyi, nimutegulenyi ku yisi yono anye si muyimanyile yaye, yono ne walezi wenyu wali si wayimanyile ng'o. Nanye kuko, molowa kuyitumamila i milungu yinji misi ne cilo, nane hamba si nolomuwonelenyi lusungu ng'o.’ ”

U wuwuye wo kulawa ku wuhamywe

14 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “I vilo vikuza lono i wanhu si wolowa kwilahila kanyhi muli'ne, ane no Mulungu mumi, mono nawawusa wanhu wa Isilayeli ku yisi ya Misili.
15 Langa wolowa kwilahila muli'ne no Mulungu mumi, mono nawawusa wanhu wa Isilayeli mu yisi ya ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, hamonga na mu wulawa mu ma'si gose mono nali niweyagazize. Nane nolowagomola kanyhi mu yisi yawo, mu yisi yono nawapela i walezi wawo. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

U wulipicizwe wono wukuza

16 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Ane nowatuma wanimula somba wenji muno, waze wawanimule wanhu wawa. Hanyuma yayo, niwaceme i wapelembi wenji, waze wawapelembe mu wulawa mu matunda gose, na mu vigongo, sunga na mu zimhango ze mabwe.
17 Hambi'ne, nha nghuziwona zose zono wakwituma, hasina hamba cinji co cose cono cihundicigwe ha meso gangu. No wubi wawo wose nha wijeye du calinze ha meso gangu.
18 Nane nowalipiciza nghanghulo nyejete, ku soko yo wubi wawo, ne metumo gawo mabi. Hambi nha wayavufya yisi yangu ku soko ye milungu miseswe, yono yisina wumi, yihwanile ne mivimba du. No kuyimemya yisi yangu ne milungu yawo yo wulambi.”

Yelemiya yakulomba yapetuce hali Muwaha Mulungu

19 Gwe o Muwaha Mulungu,

yo gwegwe du mono kundimaga,

no kumhela vilungo,

kanyhi yo gwegwe du mono kumhelaga wutazi mu mapango go wugaye.

Ne makungugo nha golokuziza gakulawa

mu zimhelo zose ze yinze wawe kutya,

“I walezi wetu hamba si wahala cinji,

langa wahala i milungu yo wulambi,

hamonga ne vihwani viseswe,

vyono visina hamba cambuzi co cose.

20 Alu mwene,

mbeka u munhu kosuma kwinozeza mweneco milungu yakwe hongo?

Na mbeka hamba si kosuma yaye.

Na hono yayisese,

ayo hamba si yowa milungu ye mbeka yaye.”

21 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Lanje ta, alu noganoza i makungugo gose gaviwone vilungo vyangu hamonga no wusume wangu. Nawo nhondo ho womanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 17

U wubi no wulipicizwe wa Yuda

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Anye nyo wanhu wa Yuda, u wubi wenyu wandicigwe na kalamu ya cuma, wukakatwe mu zinhumbula zenyu ne cisonje ca alimasi, na mu zinhuzi ze vikumbicilo vyenyu.
2 Hambi i wanhu wako nha wahembaga ha vikumbicilo hamonga ne vihwani vyo mulungu we cicekulu Ashela, behi ne mabici gono gena mahamha madece mu matunda matali,
3 sunga na mu matunda gono gali mu'si mwi'hala. Alu nane nowanoza i walugu wenyu waze wayisole sawo yenyu yose, hamonga no wugoli wenyu, ku soko yo wubi wono mwetuma mu yisi yenyu yose.
4 Nanye yolowa yimunojelenyi muyilece yisi yono namupelenyi, nane nomunozenyi muwe wetumami we walugu wenyu, mu yisi yono si muyimanyile yaye. Hambi likuwo lyangu nha linha moto, nalyo lyowa kwaka cibitilila.”

I zinghani ze hayo hayo

5 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Nane nomulipiciza munhu wo wose mono yakundeka 'ne,

no kumwitumbila munhu muyagwe,

kawa kumunoza munhu muyagwe yiwa yo nyhito yakwe.

6 U munhu we liwala lilyo,

yehwanile nhe cititika cono cili mu nyika,

cono cikuwilucila mu nyika nghavu,

mu muhangha wo mwino,

i hanhu hono hasina cinji co cose cono ciwilukaga yaye.

Kanyhi hasina hamba nghani swanu,

yono yosuma kumuziza ng'o.

7 Ane langa, nomumotya u munhu

mono yanyitumbiye nene du.

8 Hambi u munhu yuyo,

nha kowa yehwanile no mubici wono wukuwilucila behi ne cikolongo,

wono wuhimbize milela yayo mu malenga.

No mubici wuwo,

si wogopaga lizuwa hamba likalipe muno.

Hambi i mahamha gayo, nha gekalaga gadedewaye du.

Hamba mvula yilece kuwutonyela,

wene wikalaga wusina mahwele yaye,

langa wene wujendelelaga kulela mikalo du.

9 “Alo mwene, nani 'mbi mono kosuma kuyimanya

nhumbula yo munhu?

Hambi nhumbula yo munhu nha yo nghoci muno

kuciza vinhu vyose,

kanyhi yina wutamwa wono si wosuma kuponywa yaye.

10 Ninga ane no Muwaha Mulungu,

yo nene du mono nigajelaga mahala,

no kuzisunjilila zinhumbula ze wanhu,

ho nimutendele munhu mu nghango ye zinzila zakwe,

mu nghango ye mikalo ye metumo gakwe.”

11 No munhu mono ketujilaga sawo mu zinzila zimbi,

yehwanile ndeje yono yigonelaga magangha

gono si yitajile yeneco.

Nayo koyajiliza sawo yakwe mu wuzelelo wakwe,

na ku ndumo yayo, kowa mulele du.

12 Ne Nyumba yo Mulungu wetu, yihwanile ligoda lye nhojo,

lyono lyasimywa mucanyha ye litunda ku wulawa na katali.

13 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe yo gwegwe go witumbilo wa Isilayeli,

no munhu wo wose mono kokuleka 'gwe,

kokwikoza cevu mweneco.

Hambi nha wolokwajilila wose,

nhe litagwa lyono lyandicigwe mu manghundi.

Ku soko vyono wakuleka gwegwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

go muvwilo we malenga momi.

Yelemiya yakulomba wutazi kuli Muwaha Mulungu

14 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghukulomba wumhonye,

nane noponela lukulu, wunhaze,

nane nokwikala mholile.

Hambi nha yo gwegwe du wuli wene,

mono nghukumyaga!

15 I wanhu nawo, wakumbuza wakutya,

“Nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu yili hayi lo?

Ale lu, wuwasolaje wuwawuse.”

16 Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe go mweneco, wumanyile nha vyono,

ane si calongana nagwe,

ho wuwaletele wongomezwe wanhu wawa.

Hamba si nakulomba ho wuwaletele lizuwa lye magazo yaye.

Ninga agwe wuzimanyile zose zono nalonga.

17 Holeka, nghukunena wulece kunyogofya 'ne,

hambi agwe,

nha yo gwegwe wutizizo wangu mwipango lyo wugaye.

18 Holeka 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuwakoze cevu, wose wono wakunyiwonela'ne.

Ninga 'ne nghukunena wundece 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu.

Wuwogofye wene muno muno,

ninga ane, wulece kunyogofya.

Wuwaletele wongomezwe, wuwabenanjize.

U wusiwanulwe we lizuwa lye Sabato

19 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Gwe Yelemiya, alu wubite wukeme ha mulango we wanhu, u mulango wono u mutemi wa Yuda kawitumamilaga lono yakwinjila no kulawila mwi'kaya. Wuwalonjele wanhu nghani yangu. Hanyuma ho wuwe kubita na mu milango yose ye likaya lya Yelusalemi.
20 Na mumo mose, wuwalonjele watemi, hamonga ne wanhu wose wa Yuda, sunga na wose wono wekaye mu Yelusalemi, no kwinjila mu milango yiyi, vyono yiwanojeye wose wazihulice zono ane nghulonga.
21 Wuwalonjele nha vyono, hono wawe wakali wendile kuwumipa, yiwanojeye walece kudinghula mitegulo yo yose mwi'zuwa lye Sabato. Hamba kuheleza cinhu cinji co cose mu milango ya Yelusalemi.
22 Hamba kulavyaniza cinhu cinji co cose, kunze ye zikaya zawo mwizuwa lilyo lye Sabato. Kanyhi walece hamba kwituma mulimo wunji wo wose mwizuwa lye Sabato. Langa iwanojeye wayinoze nhe lizuwa lisiwanule kuli wene, nha vyono nakondya kuwalajiliza i walezi wawo.
23 Ninga i walezi wawo, hamba si wahulika 'ne yaye, hamba kuzitajila mu zinhumbula zawo ng'o. Langa wene wakacila kuwa wasina cihuliko du kuli'ne, hamba si wasaka kugabocela ne mafundo gangu yaye.

24 “Holeka wuwalonjele wanhu wawa, nha vyono yiwanojeye wagakamise malajilizo gangu. Walecele lukulu kudinghula mutegulo wo wose, no kuwukoza mu milango ye likaya lili lya Yelusalemi, mwizuwa lye Sabato. Langa yiwanojeye wayibate muno Sabato, wayinoze nhe 'zuwa lisiwanule kuli wene. Holeka yiwanojeye walece kwituma mulimo wunji wo wose mwizuwa lilyo.
25 Holeka ne watemi wawo, hamonga ne wawaha wawo, wolosuma kwinjila mu milango ya Yelusalemi, wekale mwi'goda lyo wutemi lya Dawudi, ne wanhu wose wa Yuda na wa Yelusalemi, wose wolopanda mu mituka yo wulugu na mu zifwalasi. Ne likaya lya Yelusalemi, lyolowa likaye limemile wanhu nhondo zose.
26 Ne wanhu wose wolowa kwikongholela baho, wakulawa mu makaya gose ga Yuda hamonga na mu vitumbi vyono vipilime Yelusalemi. Na wanji wolowa kulawa mu yisi ya Benyamini, mucanyha ye matunda, na mu mwalambalo we vigongo, sunga na ku lubavu lwa kutakama yose ye yisi ya Yuda. Wolowa kuza baho mu Nyumba yangu, wakundetela vilavywa vinyanywe, ne makumbiko, ne vilavywa vyo wusaje, no wuvumba, hamonga ne vilavywa vyo wulumbize.
27 Ninga yiwanojeye wahulicize 'ne, no kuyikamisa Sabato, i lizuwa lisiwanulwe. Kanyhi walecele lukulu kudinghula mutegulo wo wose, no kuwukoza mu milango ya Yelusalemi mwizuwa lilyo. Na hono wejelele du wanoze nhavivyo, nane noyipembela moto milango yose ya Yelusalemi. No moto wuwo wologakongoga i makaya gose go wutemi ga mu Yelusalemi. Na holowa hasina hamba munhu monga mono kolosuma kuwuzimya moto wuwo.”


Yelemiya 18

Yelemiya ha kaya yo muwumbi

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya nhavi,
2 “Gwe Yelemiya, alu, ta wubite ku kaya yo muwumbi, nane kuko ko nokulonjelela i nghani yangu.”
3 Ale du, nane nghawa kubita, nghawa kumwagana u muwumbi, yali mu mulimo wakwe wo wuwumbi, yakwitumamila liguludumu.
4 Ne ciya co cose cono cejelaga si cilawiye viswanu lono yaciwumbaga, yasolaga lilongo lyaco, kawumba ciya cinji kanyhi, nha vyono yasakaga mweneco.

5 Nane lono nazilangaga zizo, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakutya,
6 “Wule! Alu ane si nosuma kumunozezenyi nhavivi, anye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli lo, nha vyono u muwumbi yalinozize lilongo lili? Nanye nha muli mu miwoko yangu, nha vyono linha lilongo mu miwoko yo muwumbi.

7 “Na mu mapango go gose, hono nene no mweneco sace, niwuwuse, hamba niwubenanje, hamba niwongomeze wutemi wo wose, hamba nghungugo yo yose,
8 na hono nghungugo yiyo yono niyitambuye yipituce no kuwuleka wubi wayo, hodu, nane si noyinoza vibi nha vyono nali nihwesize.
9 Na ku lubavu lunji, hono nitye, alu nowuhadika no kuwuzenga wutemi wo wose, hamba nghungugo yo yose,
10 na hono nghungugo yiyo yilece kuwa ne cihuliko kuli'ne, yijendelele kwituma ze metumo mabi, hodu nane si nosuma kuwatendela ziswanu zono nali nihwesize niwatendele.
11 Holeka luno, wuwalonjelaje i wanhu wa Yuda, hamonga ne wanyawusi wa Yelusalemi, nha vyono, nane nhondo, neza kwiwikaniza niwalipicize. Holeka wuwalonjele vyono, yiwanojeye walece kujendelela ne mikalile yiyo yo wituma wubi. Langa yiwanojeye luno wazipitule zinzila zawo, hamonga ne zimbi zono wakwituma.
12 Ninga wene wokupitula watye nhavi, ‘Aa, ng'o! Ase calema! Langa 'se, cojendelela du kugatyatya mahweso getu co weneco, no kwituma munhu no wuhile wakwe mweneco, nha vyono yakusaka mu nhumbula yakwe.’ ”

I wanhu wakumulema Muwaha Mulungu

13 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ale ta, wuwawuzaje we makungugo gose,

hono ku wulawa na katali, yejela nghani yono yinha yiyi.

Nye! I wanhu wa Isilayeli, mbeka wetuma nghani yo wizine!

14 Hamba mu mabwe matali ga mu Lebanoni,

ho hali mapango ganji, gono gekala gasina seluji lo?

Hamba i malenga ge mbeho, gono gakulawa

mu vikolongo, vyono vilawiliye mwi'tunda, vinyalaga lo?

15 Ninga i wanhu wangu wakondya kusemwa 'ne

nalu wakuvifusiza wuvumba i vihwani.

Nawo wekobaza mu zinzila zawo,

hamba si wakusuma kuzityatya kanyhi zinzila ze katali,

alu weza kukola mu zinzila zono si nozelezwe yaye.

16 Nalu, wakondya kuyinoza yisi yiyi,

yaweza cinhu ca kogofya muno muno,

alu yeza yisi yono yowa yibedegaywe cibitilila.

Na wose wono wowa kukola behi nayo,

wocelewala muno ku soko ya zono woziwona,

wowa kuyizugunza ne mitwe yawo ko wizine wuwaha.

17 Holeka nane luno, noweyagaziza wanhu wangu

kuli walugu wawo,

wowa nhe manghundi, gono gakuputwa

ne mbeho ya kucilima.

Nane nowapitucila mugongo.

Hamba si nowapela wutazi kanyhi,

mwizuwa lyo wugaye wawo.”

Yelemiya yakunozezwa ciwungu cibi

18 Hanyuma i wanhu nawo wakalongana weneco wose, wakutya, “Alu nye! Alenyi cimunozeze Yelemiya ciwungu cibi! Hambi nha cowa cina wakohani nhondo zose, wono wowa kucijiza, ne wanhu we mahala, wono wowa kucitonga, hamonga ne wawizi, wono wowa kuyiganula i nghani yo Mulungu. Holeka lu muze cimulonganje, cilece kuzihuliciza kanyhi ne zinghani zakwe.”

19 Ale nane baho, nghawa kumulomba Mulungu nghatya,

“Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghukunena wuhulice zono i walugu wangu wakumhinjila'ne.

20 Alo 'mbi u wuswanu,

wukuwa kulipwa i zimbi?

Hambi nha wakondya kuhimbila likombo,

ane ho ngwile mumo.

Ninga wukumbucile nha vyono,

nakuziza nghalongana nagwe ku soko ya wene,

nghukunena, nha vyono wulece kuwatendela mwi'kuwo lyako.

21 Ninga aluno 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuwalekaje wana wawo wafwe ne nzala,

nawo wawulajilwe ku wulugu.

Nawo i wacekulu, wawajilize i walume zawo,

hamonga ne wana wawo.

Nawo i walume matamwa gawawulaje,

ne wazelelo nawo wafwile ku wulugu.

22 Wuwategulile ciwumbi ca wanhu,

wakazitece zikaya zawo nhinicizo.

Wuwalece walile muno ko woga wuwaha,

hambi nha wakondya kuhimbila likombo,

ane ho ngwile mumo,

kanyhi wanhejile no mutego ho wangwize.

23 Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuwumanyiye lukulu wuhile wawo,

nha vyono wakusaka kumbulaga.

Holeka, wulece kuwanenela masutya gawo,

hamba kuwupata wubi wawo.

Langa wuwagwisaje hasi, wasumwe,

kanyhi wuwaling'anize mu madaha ge likuwo lyako.”


Yelemiya 19

Nyungu yono yitulice

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela mbite nghagule nyungu ya malenga ko muwumbi, niwasolaje ne wanyamhala wanji we wanhu, hamonga ne wakohani wanji,
2 hanyuma ngholele ku mulango wa Potishedi, mbite sunga ko kwi'lolo lyo mwana wa Hinomu. Nghaganule kuko zinghani zono kononjela.
3 Hanyuma yakanonjela mbite nghatye nhavi, “Anye nyo watemi wa Yuda, hamonga ne wanyawusi wa Yelusalemi, ale muhulicize ta, zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli nghusaka kuzilonga. Ane nohaletela wongomele wuwaha muno i hanhu baha, u wongomele wono munhu wo wose kolo yakuzihulika, zinghani zizi, mbeka kocelewala.
4 Nane nozituma zizi, vyono i wanhu wangu wakondya kundeka, no kuhavufya i hanhu baha, so wulavya makumbiko kuli milungu yinji, i milungu yono weneco, hamba i walezi wawo, hamba i watemi wa Yuda, wali si wayimanyile yaye. Nawo wakondya kuhamemya hanhu bahano, ne sakami ye wanhu wono wasina masutya.
5 Kanyhi wakondya kumuzenjela Baali vikumbicilo vinji, vyono wowa kuwanyanyila i wana wawo mu moto, wawe likumbiko linyanywe kuli Baali. Ne nghani yiyi, yo nghani yono ane si nawalajiliza yaye, yo nghani yono nghali nehwesa na mu nhumbula yangu.
6 Holeka ku soko yiyo, i vilo vikuza, lono i hanhu bahano, si holocemwa kanyhi Tofeti, hamba Lilolo lyo mwana wa Hinomu. Langa lyolomanyika nhe Lilolo lyo Wuwulaji.
7 Ne hanhu bahano, ho nologewajiziza i mahweso ge wanhu wa Yuda, hamonga ne wanyawusi wa Yelusalemi. Nolowaleta i walugu wawo, waze wawasume, no kuwawulajila ku wulugu. Ne mivimba yawo, nolozipela zindeje ziyilye, hamonga ne malimu ga mu mbago.
8 Nane nololeta wogofywe, hamonga no wuhodanjice mumu mwi'kaya lilino, sunga ne wanhu wose wono wowa kukola behi nalyo, wowa kuyisinjisa ne mitwe yawo ko wizine wuwaha.
9 Ne walugu nawo wololipilima likaya, wawawulaje i wanhu wose wono wali mumo. No wupilimwe wuwo we likaya, wolowa wa wogofye muno. Hambi i wanhu wono wolowa wali mugati, nha wolowa kwiyilya weneco wose, wolowa kulya sunga ne wana wawo.”

10 Hanyuma ya zizo, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela niyitule nyungu yiyilya, niyitulile ha mwande ye wanhu wawalya wono nabitana nawo.
11 Niwalonjele nha vyono, u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Ane nowabenanjiza i wanhu wawa, hamonga ne likaya lili, nawo wokwihwana ne nyungu yiyi ye lilongo, yono si yosumika kuyilunza hamonga kanyhi mazaye gayo. Nawo i wanhu, wolowa kuyizika mivimba ye wandugu zawo, sunga na kuko ku Tofeti, hambi nha holowa hasina kanyhi hanhu hanji ho hose ho kuwazika.
12 Nane nehuwiza nha vyono nolinozeza likaya lili, hamonga ne wekazi walyo wose, nha vyono nayinoza Tofeti.
13 Ne zikaya zose za Yelusalemi, ne zikaya zose ze watemi wa Yuda, du ne zikaya zose, zikaya zono zafusiswaga wuvumba kuli zinyelezi, hamonga ne hanhu hono halavizwaga i vilavywa vye divayi, ku soko yo kuyilavizya milungu yinji. Wawo wose wokwihwana ne Tofeti i nyavufywe.”

14 Hanyuma ya zizo, hodu, nghawa kuwuka kuko ku Tofeti, kono u Muwaha Mulungu yanhuma, mbite nghayiganule nghani yakwe. Nghawa kubita kwima ha mahakama ge Nyumba yo Mulungu, nghawa kuwalonjela wanhu,
15 nha vyono u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli katya nhavi, “Alu nane noliletela likaya lili, wulipicizwe wuwaha muno, hamonga ne vikaya vye behi behi, u wulipicizwe wono nali nawulonga. Hambi anye, nha muli wawanji muno, musina cihuliko, hamba si mosuma yaye kuzihulika zono 'ne nghuzilonga.”


Yelemiya 20

Wisumanize wa Yelemiya no mukohani Pashuli

1 Nayo Pashuli mukohani, u mwana wa Imeli, mono yali yo mulindilizi muwaha we Nyumba yo Mulungu, yali yeza kuhulika vyono ane nghuziganula zizi,
2 hobaho yakawa kulajiliza nhowe, mbopwe ne manyolo behi no mulango wa Benyamini wa mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.
3 Ne mitondo yaco, lono Pashuli yali yakali nambopolela mu manyolo, ane nghamulonjela nghatya, “U Muwaha Mulungu si yakucemela litagwa lilyo lya Pashuli, langa lya ‘Nghakamo Hanhu Hose.’
4 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu, mweneco yo yalonga nha vyono, ‘Nokunoza wuwe munhu wa nghakamo gwegwe mweneco, na kuli wayago wanji. Nagwe kolowawona wose wakuwulagwa ko mumaje we walugu wawo. Nane nowatajila i wanhu wose wa Yuda, mu miwoko yo mutemi wa Babiloni. Nayo kowasola wanji nha wafungwa, yawategule kutali ku yisi yakwe, na wanji nawo kowawulaga.
5 Kanyhi nowaleka i walugu wawo, waze wayisole ne sawo yose ya mwi'kaya lili, wavisole ne vinhu vyayo vyose, hamonga ne viwutwa vye watemi wose wa Yuda, wavitegule ku Babiloni.
6 Sunga na gwegwe Pashuli, hamonga na we mhuga yako, wose molotekwa mutegulwe ku Babiloni. Nagwe Pashuli, kolofwila kuko, wakuzice kuko, hamonga ne wayago wono wali wuwendile, wono wawalonjelaga nghani nyinji zo wulambi.’ ”

Yelemiya yakumung'ung'ulicila Muwaha Mulungu

7 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, kunghonga'ne,

nane niwa kukongwa-kongwa.

Hambi agwe nha wina vilungo wunyhizize'ne

kumbusiza sunga ne vilungo vyangu,

nalu wanhu wose wakusekanga,

wakunzwajiza misi yose.

8 Na ho hose lono nghulonga, nghulila du nzozize, nghutya,

“Wukali! No Wongomele.”

Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, alu'ne nha nghuhong'ongwa,

hamonga no kusowa misi yose.

Ku soko'ne vyono nghuyiganula nghani yako.

9 Na kolo nghutya,

“Alu hodu ciba nimusemwe Muwaha Mulungu

ndece no kulitambula ne litagwa lyakwe.”

Hobaho nghuyiwona nghani yako yinha moto

yikunyanya mu nhumbula yangu.

Nane nghujela muno muno kukangalila,

ninga hamba si nghusuma kwiyimila yaye.

10 Nane nghuwahulika wanhu wose wakupwepwa wakutya,

“Nghakamo yili hanhu hose!

Holeka, alenyi le, cikawalonjele wono wena wulajilize.”

Sunga ne weyendi wayangu, wose walindiye du,

wawone wugwe wangu, akuno wakutya,

“Na lunji kosuma kukocwa-kocwa,

nase baho ho cosumila kumwibata, cilipicize.”

11 Ninga 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nha wuli hamonga nane,

yo gwegwe wuli vilungo vyangu,

hamonga no wusume wangu.

Nawo wono wakungaza,

wose wogwa citawima,

hamba si wosuma yaye,

langa wokozwa cevu cibitilila.

Ne cevu cawo hamba si colosemigwa yaye.

12 Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga

kuwajelaga wanhu we ling'ani.

Nagwe wuzimanyile zose zono zili mu zinhumbula zawo,

na mu mahweso gawo.

Holeka ane nghulindiye mbone wulipicizwe

wono kowalipiciza i walugu wangu,

hambi 'ne nha nakulecela gwegwe zinghani zangu.

13 Ale, mwimbilenyi u Muwaha Mulungu!

Mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu!

Hambi nha kamuponya mono yali kwiwonelwa,

mu miwoko ye wanhu wabi.

14 Lilijitwe lizuwa lyono nalelelwa,

Lilece kulugutizwa yaye,

lizuwa lilyo lyono yaya yandelela.

15 Yalijitwe munhu yuyo mono yamupela baba nyemo,

mono yamulonjela zinghani yakutya,

“Kulelelwa mwana we cilume.”

16 Yimunojeye munhu yuyo, yawe nhe makaya gago,

gono u Muwaha Mulungu yagongomeza yasina lusungu.

Yahulice cililo co wusungu ca hakali mitondo

hamonga ne lizozo lyo wulugu,

lya mu mapango ge misi.

17 Hambi nha si yambulaga'ne

lono nali nghali nalelwa,

ne 'nda ya yaya, yali yiwe co citenjelo cangu.

18 Alu mwene nye! Nhawule'ne nghalelwa?

Alo mwene nalelwa ho nhuje magaziko no wukalale hongo?

Ne vilo vyangu vimalicile mu cevu du?


Yelemiya 21

U wugwe wa Yelusalemi wukuwilwa

1 Zedekiya u mutemi wa Yuda, yakamutuma Pashuli u mwana wa Malikiya, hamonga no mukohani Zefaniya, u mwana wa Maseya, waze wanonjele nha vyono,
2 “U mutemi Zedekiya yakutya, vyono, wucilombele ase kuli Muwaha Mulungu. Hambi Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, hamonga ne lipuga lyakwe lye wasilikale, nha walipilima likaya yacihome. Lunji u Muwaha Mulungu, kosuma kucinozeza nghani monga yo wizine, yamuwinje Nebukadineza yacilece.”

3 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakalongana nane, baho nane nghawa kuwalonjela i wanhu wawalya, wono watumwa kuli'ne,
4 wabite wakamulonjele Zedekiya, zono u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli yalonjile, katya, “Agwe Zedekiya, ane no mweneco, yo nokwihoma ne wasilikale wako, wono wakwihoma ne wasilikale wa Nebukadineza, u mutemi wa Babiloni, niwasume. Nokongholeza sunga ne vitwalo vyawo vyose, nivilundice haciligati ye likaya lya Yelusalemi.
5 Nane nokwihoma nagwe kwe vilungo vyangu vyose, na kwe likuwo lyangu liwaha, hamonga na ko wukali wangu wose.
6 Nane nowawulaga wose wono wekaye mumo mwi'kaya, i wanhu, hamonga ne zimitugo, vyose vyofwa ko wutamwa wubi muno.
7 Ninga agwe go mweneco, ne wetumami wako, hamonga ne wanhu wono wosigala, wono si wofwa ko wulugu, hamba nzala, hamba matamwa, anye wose nomulecenyi musolwe no mutemi Nebukadineza, hamonga ne wasilikale wakwe wono wali saka wakuwulaje. Nayo Nebukadineza kolokuwulaga 'gwe. Kanyhi hamba si kolosigaza munhu monga muli'nye. Hamba si kolomuwonelenyi lusungu, hamba ku munenelenyi yaye. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

8 Kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela vyono niwalonjele i wanhu nitye nhavi, “Ale, nanye nyo wanhu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghumuwicilenyi ha mwande yenyu nzila nyejete, ho musume kwihaga nyenye nyo weneco, nzila monga muli zizo zinyejete, nazo zozino Nzila yo Wumi, ne Nzila ye lifwa.
9 U munhu wo wose mono kokwikala mu muno mwi'kaya, ayo kowulagwa no wulugu hamba ne nzala, hamba ne litamwa. Langa mono kosuma kwilavya kuli Wababiloni wono wowa wakulihoma likaya, ayo yuyo si kowulagwa yaye, langa kokwiponya wumi wakwe.
10 Hambi 'ne nha nakondya kudumula nghani, nha vyono si nolileka yaye likaya lili, langa nolyongomeza du. Nolikombola lisolwe no mutemi wa Babiloni, yalipembele moto likongojele. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

U wuling'anizwe we nyumba yo wutemi wa Yuda

11 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela, niwalonjele we nyumba yo wutemi wa Yuda, wo lulelo lwa Dawudi zinghani azi, nitye,

“Ale, muhulicize zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghumutyenyi.

12 Munojeye muwe kuling'aniza wuling'anizi we ling'ani nhondo zose.

Kanyhi mweze kumutaziza munhu wo wose,

mono yakukongwa-kongwa no kwiwonelwa no munhu yunji.

Na hono mulece kwituma nhavivyo,

u wubi wono mukwituma,

wonghoza likuwo 'ne,

nalyo lyokwaka nha moto,

wono si wosumika no kuzimywa yaye.

13 Nagwe Yelusalemi,

mono wikaye wijeye hacanyha ye malolo,

mono wihwanile nhe litalawe lyono lilawiliciye mu yisi yo wuhihi.

Agwe mono wukwiyitya,

nha vyono hasina munhu wo wose mono kokuhoma,

hamba kugakumula magomawakali gako.

14 Ninga 'ne nokuhoma nghulipicize,

ku soko ye metumo gako.

Nolipembela moto,

likaya lyako lyo wutemi,

nivikongojeze ne vinhu vyose vyono vilipilime.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 22

I nghani ya Yelemiya, kuli nyumba yo wutemi wa Yuda

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya, mbite ku kaya yo wutemi, yo mutemi wa Yuda, wo lulelo lwa Dawudi.
2 Nghamulonjele u mutemi ne wetumami wakwe wose, hamonga ne wanhu wose wa Yelusalemi, wayihulicize nghani yono yakulonga u Muwaha Mulungu, yakutya,
3 “Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghumulajilizenyi nha vyono munojeye mweze kwituma ze ling'ani na zo mugoloka. Mweze kumutaziza u munhu wo wose mono yakukongwa-kongwa no kwiwonelwa no munhu yunji. Kanyhi mulecele lukulu kuwewonela, hamba kuwatendela vibi i wajenzi, hamba i wana wono wasina walezi, hamba i wacekulu wafwile. Hamba mulece kuwawulajila i hanhu hono helile, i wanhu wono wasina masutya.
4 Na hono mwitume nha vyono nghumulajilizenyi, ale, no lulelo lwa Dawudi lojendelela kuwa watemi. Kanyhi wene, hamonga ne wetumami wawo, ne wanhu wawo, wojendelela kukola mu milango ye kaya yiyi yo wutemi, hamonga ne mituka yawo yo wulugu, ne zifwalasi zawo.
5 Ninga hono mulece kugebata malajilizo gangu, mbeka nghumwilahilenyi nha vyono, likaya lili lyo wutemi lyologwa, gawe matongo du. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

6 “Ninga ku lubavu lwangu,

ane nghuyiwona kaya yo wutemi wa Yuda,

swanu muno yihwanile nhe yisi ya Gileyadi,

nhe matunda ga Lebanoni.

Ninga ane noyinoza yiwe yisi nyhiwa,

yono yowa yisina munhu wo wose mono kokwikala mumo kanyhi.

7 Hambi nha notuma wanhu wabite wakayongomeze,

ne wanhu wawo,

woteguliza hamonga ne zinhemo zawo,

wakayitemanje ne mihagata,

i mibici yayo miswanu muno,

wayipembele mu moto.

8 “Na hanyuma yayo, i wajenzi wolowa kukola baho akuno wakwiwuza weneco wose, wakutya, alo so cici u Muwaha Mulungu, kalitendela likaya lili liwaha, zono zinha zizi lo?
9 Ninga kanyhi wokwipitula weneco wose wawe kutya, vyono, zizi zimwaganenyi ku soko anye mwakondya kucileka i Cilagane cono mwacinoza nane, ane no Mulungu wenyu, mukawa kuyihemba no kuyitumamila milungu yinji.”

I nghani ya Yelemiya kuli za Yowahazi

10 Nye nyo wanhu wa Yuda,

mulece kumulilila u mutemi Yosiya ye,

hamba mulece kulila ku soko yo wufwe wakwe.

Langa mumulililaje ko wusungu wuwaha muno,

Yowahazi u mwanagwe,

hambi nha womusola, wamutegule kutali,

nayo si kolowuyila lukulu

hamba si koloyiwonela lukulu kanyhi

ne yisi yono yalelelwa.

11 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu, kuli za Yowahazi u mwana wa Yosiya, mono yawuhala wutemi wo sogwe Yosiya, yakawa mutemi wa Yuda, yakutya, nhavi, “Alu, kakondya kubita kutali, hamba si kolowuyila lukulu kanyhi.
12 Langa kolofwila du kuko ku yisi yono kolotegulwa. Hamba si koloyiwonela lukulu kanyhi yisi yiyi ng'o.”

I nghani ya Yelemiya kuli za Yehoyakimu

13 Woyi, hee! Kuli munhu mono yakuzenga kaya yakwe

mu nzila yono si ye ling'ani,

no kuyonjeleza yiwe mbaha, mu nzila yono si ye ling'ani.

Mono kawetumyaga i wanhu wanji bwete,

hamba kuwalipa musahala wawo.

14 Woyi hee! Kuli munhu mono katezaga,

“Ane nozenga kaya yo wutemi

yono yowa yina vyumba vigazi muno

ku golofa yayo.”

Ale, hanyuma kawa kuwicila sunga

ne madilisha mu kaya yakwe,

kayigubika ne mihagata mucanyha yayo,

kayihiliwa ne lanji ndunghu.

15 Alu mwene gwee!

Kolo wazenga kaya swanu ko mubici wo muhagata,

alo kaya yiyo yo yokunoza wuwe mutemi muswanu lo?

Mono kowa wuli muswanu,

wucizize sunga ne watemi wanji lo?

Ninga wumanye nha vyono,

u sogo yekala mu mikalile yo wumoti,

Nayo yali yali ling'ani, na we mbeka vilo vyose.

Na ku soko yiyo, yakamoteswa mu milimo yakwe yose.

16 Yawaling'anizaga waciwa kwe ling'ani,

holeka na zose zikamuwela swanu muno.

Na yiyi yo yikulajila nhunizo yo

kumumanya Muwaha Mulungu.

17 Ninga agwe wukukwegwa no wihwesa zako du go mweneco,

wukuwawulaga i wanhu wono wasina masutya

wukuwewonela i wanhu wako ko wukali.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

18 Holeka u Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga za kuli Yehoyakimu u mwana wa Yosiya u mutemi wa Yuda, yakutya nhavi,

“Hasina hamba munhu monga mono kolocililila cifo cakwe,

hamba kucilonga-longa kose yawe kutya,

‘Ka, nye! Mhola ndugu yangu si!’

Hamba hasina munhu mono

kolomulilila hamba kwikunguza yawe kutya,

‘Mhola mulongola wangu!

Mutemi wangu!’

19 Nayo kolozikwa nha vyono ndogowe yizikwaga,

nha cinji, kolokwegwa, yawe kugumilwa du,

kunze ye milango ya Yelusalemi.”

I nghani ya Yelemiya kuli ze zimbali zono zoyagana Yelusalemi

20 Nye nyo wanhu wa Yelusalemi,

alu mubite ku Lebanoni,

mukalile muzozize,

bitenyi na ku yisi ya Bashani,

kuko nako mukalile,

mucemeleze mu wulawilila mu matunda ga Mowabu,

hambi i wendece wako wose nha wakondya kongomela.

21 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu, nha yalongana nagwe,

lono wali wukali wina no wumoteswe,

ninga agwe wukalema kumuhuliciza,

Nagwe wukawa wusina cihuliko mu mikalile yako yose,

hamba si wasacila lukulu kumuhulika Muwaha Mulungu.

22 Holeka i walongola wako,

wolopemhelusizwa kutali ne mbeho,

ne wendece wako nawo wolosolwa nha wafungwa mu wulugu,

ne likaya lyako, nalyo mbeka lyolokozwa cevu no kupuwa.

Ku soko yo wubi wose wono wetuma.

23 Nagwe mono wikaye wupolile mu mihagata

yono yaletwa ku wulawa ku Lebanoni,

kolowa wuli munhu wa kuwonelwa lusungu muno,

kolo wusungu wakwagana,

u wusungu wono wihwanile wo mucekulu

mono yali mu nghunga yo lusawo.

Wuling'anizi wo Mulungu kuli Yehoyakimu

24 U Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela u mutemi Yehoyakimu, u mwana wo mutemi Yehoyakimu, u mutemi wa Yuda, yakatya, “Na mbeka, nha vyono yo nene u Mulungu mumi, holeka hamba wuwe agwe wali wuli nyamwidole yangu ye citanjilo, mu muwoko wangu wo kulume, ninga alu nokuwopola,
25 nghuwice mu miwoko ye wanhu wono agwe wuwogopile muno, i wanhu wono wakusaka wakuwulaje. Nokuwika mu miwoko ya Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, hamonga ne wasilikale wakwe.
26 Nokusola gwegwe hamonga no nyina yako, nimutegulenyi ku wuhamywe. Nanye molotegulwa sunga ko ku yisi, yono hasina hamba monga wa muli nyenye, mono yalelelwa kuko. Nanye wose molofwila kuko du.
27 Nanye lono muli kuko, moloyisukwa muno muno yisi yenyu yiyi, ninga hamba si molowuyila lukulu ng'o.”

28 Nane nghatya,

“Alo mutemi yuyu Yehoyakimu,

yehwana njilo ye malenga yono yakondya kutulika,

yakondya kutagwa kunze,

hambi nha yeza yisina cambuzi lo?

Hamba yiyi yo soko yono yikaleka mwene,

hamonga ne wanagwe wose,

wakahamywa,

wakategulwa ku yisi yono si wayimanyile yaye lo?”

29 Gwe go'si, gwe go 'si, gwe go'si!

Wuhulicize ta zono Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga, yakutya,

30 “U munhu yuyu,

yakondya kuling'anizwa yawe yasina wana,

yawe munhu mono si kolomoteswa kanyhi yaye.

Kolowa yasina kanyhi wana, wono wolotema mu yisi ya Yuda,

wawe wo wahazi we ligoda lyo wutemi lya Dawudi.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 23

U witumbilo wa mu vilo vyono vikuza

1 Ninga u Muwaha Mulungu, kolowalipiciza wulipicizwe wuwaha muno, i walongola wono wakuwahodanga, no kuweyagaza wanhu wakwe!
2 Azi zo yakulonga u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, kuli ze walongola wono wo wanojeye kuwadima wanhu wakwe, yakutya nhavi, “Alu lanje, hamba si mukuwadima wanhu wangu yaye, langa mwaweyagaza no kuwawinjila kutali. Holeka nane ku soko yiyo, nomulipicizenyi ku soko yo wubi wono mwetuma.
3 Nane nolowakongholeza i wanhu wangu wono wolowa wasigaye, ku wulawa mu ma'si gose kono nali niweyagazize, nolowagomola kanyhi ku yisi yawo weneco. Nawo wolotuga wana wenji, no konjelezeka muno liwalo lyawo.
4 Nane baho, nene no mweneco, nolowahagulila i walongola wawo wono wolowadima. Na mu vilo vivyo, i wanhu wolowa wekaye wasina woga hamba mutinu bwete. Nane hamba si nolowalipiciza kanyhi yaye. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

5 Alu u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “I vilo vikuza, vyono nene no mweneco, nolokwihagulila u mutemi we ling'ani mu wulawa mu lulelo lwa Dawudi. No mutemi yuyo kolotema kwe mahala, kanyhi kolowa kwituma ze ling'ani na zo mugoloka mu yisi yose.
6 No mutemi yuyo kolo yatema, i wanhu wa Yuda, wolowa wekaye wapolile. Hambi u mutemi yuyo nha kolocemwa, ‘U Muwaha Mulungu Muponyi wetu.’ ”

7 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “I vilo vikuza lono i wanhu si wolowa kwilahiza mwi'tagwa lyangu, ane no Mulungu mumi, mono nawawusa wanhu wa Isilayeli mu yisi ya Misili.
8 Langa wolowa kwilahiza mwi'tagwa lyangu ane no Mulungu mumi, mono nawawusa wanhu wa Isilayeli ku wulawa mu yisi ya kulubavu lwa kusumuka, na mu ma'si ganji gose, kono nali niweyagazize. Nawo nhondo, ho wolokwikala mu yisi yawo weneco.”

I nghani ya Yelemiya kuli ze Wawizi wabi

9 Nye nhumbula yangu yapaya si,

sunga nalu, neza kucicima.

Neza nho munhu mono yagalile wujimbi,

nho munhu mono yang'weye divayi nyinji muno,

ku soko yo Muwaha Mulungu,

na ku soko ye zinghani zakwe zono zelile.

10 Alu yisi yose yimemile

i wanhu wono si wehuwilwa kuli Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi wanhu nha wekaye mu mikalile yo wituma ze metumo mabi,

ne vilungo wasoceye.

Nalu yisi yikulila ku soko ye lilijito lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

no wudimo wose wakondya kunyala.

11 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Hambi i wawizi hamonga ne wakohani,

wose, nha weza si wakumucelelela Mulungu,

hambi nha nakondya kuwawona

wakwituma zimbi mu Nyumba yangu.

12 Holeka ku soko ya zizo, zinzila zawo zono wakolile,

zolowa na cinhelezi hamonga ne citi.

Nane nolowanoza wekobale, wagwe,

nowaletela magazo mawaha,

ne lizuwa lyo wulipicizwe wawo nha likuza.

Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

13 Nane nakondya kuwuwona u wubi we wawizi wa ku Samaliya.

Hambi nha wakulonjela mwi'tagwa lya Baali,

nalu wakondya kuwasutizya i wanhu wangu.

14 Nghondya kuwawona sunga ne wawizi,

wono wali mu Yelusalemi, nawo wakwituma vibi muno muno,

hambi nha wakwituma zo wusenyha,

akuno wakulonga zo wulambi,

wakuwatonga wanhu ku wituma zimbi du.

Na ku soko yiyo, holeka hasina hamba monga,

mono yakusuma kuleka kwituma zimbi.

Holeka ku lubavu lwangu, ane nghuwawona

vyono wose wabi du,

wehwanile ne wanhu wa Sodoma na Gomola.

15 “Holeka ku soko yiyo, nane no Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, nghuwalonjela i wawizi wa mu Yelusalemi, nghutya nhavi,

Nowapela libici likali walye,

hamonga ne sumu, waying'we.

Hambi nha wakondya kuwutandaza

wu wula kumucelelela Mulungu, mu yisi yose.”

16 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga yakuwalonjela i wanhu wa mu Yelusalemi, yakutya nhavi, “Nye nyo wanhu wa mu Yelusalemi, mulece kuwahuliciza i wakohani, zono wakulonga ye. Hambi nha wakumupelenyi witumbilo wo wulambi. Wakumulonjelenyi ze zindoto zawo weneco du, ninga ane vyo sinonjile yaye.
17 Wene wakuwalonjela i wanhu wono wasina cihuliko kuli'ne, nha vyono zose zowawela swanu du. Na kanyhi wakuwalonjela wose wono wasina cihuliko, nha vyono magayo go gose si gowagana yaye.”

18 Nane nghatya nhavi,

“Muli wawizi wawo wose,

hasina hamba muwizi monga,

mono yasuma kugamanya,

i mahweso ge cinyele go Muwaha Mulungu.

Kanyhi hasina hamba monga muli wene,

mono yayihulika nghani yakwe,

yakayizuka, hamba kuyimanya kose,

hamba kwenda kuyihulika kose,

hamba kucelelela kose,

kuli zono Muwaha Mulungu yakuzilonga.

19 Ninga likuwo lyakwe lyo mukukunzo,

lyono linha mbeho nghali,

lyowaziza i wabi wose.

20 Nalyo si lyowuka yaye,

sunga yawe yakondya kuzifisiliza zose,

zono yali kusaka kuzituma.

Na mu vilo vivyo vyono vikuza,

i wanhu wakwe nhondo,

ho woloyimanyila lukulu nghani yiyi calinze.”

21 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ane si nawatuma yaye i wawizi wawa,

ninga wene wakabita.

Hamba 'ne si nawalonjela nghani yangu yo yose

ninga wene wakabita kulonjela mwi'tagwa lyangu.

22 Ninga mbeka hono wali wawe wagamanyile mahweso gangu ge cinyele,

baho langa,

ho wali wasume kuyiganula nghani yangu kuli wanhu wangu,

no kuwanoza wasume kugaleka i metumo gawo mabi,

gono wetumaga hamonga ne zinzila zawo zimbi.

23 “Ane yo nene Mulungu mono nili hanhu hose, nane si nikalaga hanhu hamonga du yaye.
24 No munhu wo wose hamba si kosuma kwihundika ha meso gangu, nane ho ndece kumuwona. Hamba si mumanyile yaye vyono ane nikaye hanhu hose, ku wulanga na mono 'nze?
25 Nane nha nizimanyile zose zo wulambi zono i wawizi wawo wakulonjela mwi'tagwa lyangu, akuno wakutya ati, yo nene mono niwalonjeleye zinghani zangu mu zindoto.
26 Alu mwene i wawizi wawa, ho wojendelela sunga nili no wulambi wawo, no kuwajiliza i wanhu wangu?
27 Wene wakwihwesa nha vyono i zindoto zawo zono wakuzilonga, zosuma kuwanoza i wanhu wangu wasume kundeka 'ne, nha vyono, i walezi wawo wasemwa 'ne, wakawa kuhemba Baali.
28 U muwizi mono yena zindoto, yalonje ze zindoto zakwe du. Nayo muwizi mono yena i nghani yangu, yayilongaje nghani yangu ko wihuwilwa. Alo mawaje gena cambuzici, ho wugehwanicize no wuhemba?
29 Ninga i nghani yangu, yihwanile moto mukali, kanyhi yihwanile ne nyondo yono yitulangaga mabwe mawaha gawa vibwe vidodo dodo.
30 Holeka ku soko yiyo, ane no Muwaha Mulungu nili mulugu we wawizi wawo, wono wakwiboka munhu na muyagwe zinghani zangu,

31 “Kanyhi ane nili mulugu wa wawo i wawizi, wono wakuganula zinghani zono zikulawa mu mahweso gawo weneco du, hanyuma wawa kutya vyono yo nene mono niwatumile.
32 Alu muhulicize ta, zono nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu! Ane mbeka, nili mulugu we wawizi wawo, wono wendile kulonga ze zindoto zawo, zono zimemile wulambi wose du. Hambi nha wakwitumamila zizo zindoto zawo, no kuwajiliza wanhu ku soko yo wulambi wawo, hamonga no widunhumuzi wawo. Ninga ane hamba si niwatumile yaye, hamba kuwalajiliza kose. Kanyhi wasina hamba nghani yo yose, yono yosuma kuwataza wanhu. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

U mutegulo wo Muwaha Mulungu

33 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya nhavi, “Gwe Yelemiya, hono munhu wo wose wa muli wanhu wangu yakuwuze, u muwizi, hamba mukohani, yawe kutya, ‘Alu wuli hayi u mutegulo wono u Muwaha Mulungu yakucidinghuza lo?’ Nagwe wuwalonjele wuwatye, ‘Anye nyo wanhu yo mweza muli mutegulo muwaha kuli Muwaha Mulungu, holeka nayo komutajilenyi lukulu.’
34 Na hono munhu wo wose wa muli wanhu wangu, hamba muwizi, hamba mukohani, hono yetumamile nghani yo wutya, ‘U mutegulo wuli hayi wo Muwaha Mulungu.’ Nane nomulipiciza u munhu yuyo, hamonga na wa mu nyumba yakwe wose.
35 Ninga langa, yiwanojeye wawe kwiwuza munhu ne weyendi wayagwe, hamba ne wanduguze wakutya, U Muwaha Mulungu kapitula katya nhawule, hamba kalonga nghanici?
36 Ninga si yiwanojeye wajendelele kwitumamila zinghani zo wutya, ‘U mutegulo wo Muwaha Mulungu.’ Na hono munhu wo wose yejelele du yanoze nhavivyo, nane noyinoza nghani yangu mbeka yiwe mutegulo kuli munhu yuyo. Hambi i wanhu nha wakondya kuzileka zinghani zo Mulungu wawo, u Mulungu mumi, u Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga.
37 Holeka agwe Yelemiya, wuwawuzaje ta wawizi, wuwatye nhavi, ‘Munonjelaje ta, lipitulo lyono u Muwaha Mulungu yamupitulenyi? Hamba u Muwaha Mulungu kalonga katyaci?’
38 Na hono walibedegale lilajilizo lyangu, no kujendelela ne nghani yiyo du yo wutya, ‘U mutegulo wo Muwaha Mulungu. Ale wuwalonjelaje wutye,
39 vyono, ane nowasemwela lukulu, niwagumile kutali muno muno, i wanhu weneco, hamonga ne likaya lyono nali nawapela, hamonga ne walezi wawo.
40 Nane nowaletela cevu ce cibitilila, hamonga no wubuganice wono wowa kukumbucilwa nhondo zose.’ ”


Yelemiya 24

Nhoto nyejete zono zina nghuyu

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakandajila nhoto nyejete zono zali zina nghuyu, zono zali ziwicigwe hamwande ye Nyumba yo Mulungu. (Na zizi zejela lono Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, yali yakondya kumusola ku Yelusalemi u mwana wa Yehoyakimu, mono yacemwaga Yehoyakimu, u mutemi wa Yuda, no kumutegula nha mufungwa ku Babiloni, hamonga ne walongola wose we yisi ya Yuda, ne wanghula mu wuzenji, hamonga ne watyani we vyuma.)
2 Ne nhoto monga ya muli zinhoto zizo, yali yimemile nghuyu swanu muno, zono zali zipiye lulo baho. Nhoto nyagwe, nayo yali yimemile nghuyu zono 'mbi muno, zono sizisumice hamba ku wulye kose.
3 Ale, baho u Muwaha Mulungu yakambuza yakutya, “Gwe Yelemiya, alu wukuwona cinhuci?”

Nane nghamupitula nghamutya, “Nghuwona nghuyu. Nghuyu ziswanu muno, ne zinghuyu zimbi muno muno, zono si zisumice hamba ku wuligwe kose.”

4 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
5 “Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, mono nghuwehweseza muno muno, i wanhu wangu wono wasolwa no kutegulwa ku Babiloni, hambi nha wehwanile ne zinghuyu zizo ziswanu muno. Holeka nane nowatendela ko wuswanu muno.
6 Nowadima sunga nize niwagomole, wawuye mono yisi yiyi, kanyhi nowakamisiza mu ziswanu zose, hamba si nowaleka yaye. Nowazenga, hamba si nowamwagula yaye. Kanyhi nowahadika, hamba si nowakula yaye.
7 Nane nowasikamya, ho wamanye ane, vyono yo nene u Muwaha Mulungu. Nawo wowa wanhu wangu, nane nowa Mulungu wawo. Hambi lulo, nha wolombuyila 'ne, ko mutima wawo wose.”

8 “Na kuli za Zedekiya, u mutemi we yisi ya Yuda ne wawaha wakwe, hamonga ne wanhu wa Yelusalemi, wono wasigala mu yisi yiyi, hamonga na wono wabita ku Misili, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nowatendela wawo wose, nha vyono zinha zinghuyu zizi zono mbi muno muno hamba ku wulye kose.
9 Nane noloweyagaziza mu zinghugugo zose za kono'nze, ho watuje wongomezwe wubi. Ne wanhu wose wolowezwajiza no kuwahosa hamonga no kuwasekanga, no kuwalijita, ho hose hono noloweyagaziza.
10 Nane nowaletela wulugu, ne nzala, hamonga ne matamwa, sunga kolo hakuwa hasina hamba munhu monga muli wene, mono kolosigala mu yisi yono nawapela wene, hamonga ne walezi wawo.”


Yelemiya 25

I walugu wono wakulawa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka

1 Na mu mwaka we kane, wa mu wutemi wa Yehoyakimu u mwana wa Yosiya, mu yisi ya Yuda, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela za kuli wanhu wa Yuda. (No mwaka wuwu, wali wo mwaka mulongozi, wo wutemi wa Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni.)
2 Nane nghawa kuwalonjela i wanhu wose we yisi ya Yuda na Yelusalemi nghawatya,
3 “Nye wayangu! Mumanyile nha vyono mu myaka yiyi yose makumi mejete ne 'datu, ku wulawilila mu mwaka we likumi ne 'datu wo mutemi Yosiya, u mwana wa Amoni, lono yali yatemile mu yisi ya Yuda, sunga mwi'zuwa lye zuwali u Muwaha Mulungu kanonjelaga zinghani zakwe, ho nimulonjelenyi nanye. Nane nghali si naleka hamba kamonga ku wulawa lulo, kumulonjelenyi zose zono yakuwa kunhuma nimulonjelenyi. Ninga 'nye, hamba si muzityatililaga yaye.
4 Holeka yikwijela nha vyono kumbe, hamba yiwe u Muwaha Mulungu, yajendelela kumutumilenyi i wetumami wakwe wawizi, ninga anye hamba si mwawahulika yaye.
5 Wene wawizi wakamulonjelenyi wakatya, ‘Ale, mupituce muzilece zinzila ze mikalile yenyu mibi, na mu wubi wenyu wono mwitumaga, ho musume kubita kwinjila no kwikala mu yisi, yono u Muwaha Mulungu yamupelenyi nyenye hamonga ne walezi wenyu, ho yiwe wuhazi wenyu we cibitilila.
6 Ne wawizi wawo wakamulonjelenyi kanyhi, nha vyono munojeye mulecele lukulu, kuyihemba, hamba kuyitumamila milungu yinji, hamba kumukoza likuwo u Muwaha Mulungu, so wuhemba vihwani vyenyu vyono mwisesese nyo weneco. Nanye lulo, hono mwali mumuhulice Muwaha Mulungu, nayo si yali yamulipicizenyi yaye.
7 Ninga u Muwaha Mulungu mweneco, yakutya nha vyono, anye si mwamuhulika ng'o, langa mukacila kumukoza likuwo, ku soko ye vihwani vyenyu viseswe. Na ku soko yiyo, mukeletela wulipicizwe nyenye nyo weneco.’

8 “Holeka vyono mukalema kumuhulika mwene, alu nayo u Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, yakutya nhavi,
9 ‘Mulanje, ta! Nane nowatuma i wanhu wo lubavu lwa kusumuka, hamonga no mwitumami wangu Nebukadineza, u mutemi wa Babiloni. Waze wayihome yisi ya Yuda, ne wekazi wayo wose, hamonga ne makungugo gose gono gayipilime. Nane nolyongomezeza lukulu likungugo lili, hamonga ne wekalani wayawo wose, niwalece mu wongomele we cibitilila, wawe cinhu co kogofya no kuhowelezwa, hamonga no wucelewazwe wuwaha. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.
10 Kanyhi nowawusiza lukulu ne mazi gawo go luseko, ne mazi gawo ge nyemo, hamonga ne zinyemo zawo ze zindigwa ze zi halusi. Kanyhi wolowa wasina hamba mafuta so zitala zawo, kanyhi holowa hasina hamba wuhemba yaye.
11 Ne yisi yiyo yose, yololekwa nyhiwa hamonga no wucelewale. Nago i makungugo gono gapilime, golomwitumamila Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, myaka makumi mupunghati.
12 Ne myaka yiyo kolo yakondya kupuluta, nane noloyilipiciza Babiloni, hamonga no mutemi wayo, ku soko yo wubi wawo. Noloyongomezeza lukulu yisi yiyo, niyilece matongo cibitilila.
13 Noloyilipiciziza Babiloni mu magazo gose gono nagalonga katali, nha vyono nalonga na Yelemiya, nha vyono nologaleta magazo gago muli zinghungugo zose. Nha cinji, i magazo gose gono gandicigwe mu citabu cicino.
14 Nane nolowalipa i Wababiloni mu nghango ye metumo gawo. Ne makungugo menji, hamonga ne watemi wawaha, wolowanoza Wababiloni wawe wawanda wawo.’ ”

U Mulungu yakugaling'aniza makungugo

15 Hanyuma kanyhi, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Gwe Yelemiya, ahano hocili cikombe cono cimemile likuwo lyangu. Alu, wucisolaje, wubite naco kuli zinghungugo zose kono nokutuma, nagwe wuwang'wese cikombe cico.
16 Nawo kolo wakondya kucing'wa cikombe cico, wogala no kulelepa ku soko yo wulugu wono nowaletela.”

17 Ale, nane nghawa kucibocela cikombe cico mu mawoko go Muwaha Mulungu, nghawa kuwapela i makungugo gose, kono Muwaha Mulungu yanhuma, nawo wakacing'wa cikombe cico.
18 Nayo Yelusalemi ne makaya gose ge yisi ya Yuda, hamonga ne watemi wayo, ne walongola wayo, wose wakacing'wa cikombe cico, ho wanozwe wawe nyika no wizine, hamonga no wuhowelezwe. Ne wanhu walitumamila litagwa lyawo nha wulijitwe, nha vyono wanozaga.
19 Ali lye 'walo lya wanji wose wono wali wanojeye wacing'we cikombe cico. U mutemi wa Misili, ne wetumami wakwe, hamonga ne walongola wakwe.
20 I wamisili wose, ne wajenzi wose wono wali mumo mu Misili. Hamonga ne watemi wose we yisi ya Uzi, ne watemi wose we makaya ge Wafilisiti na Ashikeloni na Gaza na Ekiloni, na wose wono wowa wasigaye wa mu Ashidodi.
21 Hamonga ne wanhu wose wa mu yisi ya Edomu, na Mowabu ne yisi ya Amoni.
22 Hamonga ne watemi wose we zi'si za Tulo na Sidoni, ne watemi wose we zi'si zono zipilime lilenga lya Meditelaneya.
23 Hamonga ne makaya ga Dedani, na Tema, na Buzi, ne wanhu wose wono wenyonyolaga zimvwili zawo ziwa fupi.
24 Hamonga ne watemi wose we yisi ya Alabiya, ne watemi wose we zimbeyu zose wono wekaye mu nyika.
25 Hamonga ne watemi wose we zi'si za Zimuli, na Elamu, na Mediya.
26 Hamonga ne watemi wose we zi'si zo lubavu lwe sumuka, ze behi na zono zili kutali, zowa kung'wa cikombe cico zikwilecela. Ne makungugo gose du gono gali mono'nze, gong'wa cikombe cico. Nayo mutemi wa Babiloni, ayo kolong'wa cikombe cico ku ndumilizo ya wose.

27 Hanyuma kanyhi, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Ale, wuwalonjele wanhu, nha vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yo nene mono nghuwalajiliza wose wang'we wagale muno muno, sunga wadece, wagwe ho hasi, walece kusuma hamba no kwinuka kose, ku soko yo wulugu wono ane nowaletela.
28 Na hono wejelele, waleme kucibocela cikombe cico mu mawoko gako, no kucing'wa, wuwalonjele nha vyono, u Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, vyo yalonjile nha vyono yiwanojeye wose wacing'we du cikombe cico.
29 Nane nowanza u mulimo wangu wo kongomeza, nowanziza mwi'kaya lyangu no mweneco. Alo wene wakwihwesa nha vyono si wolipicizwa lo? Ng'o yaye, langa wolipicizwa du. Hambi 'ne, nha nowaletela wulugu i wanhu wose wono wali mono'nze. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu, we Mihanga.

30 “Gwe Yelemiya, yikunojeye wuziganule zose zono nghulonjeye. Wuwalonjele wanhu wawa zizi zose.

U Muwaha Mulungu kokoloma ku wulawa ku wulanga,

nayo kokwinula muno lizi lyakwe ku wulawa mu

wikalo wakwe wono welile.

Kolowakolomela wanhu wakwe, kolozoza nha munhu mono yakukanyaga zabibu.

Ne wanhu wose wa mono'nze, wolomuhulika.

31 Ne lizi lyakwe lyolobocelela sunga ko ku zimhelo ze yinze.

Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yakwisumaniza ne makungugo.

Nayo kolowaleta wanhu wose ha wuling'anizwe,

yawasole i wanhu wabi yawawulaje.

Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.”

32 U Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga yakutya,

wongomele wukuza,

nawo wowa kwitananhiza muli makungugo gose limonga limonga.

No mukukunzo muwaha wokwinulwa wulawa mu zimhelo ze 'nze.

33 Na mwi'zuwa lilyo, i miwili ya wono wolowulagwa no Muwaha Mulungu, yolowa yiyagaye caka ku wulawilila ku mhelo ayino ye 'nze, sunga ko ku mhelo nzagwe. Na holowa hasina munhu wo wose mono kolowalilila, hamba si wolowuswa baho hono wowa wagwiye no kuzikwa yaye. Langa wolowa walabalaye baho hasi du, nhe malundo ge mboleya.

34 Ale, mucenje lwanji,

mulile anye nyo walongola,

anye nyo wadimi we wanhu wangu.

Mulile mwihindile muno muno!

Mulile, mwigalagaze mu mavu.

Hambi i vilo vyenyu vyo wuwulagwe wenyu,

nha vyakondya kufika.

Nanye mokasikwa nha nyama ya ngholo.

35 Na mwi'zuwa lilyo,

molowa musina hanhu ho hose hono molokwiponyezya.

36 Holeka luno, mulile ko wusungu wuwaha muno,

hambi u Muwaha Mulungu, so likuwo lyakwe,

nha kakondya kulyongomeza likungugo lyenyu,

37 no kuyileka yisi yenyu yo mulamu yeza matongo gose.

38 U Muwaha Mulungu, yawaleka i wanhu wakwe,

nha vyono simba yiyilekaga mhango yayo.

Ne yisi yawo yakondya kuwa nyika

ku soko ya wogofywe wuwaha wo wulugu,

hamonga no wukali we likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu.


Yelemiya 26

Yelemiya vikwitang'anila ne vimizi

1 Ha mwamulizo wo wutemi wa Yehoyakimu u mutemi wa Yuda u mwana wa Yosiya,
2 u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Ale, wime he wanza lye Nyumba yo Mulungu, wuziganule kuli wanhu wose wono wakulawa mu makaya ga Yuda, wakuza kuhemba bahano. Wuwalonjele zinghani zose zono nghulajilize kuli wene. Ninga wulanjilile, wulece kusigaza hamba nghani monga yaye.
3 Na lunji wanhu wohulika, wazilece zinzila zawo zimbi. Na hono wanoze nhavivyo, nane, si nowongomeza kanyhi, nha vyono nali nakondya kwihwesa niwongomeze, ku soko yo wubi wawo.”

4 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela nha vyono, niwalonjele wanhu nitye nhavi, “Ane no Muwaha Mulungu nalonga nha vyono, anye munojeye muwe na cihuliko kuli 'ne, mugebate i mejizo gangu gono namwijizenyi.
5 Kanyhi na vyono, munojeye muzicelelele zinghani ze wetumami wangu i wawizi, wono nikalaga nghumuletelenyi ku wulawa na katali. Ninga anye si mwazicelelela yaye zono wamulonjelajenyi.
6 Ninga luno mwilanjililaje muno muno, vyono hono mujendelele du kuwa musina cihuliko, nane noyinozeza i Nyumba yangu yiyi, nha vyono nayinozeza Shilo. Ne makungugo gose ge yinze, wolitumamila litagwa lye likaya lili nhe 'lijito.”

7 Ale, du, i wakohani ne wawizi, hamonga ne wanhu wose, wakahulika lono nazilongaga zinghani zizi baho ha Nyumba yo Mulungu.
8 Nane nali neza kukondya kuzilonga zose zono Muwaha Mulungu yali yandajilize, hodu, hobahobaho du wakawa kunyibata, wakawa kuzoza wakutya, “Agwe Yelemiya, mbeka kowulagwa, ku soko ye zinghani zako zizi!
9 Alu nhawule wukulonjela mwi'tagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu, nha vyono Nyumba yiyi yo Mulungu, yolokwihwana Shilo, kanyhi na vyono, likaya lili lyolokongomezwa, na vyono kanyhi, holowa hasina hamba munhu monga mono kolokwikala mumo?” Hodu, hobaho i wanhu wenji wakawa kumhilima mbavu zose.
10 Ale, nawo i wawaha wa mu yisi ya Yuda, wali weza kuzihulika zono zijeye, hobaho, nawo wakawa kuza musunho wakulawilila mwi'kulu, wakawa kwima hanhu hawo, ha mulango mupya.
11 Hanyuma i wakohani hamonga ne wawizi, wakawalonjela i wawaha wawo, hamonga ne wanhu wose, wakutya, “U munhu yuyu, mbeka yanojeye yaling'anizwe yafwe. Hambi nha kalonga nghani 'mbi kuli likaya lyetu. Nanye mumuhulika nyo weneco kwe makutu genyu.”

12 Nane nghapitula nghatya, “Nye wayangu, u Muwaha Mulungu, yo yanhumile 'ne, nonje zose zizi zono muzihulice nghuzilonga, za kuli Nyumba yo Mulungu, na za mwi'kaya lili.
13 Holeka anye munojeye du, mukawanize i mikalile yenyu mibi yono mwikalilaga, hamonga ne zinghani zenyu zimbi zono mwitumaga, kanyhi munojeye mumuhulice u Muwaha Mulungu u Mulungu wenyu. Nanye hono mwitume nhavivyo, nayo kowupitula wuhile wakwe wo kumongomezenyi, nha vyono yali yakondya kwihwesa.
14 Ninga ku lwangu, ane nili mu miwoko yenyu du! Holeka mosuma kunhendela nghani yo yose yono mukuyiwona yo swanu, kanyhi yili ling'ani.
15 Ninga hamba nhavivyo mweze mumanyiye lukulu, nha vyono, hono mumbulaje, nyenye hamonga ne wanhu wose wa mwi'kaya lili, mowa mwenjila mu masutya so kumuwulaga munhu, mono yasina lisutya lyo lyose. Hambi 'ne nha Muwaha Mulungu du mweneco, yo yanhumile nize nimusugulenyi muli zizi.”

16 Ale, i walongola hamonga ne wanhu, wakawa kuwalonjela i wakohani hamonga ne wawizi wakatya, “Nye, u munhu yuyu, kacilonjela zizo zose mwi'tagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu, holeka hamba si yanojeye yawulagwe yaye.”
17 Hanyuma ya zizo, i walongola wanji wakema ha mwande ye lipuga lye wanhu wose wono wali wekongholeye baho, wakawalonjela wakatya,
18 “Nye wayetu, mu vilo lono Hezekiya yali mutemi we yisi ya Yuda, yakaza muwizi yunji, mono yacemwaga Mikaya, munya Molesheti, u muwizi yuyo yawalonjela wanhu nha vyono, u Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga yalonga yakutya,

‘Agwe go Sayuni kololimwa nha mugunda,

Nagwe Yelusalemi kolowa yilundo lye matongo,

Ne litunda lye Nyumba yo Mulungu, lyolowa mbago.’

19 Ninga u mutemi Hezekiya, hamonga ne wanhu wa Yuda, hamba si wamuwulaga Mikaya yaye. Langa Hezekiya yakalajila cogopo du hali Muwaha Mulungu, yakamunena yawawonele lusungu. Nayo Muwaha Mulungu, yakawupitula wuhile wakwe, hamba si yawaletela kanyhi wongomezwe, wono yali yehwesa nha vyono kowaletela. Holeka nase cilanjilile, hambi nha cikusaka ciyiletele magazo mawaha sese co weneco.”

20 (Na kanyhi, hali na munhu yunji litagwa lyakwe Uliya, u mwana wa Shemaya, munya Kiliyati Yeyalimu, mono nayo yalonjela mwi'tagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu kuli ze likaya lili, hamonga ne likungugo, nha vyono yanozize Yelemiya.
21 Ninga u mutemi Yehoyakimu ne wasilikale wakwe, hamonga ne wetumami wakwe, wali weza kuzihulika zizi zono yazilonga Uliya, hobaho u mutemi yakasaka Uliya yawulagwe. Ninga Uliya yali yeza kuzihulika zizo, yakawa kutiziza ku Misili so woga.
22 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, u mutemi Yehoyakimu, yakatuma Elinatani u mwana wa Akiboli, hamonga ne wanhu wanji, wakabita ku Misili wakamulondole Uliya wamulete.
23 Nawo wakabita, wakamuleta Uliya kuli mutemi Yehoyakimu, nayo mutemi Yehoyakimu yakawa kumuwulaga, yakawa kuwuzika muwili wakwe mu vitenjelo vye wanhu wose.)
24 Ninga Yelemiya, vyono yali yali mu wutazi wa Ahikamu u mwana wa Shafani, holeka siwamukombola mu miwoko ye wanhu ho yawulagwe.


Yelemiya 27

Yelemiya yakuvwala lubano

1 Mu mwamulizo wo wuteme wo mutemi Zedekiya u mutemi wa Yuda, u mwana wa Yosiya, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela,
2 niyinozeze no mweneco lubano lwa viwopelo vya mikowa, hamonga ne mabici gayo, nivwice mu singo yangu.
3 Hanyuma kanyhi, yakanonjela mbite nghawalonjele zono yanhumile, i watemi wa Edomu, na Mowabu, na Amoni, na Tulo, hamonga na Sidoni. Niwalonjele mu nzila ya wono watumwa, wono wazaga ku Yelusalemi kuli mutemi Zedekiya.
4 U Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yanonjela nha vyono, niwalajilize wawo wabite wakawalonjele i watemi wawo, zono Muwaha Mulungu yalonjile nhavi,
5 “Mumanye vyono yo nene mono nayiwumba yinze kwe vilungo vyangu, hamonga no munhu, ne vilimu vyose vyono vikaye mono'nze. Nane nimupelaga munhu wo wose nha vyono nyendile nene no mweneco.
6 Kanyhi yo nene mono nagawika makungugo gaga gose mu miwoko yo mwitumami wangu Nebukadineza, u mutemi wa Babiloni. Nghavinoza ne vilimu vyose vya mu mbago vimwitumamile mwene.
7 Sunga ne makungugo gose golomwitumamila mweneco. Golomwitumamila sunga no mwanagwe, sunga no mwizukuluwe. Sunga kono vilaficila i vilo vyo wugwe we likungugo lyakwe, nalyo likungugo lyakwe ho lyolowetumamila i makungugo gono gali vilungo hamonga ne watemi wawaha.

8 “Ninga hono haze hejele likungugo linji lyono si lyolosaka litemwe no mwene Nebukadineza, nane nololilipiciza likungugo lilyo no wulugu, ne nzala, hamonga ne matamwa, sunga kolo mutemi Nebukadineza yawe yakondya kulyongomezeza lukulu.
9 Holeka 'nye wayangu, mulecele lukulu kuwahuliciza wawo wawizi wenyu, hamba kuli munhu wo wose mono yakwiyitya nha vyono kosuma kuwila za ku mwande, mu nzila ye zindoto, hamba mu nzila yo wucemeleza myuhe ye wanhu wono wafwiye, hamba mu nzila yo wuhawi. Wawe kumulonjelenyi nha vyono mulece kwiyilavya kuli mutemi Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni.
10 Holeka mumanye nha vyono i wanhu wawo, wakumukonjenyi du bwete, ho wamwambuzenyi muze musolwe mu yisi yenyu, no kutegulwa kutali. Nane nize nimuwinjenyi mu yisi yenyu, mubite mukongomele.
11 Langa likungugo lyo lyose, lyono lyolokwilavya mu miwoko ya Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, no kumwitumamila, nane nololileka likungugo lilyo likale mu yisi yalyo lyeneco, liyilime no kwikala mumo. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

12 Nane Yelemiya, nghamulonjela Zedekiya u mutemi we yisi ya Yuda, zinghani zizo zose nha vyono zinha, nghamutya nhavi, “Alu 'gwe go mutemi Zedekiya, wiwikaje mu miwoko ya Nebukadineza, u mutemi wa Babiloni. Wumwitumamile hamonga ne wanhu wakwe, holeka nagwe kowumipa.
13 Alo socici mukusaka mwiyambuze nyo weneco, hamonga ne wanhu wako mufwile mu wulugu, hamba mu nzala, hamba mu matamwa? Hambi nha vyo yateye u Muwaha Mulungu, vyono zizo zo zolyagana likungugo lyo lyose, lyono lyolema kwiwika mu miwoko ya Nebukadineza, u mutemi wa Babiloni.
14 Holeka 'gwe mutemi, wulecele lukulu kuwahulicila, wawo wawizi wono wakukutya vyono, wulece kwiyilavya kuli Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni. Hambi nha wakukukonga du bwete.
15 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu mweneco, nha yalonga nha vyono, mwene si yawatumile yaye. Langa wakuwila zo wulambi du mwi'tagwa lyakwe. Ho yamuwinjilenyi kutali, mubite mukawulaganwe, hamonga ne wawizi wawo wono wakumulonjelenyi zinghani zo wulambi.”

16 Hanyuma nghawa kuwalonjela nawo i wakohani hamonga ne wanhu wose, zono Muwaha Mulungu yalonga, nghawalonjela nawo nghatya nhavi, “Ale, nanye mulecele lukulu kuwahuliciza i wawizi wono wakumutyenyi nha vyono, i viya vye Nyumba yo Mulungu, vyologomolwa kanyhi ku wulawa ku Babiloni. Wakumukonjenyi bwete.
17 Holeka mulece kuwahulicila! Anye langa mwiyilavye mu miwoko yo mutemi wa Babiloni du, ho muwumipe! Alo socici lo, mulyambuze likaya lili, liwe malundo ga matongo?
18 Nawo hono wawe wawizi mbeka, na hono nghani yangu yiwe yili muli wene, ale, walombe kuli'ne, ane no Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga ndece kuvikombola i viya vyono vyasigala mu Nyumba yangu, na mu nyumba yo mutemi wa Yuda, na mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi, vilece kusolwa no kutegulwa ku Babiloni.”

19 (Vyono mu vilo vyono mutemi Nebukadineza, yamusolela Yehoyakimu u mutemi we yisi ya Yuda, u mwana wa Yehoyakimu, no kumutegula ku Babiloni, hamonga ne wawaha wose wa Yuda na wa Yelusalemi.
20 Siyasoleleza yaye ne masumbili, ne litanji lye shaba lye malenga, ne mikokoteni, hamonga ne viya vinji vya mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.)

21 “Ale, lu muhulicize, ane no Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga ane no Mulungu wa Isilayeli, zono nghutya, kuli ze viya vye Nyumba yo Mulungu vyono vyasigala mu Nyumba yo Mulungu na mu nyumba ya mutemi wa Yuda, na mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi.
22 Navyo vyolokwikala kuko du, sunga mwi'zuwa lyono nene no mweneco nilavikumbucilila. Ale baho nhondo, ho nolovigomelela kanyhi, no kuviwika hanhu bahano. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 28

Yelemiya no muwizi Hananiya

1 Na mu mwaka wuwo du kanyhi, mu mwezi we 'hano, wa mu mwaka we kane, wo wuteme wa Zedekiya. Hananiya u muwizi, u mwana wa Azuli, munya cikaya ca Gibeyoni, lono nali nili mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, ha mwande ye wakohani, hamonga ne wanhu, Hananiya yanonjela
2 nha vyono, u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga u Mulungu wa Isilayeli yakutya, “Nakondya kuvibenanga vilungo vyose vyo mutemi wa Babiloni.
3 Na mwi'pango lya myaka myejete, ane nowa nakondya kuvigomola hanhu baha, i viya vyose vya mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, vyono u mutemi Nebukadineza yavisola, yakavitegula ku Babiloni.
4 Kanyhi nomugomola na Yekoniya u mwana wa Yehoyakimu u mutemi wa Yuda, hamonga ne wanhu wose wa Yuda wono wahamywa wakategulwa ku Babiloni. Na mbeka, novibenanga vilungo vyo mutemi wa Babiloni. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

5 Nane Yelemiya, nghamupitula Hananiya, ha mwande ye wakohani, hamonga ne wanhu wose wono wali wemile mu Nyumba yo Mulngu, nghamutya,
6 “Heya viswanu! Nane nitumbiye vyono, u Muwaha Mulungu kokwituma zizi! Nane nitumbiye vyono, kowunoza u wuwile wako, wuwe wa mbeka, yavigomole baha i viya vyose vye Nyumba yo Mulungu, ku wulawa ku Babiloni, hamonga ne wanhu wose wono wali wasolwa nha wafungwa.
7 Ninga luno, ta wuhulicize zono nane nghukulonjela, hamonga ne wanhu wose wono wali baha.
8 I wawizi wono wacilongolela katali na katali, lono agwe na ane cikali si nawa, wawila nha vyono, wulugu, ne nzala, hamonga ne matamwa, golowaziza we makungugo menji, gono gena wutemi we vilungo viwaha.
9 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, u muwizi mono yakuwila nghani zo mulamu, ne nghani yo muwizi yuyo kolo yeza kwijela, baho langa, u muwizi yuyo, ho komanyika vyono yuyo yo muwizi mbeka, mono Muwaha Mulungu yamutumile.”

10 Hanyuma ya zizo, Hananiya yakawa kuluwusa lubano mu singo yangu, yakawa kulubenanjiza,
11 yakawa kulonga ha mwande ye wanhu wose yakutya, “U Muwaha Mulungu katya nha vyono, nhavivi vyo kolubenanjiza lubano, lono u mutemi Nebukadineza, yali yagavwicile mu zisingo makungugo gose. Nayo kozituma zizi mu myaka yiyi myejete yono yikuza.” Hodu nane Yelemiya, nghawa kuwuka.

12 Hanyuma ya zizi, vilo vyali kupuluta hadodo, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanhuma kanyhi ane Yelemiya mbite,
13 cikalongane na Hananiya nghamutye nhavi, “U Muwaha Mulungu kalonga nha vyono, agwe Hananiya, heya kosuma kulubenanjiza lubano lwe mabici, ninga mwene konoza lubano lunji lwa cuma.
14 U Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, kalonga nha vyono, kogavwika lubano lwa cuma i makungugo gaga gose, nago ho gajendelele kumwitumamila Nebukadineza, u mutemi wa Babiloni. Kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yalonga yakatya, nha vyono koganoza sunga ne malimu ga mu mbago gose, gamwitumamile Nebukadineza.”

15 Namulonjela Hananiya zizi, ninga kanyhi nghonjeleza nghamutya, “Alu Hananiya! Wuhulicize ta! U Muwaha Mulungu hamba si yakutumile yaye, langa agwe wukuwanoza i wanhu wawa wawihuwile u wulambi wako.
16 Na ku soko yiyo, nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nha vyono, kokuwusa mono'nze. Holeka agwe kofwa mwakawu na lono mwaka wuwu wukali namalika. Ku soko vyono kuwanoza i wanhu wamubeze Muwaha Mulungu.”
17 U muwizi Hananiya, yakafwa mu mwaka wuwo mu mwezi wo mupunghati.


Yelemiya 29

Baluwa ya Yelemiya, kuli Wayahudi wa ku Babiloni

1 Hanyuma nghawa kwandika baluwa kuli wakohani, ne wawizi, ne walongola we wanhu, na kuli wanhu wose wono wasolwa na Nebukadineza, ku wulawa ku Yelusalemi, no kutegulwa ku Babiloni nha wafungwa.
2 Baluwa yiyi, nghayandika lono u mutemi Yehoyakimu hamonga no nyina, ne wetumami wose wa mwi'kulu, ne walongola wose wa Yuda na wa Yelusalemi, ne wanghula ku wuzenji, hamonga ne watyani, wali wakondya kusolwa no kutegulwa ku wuhamywe ku Babiloni.
3 Nane nali neza kuyandika baluwa yiyo, nghawa kumupeleza Elasa mwana wa Shafani, na Gemaliya mwana wa Hilikiya, wono u mutemi Zedekiya yawatumaga wabite kuli Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni. Ne baluwa yiyo yali yandicigwe zinghani azi.

4 “U Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yakumulonjelenyi anye nyo wanhu wose, mono mwene Mulungu, yamukombola Nebukadineza, ho yamusolenyi ku wulawa ku Yelusalemi, yamutegulenyi ku Babiloni nha muli wafungwa,
5 yakutya nhavi, ‘Alu, muzenje zikaya, mwikale mumo, muhadice mu migunda, mulye mikalo yayo.
6 Mutole wacekulu, mulele wana we cilume na we cicekulu. Muwatoze ne wana wenyu we cilume na we cicekulu, nawo walele wana we cilume na we cicekulu nhavivyo, ho musume konjelezeka kuko, hamba mulece kucepuka yaye.
7 Mwitumaje ziswanu za mu makaya gago, kuko kono namunozenyi ho muwe wafungwa. Mugalombele kuli Muwaha Mulungu i makaya gago. Ku soko, hono gatuje wumoteswe, nanye nhavivyo mowa mwamoteswa.
8 Ninga ane no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, nghumusugulenyi nha vyono, mwilanjililaje! Mulecele lukulu kukongwa ne wawizi wono mwikalane hamonga, hamba ne wanhu wanji wo wose, wono wakwiyitya nha vyono ati, wosuma kuwila za mu vilo vyono vikuza. Mulecele lukulu hamba kuzihulicila zindoto zawo.
9 Hambi nha wowa kumulonjelenyi zinghani zo wulambi du, wakuzilonjela mwi'tagwa lyangu. Ninga ane hamba si niwatumile yaye. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.’

10 “U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, ‘Kolo myaka makumi mupunghati yikufisilizwa mu Babiloni, nane baho ho nolomuzizenyi, no kucifisiliza cilagane cangu, nimugomolenyi kanyhi ku yisi yenyu.
11 Hambi 'ne, nha nigamanyile mahweso gangu gono nghumwihwesezenyi anye. Mahweso go kumuletelenyi wumoteswe, hamba si wongomele yaye. Mahweso go kumupelenyi 'nye zo witumbilo wa mu vilo vyono vikuza.
12 Nanye molonyhemeleza. Kanyhi molonyiziza, mololomba, nane nolomupitulenyi.
13 Nanye molonondola, molombona, hambi nha molonondola kwe mitima yenyu yose.
14 Heya, mbeka molombona, nane nolomugomolenyi kanyhi ku yisi yenyu. Nolomukongholezenyi ku wulawa mu ma'si gose, ne hanhu hose hono namwiyagazizenyi. Nolomugomolenyi kanyhi sunga mu yisi yenyu, yono namulecenyi musolwe no kutegulwa kutali ku wuhamywe. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.’ ”

15 “Hambi nha mukutya nha vyono, u Muwaha Mulungu kacipela wawizi mu Babiloni.
16 Ninga muhulicize zono u Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga kuli zo mutemi mono yatemile, mu wutemi wono yali yatemile Dawudi, hamonga na kuli ze wanhu wa mwi'kaya lili. Nha cinji, i wandugu zenyu wono wasigala, wono si wasolelezwa hamonga nanye.
17 U Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, yakutya nhavi, ‘Lanje, ane nomuletelenyi wulugu, ne nzala, hamonga ne matamwa, nimunozenyi muwe nhe zinghuyu zimbi zono si zisumice hamba ku wulye kose.
18 Nane nomutyatililenyi no wulugu, ne nzala, hamonga ne matamwa. Ne makungugo gose ga mono'nze, golokwinjilwa no wizine wuwaha muno. Ne hanhu hose hono nowa niweyagazize, i wanhu wa mumo wolotinuswa no kogofywa muno muno ku soko ya zizo zono zolomwijelelenyi Ne wanhu wose wolomusekanjenyi, no kulitumamila litagwa lyenyu nha cinhu cilijitwe.
19 Na zizi zose zolomuzizenyi, vyono si mwasaka kuzihulika zinghani zangu, zono namulonjelajenyi kenji na kenji, mu nzila ye wetumami wangu i wawizi, nanye hamba kuzihulika yaye.
20 Holeka muhulice nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu, anye wose mono namulecelenyi musolwe ku wulawa ku Yelusalemi, mutegulwe ku Babiloni ku wuhamywe.’

21 “U Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, kuli zinghani za Ahabu mwana wa Kolaya, na Zedekiya mwana wa Maseya, wono wakumulonjelenyi zinghani zo wulambi mwi'tagwa lyakwe, yakutya nhavi, Nowatajila mu miwoko ya Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, nayo yawawulajile ha meso genyu.
22 Nawo i wanhu wono wasolwa nha wafungwa ku Yelusalemi, no kutegulwa ku Babiloni, hono wawe kusaka kumulijita munhu, wosuma kutya, ‘U Muwaha Mulungu yakutendele nha vyono yamutendela Zedekiya na Ahabu, wono u mutemi wa Babiloni, yawakasika mu moto wali nyumi!’
23 Na zizi zo zowagana na weneco, hambi nha wetuma wubi muno mu Isilayeli. Kanyhi wakasenyha ne wacekulu we wandugu zawo no kulonga zo wulambi, wakwitumamila litagwa lyangu. Ne zinghani zizi zikawa yiwifyo kuli Muwaha Mulungu. Nayo yazimanyile zose zono wakwituma, kanyhi mwene yo muhomelezi muli wene. Vyo yakutya, u Muwaha Mulungu.”

Baluwa ya Shemaya

24 U Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yakanonjela nghani,
25 ho nimulonjele Shemaya munya Nehelamu, mono yandika baluwa mwi 'tagwa lyakwe mweneco, kuli wanhu wose wa mu Yelusalemi, na kuli Zefaniya mukohani, u mwana wa Maseya, sunga na kuli wakohani wanji wose. Na mu baluwa yiyo, Shemaya yamwandicila Zefaniya yakutya nhavi.

26 “U Muwaha Mulungu yakunoza agwe Zefaniya wuwe mukohani, wuhalile i hanhu ha Yehoyada. Sunga nalu yo gwegwe mulongola muwaha wa mu Nyumba yo Mulungu. Holeka yikunojeye wuwalanjilile i wanhu wo wose, u munhu mono kowa yena likwale, no kwinoza nha muwizi, munhu yuyo wumwibate, wumuwope ne manyolo.
27 Hamba nha vivyo, nhawule lu si wazinoza zizi kuli Yelemiya munya Anatoti, mono yekalaga yakulonga nha muwizi kuli wanhu?
28 Holeka yuyo yimunojeye yemilwe muli zizo. Hambi nha yawalonjela wanhu ku Babiloni, nha vyono wokwikala wafunjigwe kuko vilo vinji. Holeka yiwanojeye wazenje zikaya, wekale, wahadice migunda, walye mikalo yayo.”

29 Nayo Zefaniya yakasomela baluwa yiyo,
30 nayo Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela ane Yelemiya yakatya,
31 vyono niwamanyize nghani yiyi ya Shemaya, wanhu wose i wafungwa, wono wali ku Babiloni, niwalonjele nitye nhavi, “U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya vyono,
32 ‘Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nomulipiciza Shemaya munya Nehelami hamonga no lulelo lwakwe. Kanyhi howa hasina hamba munhu monga wa muli wanhu wakwe mono kokwikala mumi ho yaziwone ziswanu, zono nowanozeza wanhu wangu. Hambi mwene nha kawasagahaza wanhu wangu ho waneme 'ne. Vyo yakutya, u Muwaha Mulungu.’ ”


Yelemiya 30

U wihuwizo wo Mulungu, kuli wanhu wakwe

1 U Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli
2 yakanonjela yakatya, “Yelemiya! Alu wuzandikaje mu citabu, zinghani zose zono nghulonjeye.
3 Hambi i vilo nha vyokuza vyono nolowaziza wanhu wangu Isilayeli na Yuda. Nolowagomola kanyhi ku yisi yono nawapela walezi wawo, nawo wayisole kanyhi, yiwe yisi yawo. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

4 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga za kuli wanhu wa Isilayeli na Yuda, yakutya nhavi,

5 “Nahulika lizi lye cililo co wogofye,

cililo co woga, hamba si cililo co mulamu yaye.

6 Alu, ta mwihwesaje! Mwene mbeka, u munhu we cilume,

kosuma kulela mwana hongo?

Alu nhawulenyi nghumuwonenyi anye we cilume wose,

mwibatilize zinda zenyu ne miwoko,

nho mucekulu mono yali mu nghunga yo lusawo?

Nhawulenyi, nha nghuwona wose musininghaye lo?

7 Ninga lizuwa lyo wogofye likuza,

hasina hamba lizuwa linji lyono lyolowa lihwanile ne lizuwa lilyo,

lizuwa lyo wugaye we wanhu wangu,

wo lulelo lwa Yakobo,

ninga hamba nhavivyo, wolopona du.”

8 U Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga yakutya nhavi, “Ne lizuwa lilyo kolo likuza, nololubena lubano lono luli mu zisingo zawo, niwawopole ne manyolo gawo. Na ku wulawa mwizuwa lilyo, nawo si wolowa kanyhi wawanda we wanhu wajenzi yaye.
9 Langa wolonyitumamila nene nili wene du, ane no Muwaha Mulungu wawo, hamonga no mwana wa Dawudi, mono nolomutemya yawe mutemi wawo.

10 “Nye nyo wanhu wangu, mulece kuwa no woga,

nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli, mulece kogofywa na cinji ye!

Hambi ane,

nha nomuponyenyi ku wulawa kuko ku yisi yo kutali,

ku yisi yiyo yono mufunjigwe mu jela.

Nanye molowuya du kono kwenyu,

mwikale no mulamu.

Kanyhi holowa hasina hamba munhu yunji wo wose,

mono kolomogofyenyi yaye.

11 Ane nolomuzizenyi, nimuponyenyi.

Nologongomeza i makungugo gose,

i hanhu hose hono namwiyagazizenyi.

Anye langa, si nolomongomezenyi yaye.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo, si nolomulecenyi du,

ndece kumulipicizenyi yaye,

langa nolomulipicizenyi kwe ling'ani du,

vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

12 U Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela

i wanhu wakwe yakutya nhavi,

“Alu mulanje ta,

ne zinghomele zenyu hamba si zikunoga yaye,

no wutamiswe wenyu hamba si wukulagulwa ng'o.

13 Alu lanje, hasina hamba munhu wo wose mono

yakumululenyi,

hasina hamba miti yo yose yono yokwila mu zinghomele zenyu.

Hasina hamba witumbilo wo kuponywa yaye.

14 Ne wendece wenyu wose,

wakondya kumusemwenyi,

hamba si womululenyi kanyhi yaye.

Nane nghamuhomenyi nha muli walugu,

no wulipicizwe wukawa wukali muno,

ku soko yo wubi wenyu vyono winji muno,

hambi i metumo genyu mabi,

nha gali gonjelezeka muno.

15 Holeka mulece kung'ung'ulika muno,

ku soko yo wutamiswe wenyu,

hambi nha si moponywa yaye.

Nane nghamulipicizenyi nhavivi,

ku soko yo winji wo wubi wenyu,

hambi i metumo genyu mabi,

nha gali gonjelezeka muno.

16 Ninga ku wulawilila lulu,

i wanhu wose wono wolomusolelenyi vinhu vyenyu,

ale nawo wolosolelwa vinhu vyawo nhavivyo.

Ne walugu wenyu wose,

wolosolwa wategulwe kutali nha wafungwa.

Ne wanhu wose wono wolomwiwonelenyi,

nawo wolokwiwonelwa,

ne wanhu wose wono wolomupumhenyi

nawo wolopumhwa nhavivyo.

17 Hambi nha nolomugomolelenyi kanyhi u wuswanu wenyu,

kanyhi nolomuponyenyi ne zinghomele zenyu,

hamba i walugu wenyu wawe kutya,

‘Alu mulanje,

Sayuni yakondya kulekwa hasina

hamba yunji kanyhi mono yakuyitondowoza yaye.’

Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

18 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Nowagomola wanhu wangu ku yisi yawo,

ne zimhuga zawo zose, nowawonela lusungu.

Nayo Yelusalemi yolozengwa kanyhi,

ne likaya lyayo lye likulu,

nalyo lyolozengwa kanyhi mu matongo galyo.

19 Ne wanhu wono wowa wekaye mumo,

wolokwimba zinyimbo zo lukumyo,

wolowa wazozize so nyemo mbaha.

Nane nolowamotya, waze welele wawe wenji muno,

no wumoteswe wangu wolowaletela lilumbo liwaha.

20 Nolowagomolela i vilungo vye likungugo lyawo,

nha vyono wali isonje, no kuwasikamya kanyhi.

Nolowalipiciza wanhu wose wono wawewonelaga.

21 No mutemi wawo,

kololawilila mumo du mu nghungugo yawo.

Na mono kolowatema, kolowa wa mu mhuga yawo weneco.

Nane kolo nghumucema, nayo kolonyiziza.

Hambi nha sikolokwijelela nyanyizize,

lono nene nghali sinamucema yaye.

22 Nawo wolowa wanhu wangu nane nolowa Mulungu wawo.

Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

23 Ne likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

mbeka wo mukukunzo we civulavumbi ce vilungo,

cono cowaziza ko wukali,

ciwagwile mu mitwe yawo i wanhu wabi.

24 Nalyo likuwo lilyo likali lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

hamba si lyolowuka mbela yaye,

sunga langa kolo lyakondya kwituma zose no kufisiliza wuhile wakwe.

Ne wanhu wakwe,

wolozitanga zizi, mu vilo vye ndumo.


Yelemiya 31

Wuwuye we wanhu wa Isilayeli ku yisi yawo

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“I vilo vyolokuza,

vyono ane nolowa Mulungu we zimhuga zose za Isilayeli,

nawo wolowa wanhu wangu.

2 Nghawalajila zo lusungu lwangu mu nyika,

i wanhu wono wapona mwi'fwa.

Lono i wanhu wa Isilayeli wali wena nyhilu yo kuceha,

3 ane nghelajila kuli wene ku wulawilila cidaha.

Nye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli,

ane nha nimwendilenyi du nhondo zose.

Na ku soko yiyo,

holeka njendeleye du kumulajilenyi zo lusungu lwangu kuli'nye.

4 Nane nomuzenjenyi 'nye lwe kejete kamonga du.

Na kanyhi nanye mo Isilayeli mo waswanu!

Lulo, molosola kamonga ne vigoma vyenyu muwe kuvina ko wusangalale.

5 Na kanyhi moloyihadika sunga ne mizabibu yenyu mu matunda ga Samaliya.

Na wose wa muli nyenye wono woloyihadika, wolowa kulya mikalo yayo.

6 Na heya mbeka, i vilo vikuza,

vyono i walindilizi,

wolowa wakwicemeleza mu vitunda vya Efulayimu wakutya,

‘Alenyi cibite ku Sayuni,

ko Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu.’ ”

7 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Alenyi nye nyo zinghungugo zimbaha,

alu mwimbe ko wusangalale ku soko ya Isilayeli.

Mwimbe u lwimbo lwenyu lo lukumyo, muwe kutya,

‘U Muwaha Mulungu kawaponya i wanhu wakwe,

kawakomboza na wose wono wali wasigaye.’

8 Nane nowakongholeza ku wulawa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka,

nowakonghola hamonga ku wulawa mu zimhelo zose ze yinze.

Nawo wolokuzana ne wabofu na we vilema,

ne wacekulu wono wena mida,

hamonga sunga na wose wono wali mu nghunga yo lusawo.

Nawo wolowuya kono kwawo nghungugo mbaha muno.

9 Nawo i wanhu wangu, wolowa kulila, akuno wakulomba,

mu cipango cono ane nolowa niwalongoze.

Nane nowatonga sunga ko ku vikolongo vye malenga,

nolowakoza mu nzila yono yitelemeye,

ho walece kwikobala yaye.
