Hambi ane, nha nihwanile sogo yo munhu kuli Isilayeli,

nayo Efulayimu, nha yo mwanangu mulongozi kulelwa.”

10 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Nye nyo we zinghungugo, muhulicizaje ye,

muziganule zinghani zangu,

sunga ko ku vizingwa vyo kutali.

Hambi 'ne, nha naweyagaza wanhu wangu,

ninga luno nowakongholela wose hamonga.

Niwakamise nha vyono mudimi kakamisaga litewa lyakwe.

11 Ane nawakomboza wanhu wa Isilayeli,

no kuwaleka welecele,

kanyhi nawaponya mu miwoko ye likungugo lyono lili vilungo muno.

12 Holeka nawo wonyiziza,

wanyimbile so nyemo mwi'tunda lya Sayuni,

kanyhi wendezwe ne vipegwa vyangu,

i vipegwa vyo wusaje,

ne divayi, hamonga ne mafuta,

ne vipegwa vye zingholo ne zing'ombe.

Na baho, wolowa nha busitani yono mwajilwe malenga.

Nawo wolowa wakwizize vyose vyono wakuvisaka.

13 Hanyuma i wahinza wawa,

wolokwidawala ko wusangalale.

Nawo i wanhu wadodo ne wakombi,

wose wolowa na wusangalale wuwaha muno.

Nolowasindamika, no kucinoza cililo cawo yiwe nyemo.

No wukalale wawo wuwe wusangalale.

14 Nane nolowalisa ndigwa swanu i wakohani,

no kuwapela wanhu wangu,

i visakwa vyawo vyose vyono wakuhila, sunga wawe waceye.

Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

U lusungu lo Mulungu kuli Isilayeli

15 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Lizi lihulikwa ku Lama,

lizi lye cililo co wusungu wuwaha muno,

Laheli yakuwalilila wanagwe,

si yakusaka hamba no kutuzwa yaye.

Hambi wose nha wali kusina.

16 Ninga 'gwe Laheli, alu wulece kujendelela kulila,

pate mahozi mu meso gako.

Hambi u mulimo wono witumile kuli wanago,

nha wotuga cihumbilizwa,

hambi wene nha wowuya, ku wulawa ku yisi ye walugu.

17 Kanyhi, kowuli witumbilo ku vilo vyako vyo mudumo,

ne wanago wolowuya ku yisi yawo weneco.

Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

18 “Kanyhi hulika i wanhu wa Isilayeli wakulonga

ko wusungu wuwaha wakutya,

‘Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ase, cali cihwanile nhe ndama,

yono si mbope no lufungo,

ninga wacifunda ciwe ne cihuliko.

Holeka luno, wucigomole kuli'gwe,

nase nha ciwikanize cikuwuyile gwegwe du,

go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu wetu.

19 Heya mbeka, cali cakondya kukuleka,

ninga aluno, cikusaka cikuwuyile mbela,

hambi nha wakondya kucilipiciza,

nase cinamizye i mitwe yetu ko wusungu wuwaha.

Kanyhi cikuwona soni,

no kukolwa cevu ciwaha ku soko yo wubi wetu,

wono cetuma mu wuzelelo wetu.’

20 “Gwe Isilayeli, nha yo gwegwe go mwanangu,

mono nghwendile muno muno.

Na mu mapango go gose,

lono nghulitambula litagwa lyako,

nghuwa kukwihwesa ko wendo wuwaha muno.

Ne nhumbula yangu yikugazika so gwegwe.

Holeka nane nokuwonela lusungu.

21 Holeka anye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli,

mwiwicilaje citanjilo ce nzila,

cimutonjenyi kanyhi mu nzila yono mwayileka.

Alu wuyenyi kwenyu,

mubite ku vikaya vyono mwali mwavileka.

22 Alo ho mokangamyanga sunga nili lo,

anye nyo wanhu wo wihuwilo wucefu?

Hambi 'ne, nha nawumba nghani yinji mhya mono'nze,

yono yisimajiye, nha vyono u mucekulu yo komulondola u mulume.”

U wumoteswe wono wowatuga i wanhu wo Mulungu mu vilo vyono vikuza

23 U Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, u Mulungu we Waisilayeli yakutya nhavi, “Kolo nghuwagomola i wanhu ku yisi yawo weneco, wolokwitumamila nghani yiyi mu yisi ya Yuda na mu vikaya vyayo, wawe kutya,

‘U Muwaha Mulungu yalilugutize litunda lyono lyelile lya Yelusalemi,

i hanhu hono helile hono mwene yekaye.’

24 “Ne wanhu nawo, wolokwikala mu yisi ya Yuda, mu vikaya vyayo vyose, nawo wolowa kulima, hamonga no kudima mitugo zawo mumo.
25 Nane nolowasikamya wono wasoceye, no kuwegutya ndigwa, wose wono wali nzala.
26 Na mu mapango gago, i wanhu wolowa kutya, ‘Nye wayangu, ane ngona zinhongo muno muno, ninuka no muwili wangu wusangalaye.’

27 “Ne vilo vikuza, lono noloyimemya yisi yose ya Yuda na ya Isilayeli, wanhu wenji, hamonga ne mitugo minji.
28 Kanyhi yolowa nha vyono, mu nghango yono nali nanjiliye muno wene ho wakulwe, wagwiswe, wapondangwe, wongomezwe, hamonga no kutendelwa vibi, ale, yo nghango yiyo kanyhi, nolanjilila muno sunga wahadikwe, wazengwe.
29 Na kolo vilo vivyo vikuza, wanhu si wolojendelela kulonga kanyhi wawe kutya,

‘I wasogo zo munhu lono walizaga zabibu zi nghali,

i meno ge wana gakafwa yitenene.’

30 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, u munhu wo wose mono kololya zabibu zi nghali, i meno gakwe golofwa yitenene. No munhu wo wose, kolofwa ku soko yo wubi wakwe mweneco.”

31 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “I vilo vikuza, lono nolonozeza cilagane cipya ne wanhu wa Isilayeli, hamonga ne wanhu wa Yuda.
32 Ne cilagane cico, si colowa cihwanile ne cilagane ce katali yaye, cono nacinoza ne walezi wawo, lono nawakamisa, nghawawusa mu yisi ya Misili. Hamba niwe nali nili mulume wawo, ninga wene si wacikamisa yaye cilagane cico.
33 Ninga aluno, i cilagane cono nocinoza ne wanhu wa Isilayeli, cocino, Ane nolowutajila wulajilize wangu muli wene, niwandice mu zinhumbula zawo mugati. Nane nolowa Mulungu wawo, nawo wolowa wanhu wangu.
34 Na mu vilo vivyo, holowa hasina kanyhi yunji mono kolowa kumwijiza muyagwe yunji yakumutya yamumanye Muwaha Mulungu, hambi i wanhu wose nha wolowa wakondya kumanya 'ne, ku wulawilila i wadodo sunga ne wanhu mututu wose. Nane nolowanenela u wubi wawo wose. Kanyhi hamba si nilagakumbucilila lukulu i mabi gawo gose gono wetuma. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

35 U Muwaha Mulungu, kawapelaga wanhu lizuwa ho liwamulicile wuzelu mu misi, nawo mwezi hamonga ne zinyelezi, zimulice mu mapango ge cilo. Yo mweneco mono kalitunhumyaga lilenga liwa kubuma, ne litagwa lyakwe kacemwaga u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga.

36 Holeka yakutya nha vyono, i malajilizo gaga hono gawuce, ne likungugo lya Isilayeli nalyo lyoduma.
37 Na hono yijele mwizuwa linji, wulanga wusumice ku wijele, ne misinji ye yinze hono yisumice ku wulondolwa hasi no kuwonwa, nayo Mulungu kowalema i wanhu wa Isilayeli ku soko ya zizo zose zono wetuma. Vyo yakutya, u Muwaha Mulungu.

38 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “I vilo vyolokuza, lono likaya lyangu lya Yelusalemi, lyolozengwa kanyhi, ku wulawilila ku munala wa Hananeli ku lubavu lwa kumwezi, sunga ko ku mulango wa ku nhuzi.
39 No mupaka wayo, wolojendelela ku wulawa kuko ku lubavu lwa kumwezi, sunga ko kwi'tunda lya Galebu, wupilime sunga ko ku Gowa.
40 Ne lilolo lyose lyono wazikaga mivimba, no kugamwaga mavu, ne migunda yose, sunga ko ku cikolongo ca Kidiloni, sunga ko ku nhuzi yo mulango we zifwalasi, ku lubavu lwa kucilima. Mumo mose, mosiwanulwa ku soko yangu. Ne likaya, si lyolokulwa kanyhi hamba kuhodangwa yaye.”


Yelemiya 32

Yelemiya yakuwugula mugunda

1 U Muwaha Mulungu, yalongana nane kanyhi mu mwaka we likumi, wo wutemi wa Zedekiya u mutemi wa Yuda, wono wali wo mwaka we likumi na munana wo wutemi wa Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni.
2 Na mu mapango gago, gali go mapango gono i wasilikale wo mutemi wa Babiloni, wayihomaga Yelusalemi. Nane nali ngalamiye kucanyha, mu wulawa mwi'kumbo lye nyumba yo wutemi, mono nali ndindiywe.
3 Hambi u mutemi Zedekiya, nha yali yandindila mumo, vyono 'ne nghuganula nghutya, u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nomuleka mutemi wa Babiloni, yaze yalisole likaya lili,
4 nayo u mutemi Zedekiya, si kololekwa yaye. Langa nayo kolotekwa nhavivyo, kolosolwa no mutemi wa Babiloni. Wolokwiwona nayo cagalanze, no kulongana nayo.
5 Nayo mutemi Zedekiya, kolosolwa yategulwe ku Babiloni, nayo kolowa kuko du sunga kono nene nilamukumbucilila. Hamba yaze yatye yehome ne Wababiloni, ninga hamba si kolowasuma yaye. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

6 Kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
7 Hanameli u mwana wo sogwe mudodo wa Shalumu, konyiziza yanyitye vyono, niwugule mugunda wakwe, wono wuli ku Anatoti mu yisi ya Benyamini. Hambi 'ne nha yo nene du mono nili nduguye we behi, holeka yo nene du mono nina ling'ani yo kuwugula mugunda wuwo wuwe wangu.
8 Na lulolulo du baho, u Muwaha Mulungu yali yeza kukondya kulongana nane, nayo Hanameli yakawa kunyiziza mumo mwi'kumbo lye nyumba yo wutemi, nha vyono yanonjela Muwaha Mulungu, yakawa kunonjela nha vyono mwene yakusaka niwugule mugunda wakwe. Nane baho nghamanya nha vyono, mbeka nghani yiyi yilawa kuli Muwaha Mulungu.

9 Nane nghawa kuwugula mugunda wuwo kuli Hanameli u muhizi wangu. Nghawa kumujelela zipesa ze cifo cayo, zikafika fweza yikumi na mupunghati.
10 Nghandika litagwa lyangu mu kalatasi yo wuhomelezi wo wuguze, ha mwande ye wahomelezi, nghamupela ne zipesa ze cifo cayo.
11 Nghayisola kalatasi yiyilya yo wugule, yono yali yina ne citanjilo, nha vyono winha u wulajilize no moto we 'si, hamonga ne lisiti yo kugulila.
12 Nghamupela Baluku u mwana wa Neliya mwana wa Maseya ha meso ga Hanameli u muhizi wangu, na hali wahomelezi wawalya wono wandika matagwa gawo nha wuhomelezi wo wuguzi, ha meso ge Wayahudi wose wono wali wacehile baho he 'kumbo.
13 Nghamulajiliza Baluku hali wene, nghatya,
14 “U Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, kalajiliza nha vyono, yo gwegwe mono wunojeye, wusole zikalatasi zizi zo wuhomelezi zose vyono nyejete, kalatasi yo wugule, yono yina ne citanjilo, hamonga na ayi yono si ndinde yaye, wuzitajile mu ciya ce lilongo, ho zikale mumo vilo vinji.
15 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga u Mulungu wa Isilayeli nha yakutya vyono, zikaya, ne migunda, hamonga ne migunda ye mizabibu, nha vyologulwa kanyhi mono yisi yiyi.”

U wulombe wa Yelemiya

16 Nane, nali neza kukondya kumupela Baluku, zikalatasi zizo, hodu, nghawa kumulomba Muwaha Mulungu nghatya,
17 “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, hambi nha yo gwegwe du mono wawumba wulanga ne yinze, kwe vilungo vyako na ko wusume wako. Hambi nha hasina nghani yinji yo yose nghamu hali 'gwe.
18 Hambi nha yo gwegwe, mono wukuwawonela lusungu wanhu, maelufu na maelufu, kanyhi yo gwegwe mono kuwalipicizaga i wanhu, ku soko yo wubi we walezi wawo. Hambi nha yo gwegwe du Muwaha, mono wuli Mulungu mono wulawiliciye. Kanyhi yo gwegwe mono wuli Mulungu, u Mudaha vyose u Mulungu we mihanga.
19 Hambi nha yo gwegwe du mono wina mahweso ge mahala, kanyhi kwitumaga metumo go wizine. Mono kuziwonaga calinze i zinghani zose, zono i wanhu wetumaga. Kanyhi kuwapelaga i wanhu, munhu mu nghango ye metumo gakwe.
20 Hambi i katali, nha wetuma metumo mawaha go wizine ku yisi ya Misili, ninga sunga nalu, vyo wukali wujendeleye kugetuma du. Wukugetuma mu Isilayeli, sunga na mu makungugo ganji, holeka sunga nalu agwe wumanyice hanhu hose.
21 Na ku soko ye mapoto gako, hamonga ne metumo gako go wizine, gono gawogofya i walugu wetu, wetumamila vilungo vyako, wukawawusa i wanhu wako Isilayeli mu yisi ya Misili.
22 Wukawapela yisi yiyi, yono yina muhangha wono wina moso mwinji, nha vyono gwegwe wawehuwiza i walezi wawo.
23 Ninga nawo wali weza kuza mono yisi yiyi, no kuyisola yiwe yawo, hamba si wasaka no kugebata ne malajilizo gako yaye, hamba kwikala mu mikalile ye mejizo gako ng'o. Hamba si wasuma kwituma nha vyono wali wuwalajilize wetume. Holeka nagwe luno wawaletela magayo gaga gose.

24 “Sunga nalu, wulanje vyono Wababiloni wakondya kusita nghambi zawo, wapilime likaya, ho walihome no kulisola. Nawo wulugu ne nzala, hamonga ne matamwa, golinoza likaya lisolwe nawo. Kanyhi kosuma kuwona nha vyono, zose zono walonga, alu zakondya kuficilicila nha vyono walonga gwegwe.
25 Ninga hamonga na zizo, yo gwegwe go Muwaha Mulungu, mono wanonjela nha vyono, yinojeye niwugule mugunda wa Hanameli ha meso ge wahomelezi, hamba yiwe luno, likaya lyeza kusolwa ne Wababiloni.”

26 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
27 “Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu we wanhu wose. Holeka hasina nghani nghamu yono yonemela 'ne.
28 Nalu wulanje! Nomupela Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni hamonga ne wasilikale wakwe likaya lili, walisole,
29 walipembele moto. Wolikongoga hamonga ne zikaya zose, zono wanhu wakuzitumamila, so wufusa wuvumba kuli Baali, mu matembe mucanyha, hamonga no kuyilavizya milungu yinji vilavywa vye divayi.
30 Ninga wumanye nha vyono, ku wulawilila katali na katali, i wanhu wa Isilayeli ne wanhu wa Yuda, si wanyendezaga 'ne hamba hadodo, kanyhi wakanghoza muno likuwo, ku soko ye metumo gawo mabi.
31 Ne wanhu we likaya lili, wekalaga wakunghoza likuwo ku wulawilila mwizuwa lyono lyazenjelwa. Holeka nane, nakondya kulamula nha vyono ciba nilyongomeze,
32 ku soko ye metumo mabi gose gono wetumaga i wanhu wa Isilayeli na Yuda, hamonga ne watemi wawo, ne walongola wawo, ne wakohani wawo, hamonga ne wawizi wawo.
33 Hambi nha wakondya kundeka 'ne, hamba niwe neyoha muno kuwejiza, ninga wene hamba si wahuliciza 'ne yaye, hamba kwiyijiza kose.
34 Ninga wene, ho wakacila kuwika vihwani vyawo vye cevu mu Nyumba yangu, yono yazengwa ku soko yo wuhembi wangu du. Na mu nzila yiyo, wakondya kuyavufya.
35 Kanyhi wazenga vikumbicilo vya Baali, mwi'lolo lyo mwana wa Hinomu, no kuwalavya mumo makumbiko, i wana wawo we cilume na we cicekulu, kuli mulungu Moleki. Kumbe nane, si nawakombola wetume zizo. Na kanyhi hamba si nali nihwesize nha vyono wosuma kwituma nghani zono zinha zizo, no kuwanoza wanhu wa Yuda wetume wubi.”

Wihuwizo wo witumbilo

36 U Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yakatya, “Gwe Yelemiya, i wanhu wakulonga nha vyono wulugu, ne nzala hamonga ne matamwa, gogoleka likaya lili lisolwe no mutemi wa Babiloni. Ninga wuhulicize zono nghulonga,
37 Nowakongholeza wanhu wose, ku wulawa mu ma'si gose, kono naweyagaziza mwi'kuwo lyangu, niwagomole kanyhi hanhu baha, niwalece wekale bahano wapolile.
38 Na wowa wanhu wangu, nane nowa Mulungu wawo.
39 Nane nowapela wuhile wumonga, ho wanyogope nene vilo vyose, so ziswanu zawo, hamonga ne ziswanu ze wana wawo.
40 Nolonoza nawo cilagane cipya. Hamba si nololecela lukulu kuwanozeza ziswanu. Nolowanoza wawe ne cogopo kuli'ne kwe nhumbula yawo yose, ho waze walece kanyhi kundeka.
41 Nane nolotuga nyemo ku soko yawo, nolowasikamya mono yisi yiyi, kanyhi nolowatendela ziswanu.

42 “Alu, nha vyono nakondya kuwaletela magayo gaga i wanhu wawa, nha vivyo vyo nowatendela ziswanu zose nha vyono nehuwiza.
43 Wanhu wakutya vyono, yisi yiyi yolowa nyika, hawe hanhu hono wanhu hamba nimu, si vyolosuma kwikala mumo, holeka yolekwa yisolwe ne Wababiloni. Ninga wumanye, nha vyono, migunda yologulwa kanyhi mono yisi yiyi.
44 Ne wanhu woloyigula, ne zikalatasi zo wuhomelezi zo wugule wazo, zolotajilwa vitanjilo no kwandikwa ne matagwa, hamonga no wuhomelezi. Na zizi, zolokwitumwa mu yisi ya Benyamini, na mu vitumbi vyose vyono vipilime Yelusalemi, na mu vikaya vya Yuda, sunga na mu vikaya vyose vya mu'si ye matunda, na mu mwalambalo we matunda, sunga na ku lubavu lwa kutakama lwa Yuda. Nolowagomola wanhu ku yisi yawo. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 33

U wihuwizo wunji kanyhi wo witumbilo

1 Nane lono nali nghali mumo mu jela, mwi'kumbo lye Nyumba yo Mulungu, i nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu yikanyiziza kanyhi.
2 U Muwaha Mulungu mono yawumba yinze, no kuyikamya, u Muwaha Mulungu lye 'tagwa lyakwe, yakanonjela yakatya,
3 “Wunyheme 'ne, nane nokupitula, nane nokulajila zinghani zimbaha, zono si wuzimanyile yaye.
4 Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli nghutya vyono, i zikaya za mu Yelusalemi, hamonga ne likaya lyo wutemi lya Yuda, vyose vyolomwagulwangwa, ciwe co citanjilo cazo co wupilimwe no wuhomwe wazo.
5 Wanji wolokwihoma ne Wababiloni, wono wolozimevya zinyumba ne mivimba ye wanhu, wono nolowawulajila nene mwi'kuwo lyangu. Nane nakondya kulileka likaya lili, ku soko ye makani mabi, gono wakwituma wanhu wayo.

6 “Ninga hamba nhavivyo, nololiponya likaya lili, hamonga ne wanhu wayo, no kuwaleka wekale ne miwili miswanu. Nane nolowalajila zo mulamu, na ze mikalile yono yipolile.
7 Kanyhi nolowamotya muno Yuda na Isilayeli. Kanyhi nolowazenga wawe nha vyono wali ha lulongozi.
8 Nolowakwema no wubi wawo wose wono wasutizya. Kanyhi nolowanenela u wubi wawo wose, hamonga no wubezi wawo.
9 Naho ha Yelusalemi, holowa wo mwamulizo wo wusangalale, ne cogopo, hamonga no lukumyo ku soko yangu. Ne makungugo gose ga mono'nze, golokwinjilwa no woga no kucicima, kolo gakuzihulika ziswanu zose zono nghuwatendela i wanhu wa Yelusalemi, hamonga no wumoteswe wono nghuliletela likaya.”

10 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “I wanhu wakutya nha vyono, i hanhu baha heza nhe nyika, hono hasina cinhu cono colokwikala cumi mumo, wanhu hamba malimu. Na zizi, wakulonga mbeka, hambi mbeka vikaya vya Yuda hamonga ne zinzila za mu Yelusalemi, nha zisina mbeka wanhu hamba malimu wono wekaye mumo. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, kolosuma kanyhi kuhulika mumo,
11 mazi ge nyemo, hamonga no wusangalale. Hamonga ne mazi nyemo ge zindigwa ze halusi. Kanyhi kolowahulika wanhu wakwimba lono wakulavya vilavywa vyo wulumbe mu Nyumba yangu. Wolowa kwimba wakutya nhavi,

‘Mulumbenyi Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha vyose,

hambi mwene nha muswanu

no lusungu lwakwe lwa cibitilila.’

“Nane noloyimotya yisi yiyi, yiwe nha vyono yali sonje. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

12 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Na mu yisi yiyi, yono yihwanile nyika, hono ne wanhu hamba malimu si wekaye mumo yaye, ninga hanyuma, holowa kanyhi hanhu ha wudimilo, hono i wanhu wolowa kudimila kanyhi zingholo zawo.
13 Na mumo mu vikaya vya mu matunda, na mu mwalambalo we matunda, na mu yisi ya Yuda, ku lubavu lwa kutakama, na mu yisi yose ya Benyamini, na mu vitumbi vyose vyono vipilime Yelusalemi, sunga na mu vikaya vyose vya Yuda, mumo mose, molowa kanyhi hanhu hono wanhu wolowa kuziwalila zingholo zawo. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

14 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “I vilo vyolokuza vyono nolowufisiliziza wihuwizo wangu wono nawehuwiziza i wanhu wa Isilayeli na Yuda.
15 Na mu vilo vivyo, nolomuhagula mutemi we ling'ani, mu wulawa mu lulelo lwa Dawudi. No mutemi yuyo, kolo kwituma ze ling'ani na zo wiling'ano mu yisi yose.
16 Ne wanhu wose wa Yuda sunga na wa Yelusalemi, woloponywa no kwikala wapolile. Ne likaya lyolocemwa, ‘U Muwaha Mulungu yo ling'ani yetu.’
17 Nane no Muwaha Mulungu nghwihuwiza nha vyono, wa mu lulelo lwa Dawudi, yo kolojendelela vilo vyose kuwa mutemi wa Isilayeli,
18 na kanyhi wo lulelo lwa wo mulango wa Lawi, wawo du wo wolojendelela vilo vyose kuwa wo wakohani wono wonyitumila, hamonga no kundavizya vilavywa vinyanywe, ne vilavywa vyo wusaje, hamonga ne makumbiko.”

19 Kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
20 “Ane nha vyono nanoza cilagane cangu ce misi na ce cilo, ho viwe kwilecela mu mapango gayo, ne cilagane cico hamba si cilabeneka yaye.
21 Ale yo nhavivyo kanyhi, nakondya kunoza cilagane no mwitumami wangu Dawudi, nha vyono kolowa vilo vyose yena mwana mono kowa mutemi. Na kanyhi nghanoza cilagane na kuli wakohani wa mu lulelo lwa wo mulango wa Lawi, nha vyono wo weneco du, wo wojendelela kunyitumamila. Ne vilagane vivyo vyose hamba si vilabeneka yaye.
22 Kanyhi nolowonjeleza liwalo lya wo lulelo lwa Dawudi u mwitumami wangu, wawe wenji, hamonga ne liwalo lye wakohani wa mu lulelo lwa wo mulango wa Lawi, wawe wenji muno, sunga kuwe kukamu na ku wuwalwe, nha vyono zinha zinyelezi ku wulanga, hamba musawawa wa mwi'lenga.”

23 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
24 “Wule, wakondya kuwawona i wanhu wono wakutya, nha vyono ane nakondya kuwaleka i wanhu wa Isilayeli na Yuda, zimhuga zizi zinyejete zono nakondya kuzihagula? Na mu nzila yiyo, wakuwabedegala wanhu wangu, no kuwehweseza vyono si yikungugo kanyhi yaye.
25 Ninga mumanye nha vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, yo nene mono nanoza cilagane cangu ce misi na ce cilo, kanyhi nghatemya no wulajilize wono wibate wulanga ne yinze.
26 Holeka vyono mbeka netuma zizi, ale, nane nocikamisa cilagane cangu, cono nacinoza no lulelo lwa Yakobo no mwitumami wangu Dawudi. Kanyhi nolomuhagula yumonga wa mu lulelo lwa Dawudi, yateme mu lulelo lwa Abulahamu, na Isaka na Yakobo. Nane nolowa no lusungu kuli wanhu wangu, kanyhi nolowamotya muno.”


Yelemiya 34

Wusugulwe wa Zedekiya

1 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela kanyhi, mu mapango lono Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, ne wasilikale wakwe wose, no wutemi wose wa mono'nze, lono wali wuli mu vilungo vyakwe, hamonga ne makungugo gose, wehomaga na Yelusalemi ne vikaya vyayo vyose vye behi behi.
2 U Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yanonjela mbite kuli Zedekiya u mutemi wa Yuda, nghamulonjele nitye nhavi, “Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nomupela mutemi wa Babiloni likaya lili, nayo kolikongoga no moto.
3 Nagwe hamba si kololekwa yaye, langa kolotekwa no kusolwa no mwene. Nagwe kolokwiwona nayo cagalanze no kulongana nayo. Hanyuma wuwe kutegulwa ku Babiloni.
4 Alu Zedekiya, wuhulicize muno kuli zono nghukulonjela. Agwe si kolowulajilwa ku wulugu yaye,
5 langa kolofwa wupolile du. Kanyhi wanhu wolokufusiza wuvumba ha wuzikwe wako, nha vyono wanhu wawafusizaga wuvumba lono wawazikaga i walezi wako, wono wakulongolela kutema. Nawo wolokulilila akuno wakutya, ‘Woyi nye! Mutemi wetu yafwiza!’ Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

6 Ale du, nane nghawa kumubitila mutemi Zedekiya, kuko ku Yelusalemi,
7 mu mapango lono wasilikale wo mutemi wa Babiloni walihomaga likaya. Ne wasilikale wazihomaga Lakishi na Azeka, i makaya gono gali gakali gasigaye mu yisi ya Yuda.

U wutendelwe vibi we wawanda

8 U mutemi Zedekiya, hamonga ne wanhu wa Yelusalemi, wali wakondya kunoza wilumbanize, nha vyono,
9 wowaleka wawe welecele, i wawanda wawo we Cihebelaniya, i walume ne wacekulu. Ho halece kuwa na munhu wo wose mono kowa yena muwanda we Ciisilayeli.
10 Ne wanhu wose hamonga ne walongola wawo, wakelumbaniza vyono wawalece wawe welecele i wawanda wawo we Ciisilayeli, walecele lukulu kuwanoza kanyhi kuwa wawanda. Na mbeka wose wakawalecela wawe welecele.
11 Ninga hanyuma yayo, wakawupitula wuhile wawo wuwo wuswanu. Wakawasola wakawagomoleza kanyhi mu wuwanda kwa vilungo.

12 Hanyuma u Muwaha Mulungu,
13 u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yakanonjela vyono, mbite nghawalonjele wanhu nitye nhavi, “Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nanoza cilagane ne walezi wenyu, lono nawawusiza mu yisi ya Misili, no kuwakomboza mu wuwanda. Nghawalonjela nha vyono,
14 mu ndumilizo yo mwaka wo mupunghati, yimunojeye munhu wo wose yamulece yawe welecele u muwanda wo wose we Cihebelaniya, mono yakondya kumwitumamila myaka mutandatu. Ninga i walezi wenyu si wazicelelela yaye, hamba kuzihulika zono nawalonjela ng'o.
15 Nanye mu vilo vicefu vyono vipulute, mwali mwakondya kupituka mu wuhile wenyu no kwituma zono zikunyendeza 'ne. Nanye wose mukelumbaniza nha vyono mowaleka wawe welecele, i wandugu zenyu Waisilayeli. Nanye mwacinozeza cilagane cici hali 'ne, mu Nyumba yangu yono nghuwa kuhembelwa.
16 Ninga mukawupitula kanyhi wuhile wenyu, si mukamhela cogopo 'ne. Ne wawanda wose wono mwali mwawaleka wawe welecele, wabite kono wali wendile, alu mwawagomoleza kwe vilungo kanyhi mu wuwanda.
17 Holeka nane, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghuwona nha vyono, anye mwawula cogopo kuli 'ne. Hambi nha si muwakomboye wawe welecele i wandugu zenyu Waisilayeli. Ale, heya, viswanu. Nane nomupelenyi nanye welecele, u welecele wo kufwila ku wulugu, na mu matamwa, na mu nzala. Nane nowanoza makungugo gose, wogofywe muno so zono nomutendelenyi 'nye.
18 Nawo i wetumami wa mu yisi ya Yuda na Yelusalemi, hamonga ne wetumami we likulu, ne wakohani, hamonga ne walongola, wose wanoza nane cilagane mu mapango gono wayidumulila ndama vipiligwa vyejete, wakakola hagati ye vipiligwa vivyo.
19 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, wacibena cilagane cangu hamba kucifisiliza yaye. Holeka nane nowatendela wanhu wawa nha vyono wene wayitendela yiyo ndama.
20 Ane nowatajila mu miwoko ye walugu wawo, wono wakuwalondola wawawulaje. Ne mivimba yawo yololigwa ne zindeje, ne malimu ga mu mbago.
21 Sunga no mutemi Zedekiya wa Yuda, hamonga ne wetumami wakwe wose, nowatajila mu miwoko ye wanhu wono wakuwalondola wawawulaje. Nolowatajila mu miwoko ye wasilikale we Cibabiloni, wono wali wakondya kuleka kumuhomenyi.
22 Nowalajiliza nawo wokuza kanyhi mwi'kaya lili. Wololihoma, walisole, no kulikongoga. Nane noganoza makaya ga Yuda, gawe nhe nyika, hawe hanhu hono hamba wanhu wo wose si wosuma kwikala mumo. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 35

Yelemiya ne Walekabi

1 Mu vilo vyono Yehoyakimu u mwana wa Yosiya, yali yo mutemi wa Yuda, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
2 “Ale, wubite ku wanhu wo mulango we Walekabi, wukalonje nawo. Hanyuma wuwalete, wuwenjize mugati ye cinyumba cimonga ca mu Nyumba yangu, hanyuma wuwapele divayi wang'we.”
3 Lulo nghamusola Yazaniya u mwana wa Yelemiya, mwana Habaziniya, hamonga ne wanduguze, ne wanagwe wose, ne nyumba yose ye Walekabi,
4 nghawa kuwaleta ku Nyumba yo Mulungu. Nghawa kuwenjiza mu cinyumba ce wana wa Hanani, mwana wa Igidaliya, u munhu wo Mulungu. Ne cinyumba cico, cali cili mucanyha ye cinyumba ca Maseya u mwana wa Shalumu, u mwitumami mulongola wa mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, behi ne vinyumba vye wetumami wanji.
5 Ale, nghawa kuwawicila vikombe ha mwande yawo wene Walekabi, hamonga ne zibakuli zimemile divayi, nghawa kuwatya, “Ale mung'we divayi yiyi.”

6 Ninga wene wakamhitula wakatya, “Ase si cing'wezaga divayi yaye. Hambi u sogo yetu Yonadabu u mwana wa Lekabi, nha yacilonjela yakatya vyono, cilecele lukulu kung'wa divayi, sese sunga ne wana wetu cibitilila.
7 Kanyhi yacilonjela nha vyono, cilecele lukulu kuzenga kaya, hamba kulima migunda, hamba cilece kuhadika zabibu hamba kuzigula yaye. Kanyhi yacilajiliza vyono, ase cinojeye ciwe kwikala mu mahema du vilo vyose, ho cisume kwikala mono yisi yiyi nha cili wajenzi du.
8 Holeka nase cigebatiye lukulu i mejizo gono yacijiza Yonadabu. Na sese co weneco si cing'wezaga yaye divayi, hamba wagolece zetu, ne wana wetu we cilume na we cicekulu.
9 Holeka si cizengaga kaya zo kwikala yaye, langa cikalaga mu mahema du. Hamba cisina migunda ya zabibu, hamba migunda yo kulima no kuhadika wuhemba.
10 Langa cigakamisize lukulu i malajilizo gono u sogo yetu Yonadabu yacilajiliza.
11 Ninga lono mutemi Nebukadineza yayisolela yisi, baho nase ho calamulila cize ku Yelusalemi, ciwe kutali ne Babiloni hamonga ne walugu we Cisiliya. Na yiyi yo soko yono yikuleka ase cili baha cikaye mu Yelusalemi.”

12 Hanyuma kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha vyose u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yakanhuma
13 mbite kuli wanhu wa Yuda na Yelusalemi, nghawalonjele nitye nhavi, “Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghumuwuzenyi anye nyo wanhu wa Yuda na Yelusalemi, nhawulenyi si mukusaka kuhulika 'ne hamba kugebata ne mafundo gangu.
14 Ninga mulanje vyono i wana wa Yonadabu wamuhulika u sogo yawo, lono yawalonjela vyono walecele lukulu kung'wa divayi. Na mbeka si wang'wezaga divayi, sunga nalu. Ninga ane nikalaga nghulonga nanye du nhondo zose, nanye hamba si mukuhulika yaye.
15 Nghamutumilenyi ne wetumami wangu wawizi kenji na kenji, nawo wakamulonjelenyi vyono mupituce, mugalece metumo genyu mabi, mwitume ze ling'ani. Kanyhi wakamusugulenyi mulece kuyihemba milungu yinji, hamba kuyitumamila, ho musume kujendelela kwikala mu yisi yono namupelenyi nyenye, hamonga ne walezi wenyu. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, anye si mwasaka kuhulika yaye, hamba kuzicelelela zono nghulonga.
16 Ninga mulanje, i wana wa Yonadabu, vyono walibata no kulikamisa lilajilizo lyono sogo yawo yawalajiliza. Ninga anye nyo wanhu nye, hamba musina cihuliko kuli 'ne ng'o.
17 Holeka ku soko yiyo, alu mowona! Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, nomuletelenyi anye nyo wanhu wa Yuda na Yelusalemi, i magazo gose gono nehuwiza. Nomutendelenyi nhavivyo, vyono anye si mukusaka kuwa ne cihuliko kuli 'ne, nghamucemenyi, nanye hamba kumhitula yaye.”

18 Hanyuma, nghawa kuwalonjela wo mulango we Walekabi, vyono Muwaha Mulungu, u Mudaha Vyose u Mulungu wa Isilayeli yateye nhavi, “Anye nyo wo mulango we Walekabi, vyono mwagebata malajilizo gono u sogo yenyu Yonadabu yamulajilizenyi, no kugatyatya mafundo gakwe, hamonga no kwituma zose, nha vyono sogo yenyu yamulajilizenyi.
19 Holeka luno, ane nane no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mudaha vyose, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, ku soko yiyo, nghwihuwiza nha vyono Yonadabu u mwana wa Lekabu, vilo vyose kowa na mwana mu mhuga yakwe, mono kowa kunyitumamila.”


Yelemiya 36

Baluku yakusoma mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, za mu kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo

1 Kukawa mu mwaka we kane wa mu wutemi wa Yehoyakimu u mwana wa Yosiya, u mutemi wa Yuda, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
2 “Alu gwe Yelemiya, wusole kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo, wandice zinghani zose za Isilayeli na Yuda, hamonga ne makungugo gose, zono nakulonjelaga. Wandice zinghani zose zono nakulonjelaga, ku wulawilila ha mwamulizo lono namula kulongana nagwe, lono Yosiya yali yakali yatemile, sunga mwi'zuwa lye zuwali.
3 Na lunji i wanhu wa Yuda, kolo wakuhulika ze magazo mawaha gono nghusaka niwatendele, lunji wopituka wagalece metumo gawo mabi. Nane niwanenele masutya gawo hamonga no wubi wawo.”

4 Ale du, nane nghawa kumucema Baluku u mwana wa Neli, nghawa kuzilonga zinghani zose hali mwene, zono Muwaha Mulungu yanonjelaga, akuno mwene nayo yakuzandika. Nayo Baluku, yakazandika zinghani zose mu kalatasi yiyo mhalinje ye ligolo-golo.
5 Hanyuma, nghawa kumulajiliza Baluku nghatya nhavi, “Alu Baluku, ane no muyago, si nghomboligwe ninjile mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.
6 Holeka nghukunena, agwe wubite wukasome zizi za mu kalatasi yiyi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo, zono ane nali kukulonjeleza nagwe wukuzandika. Wukazisome ha mwande ye wanhu wose, mwi'zuwa lyo kuleka kulya. Wubite wukazisome mumo mu kalatasi yiyi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo, kwe yizi yiwaha muno, i wanhu ho wasume kuzihulika zose, zono Muwaha Mulungu yanonjela, nane nghawa kukulonjeleza, nagwe wukawa kuzandika. Nagwe nhondo wime i hanhu hono wanhu wose wosuma kukuhulika, hamonga ne wanhu wa Yuda wono wowa wazize baha ku wulawa mu vikaya vyawo.
7 Na lunji wosuma kumulomba Muwaha Mulungu, no kupituka no kuzileka zinzila zawo i zimbi. Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu nha kakondya kuwogofya muno i wanhu wawa, kwe likuwo lyakwe liwaha, hamonga no wukali wakwe.”
8 Ale, baho nayo Baluku, yakawa kuzisoma zinghani zose zo Muwaha Mulungu mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, yakatyatya nhavivilya du vyono namulajiliza yetume.

9 Na mu mwezi we licenda wa mu mwaka we 'hano, wa mu wutemi wa Yehoyakimu, lono yali yatemile mu yisi ya Yuda, i wanhu wakaleka kulya, ho watuje wendezwe wo Muwaha Mulungu. No wuleka kulya wuwo, wukawa wuwanojeye wanhu wose wono wekaye mu Yelusalemi, hamonga na wose wono wali wazize baho, wakulawa mu vikaya vya Yuda.
10 Nawo i wanhu lono wali wakondya kwikala, wahulicize, nayo Baluku yakawa kuzisoma zinghani zose zono namulonjelezaga, nayo yakuzandika mu kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo. Nayo yakazisomela zizi zose mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, mu cinyumba ca Gemaliya u mwana wa Shafani, u mwandici wa he 'kumbo lya hacanyha. Ne cinyumba cakwe cici, cali mwi'kumbo lya hacanyha, behi no Mulango mupya we Nyumba yo Mulungu.

Kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo yikusomwa hali wetumami

11 Nayo Mikaya u mwana wa Gemaliya, kanyhi yo mwizukulu kwa Shafani, yali yeza kuzihulika zizi zikusomwa mu kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo, zono u Muwaha Mulungu yalonjile,
12 hodu yakawa kutuluka, yakawa kubita kwi'kulu ko ku cumba co mwandici we likumbo, hono wali wehangulize hamonga i wetumami wose. Na wono wali wali baho, wali wo awa, Elishama u mwandici we likumbo, na Delaya u mwana wa Shemaya, na Elinatani u mwana wa Akiboli, na Gemaliya u mwana wa Shafani, na Zedekiya u mwana wa Hananiya, hamonga ne wetumami wanji wose, wali wali baho.
13 Nayo Mikaya yakawa kuwaloloseza zinghani zose zono yazihulice Baluku yakuwasomela wanhu.
14 Ale, baho, nawo i wetumami wawa, wakawa kumutuma Yehudi u mwana wa Nataniya, kanyhi yo mwizukulu wa Shelemiya, kanyhi yo mwizukulu civu wa Kushi, yabite yakamulonjele Baluku yayilete
15 hali wene yiyo kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo, yono yayisomile hali wanhu. Nayo Baluku yakawa kuyileta hali wene. 16 Nawo wakamutya, “Ale, wikale hasi, wucisomele nase kalatasi yiyo mhalinje ye ligolo-golo.” Nayo Baluku yakawa kuwasomela zose.
16 Ninga yali yeza kukondya kuwasomela zose, hobaho wose wakawa kwilanga munhu na muyagwe akuno wena no woga wuwaha. Wakawa kumulonjela Baluku wakatya, “Yicinojeye cimumanyize no mutemi nghani zizi.
17 Ninga hamba nhavivyo cikusaka wucimanyize nase ta, vyono wuzitujile zinghani zizi zose no kuzandika? Hamba Yelemiya yo yali kukulonjeleza nagwe wukuzandika lo?”

18 Nayo Baluku yakawapitula yakawatya, “Heya mbeka! Yelemiya yo yali kunonjeleza zinghani zizi zose, nane niwa kuzandika no wino, mu kalatasi yiyi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo.”

19 Nawo wakawa kumutya Baluku, “Alu hono yili nhavivyo, alu agwe hamonga na Yelemiya, mutize mubite mukehundice, hamba munhu yunji wo wose yalece kuhamanya hono mowa mwihundice.”

U mutemi Yehoyakimu yakuyikongoga yi yilya kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo

20 Nawo wetumami wakawa kuyisola kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo, wakawa kuyiwika mu cinyumba ca Elishama u mwandici we likumbo. Wene wakawa kubita kwi'kumbo lyo mutemi, wakawa kumuloloseza mutemi zinghani zose.
21 Nayo mutemi yakawa kumutuma Yehudi yabite yakayilete yiyo kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo kuli mwene. Nayo Yehudi yakawa kubita yakawa kuyisola mu cinyumba ca Elishama, yakawa kumusomela mutemi hamonga ne wetumami wose wono wali wemile baho wamupilime.
22 Na mu mapango gago, wali mwezi we licenda, nayo mutemi yali yekaye i hanhu hono ho wuli moto mumo mwi'kulu, yakota moto ku soko vyali vilo vye cinyuli
23 Nayo Yehudi yaza kuwa yasoma kalatasi yidatu hamba yine nhavi, hodu mutemi yakayidemulanga kalatasi yiyo ko mumaje, yakawa kuyigumila mu moto, nayo yikawa kuticimilila lukulu yose mu moto.
24 Ninga nghani yo wizine, vyono mutemi hamonga ne wetumami wakwe wono wazihulika zizi zose, hamba si walajila wukalale yaye mu zinhumbula zawo.
25 Hamba yiwe Elinatani na Delaya, hamonga na Gemaliya, wajela kumukaga mutemi, yalece kuyiticimiza kalatasi yiyo, ninga mwene mutemi hamba si yawacelelela yaye.
26 Hanyuma ya zizo, u mutemi yakawa kumulajiliza Yelameli u mwana wo mutemi, hamonga na Selaya u mwana wa Aziliyeli, na Shelemiya u mwana wa Abideli, waze wanyibate ane Yelemiya, hamonga na Baluku u mwandici wangu. Ninga Muwaha Mulungu, yali yakondya kucihundika.

Yelemiya yakwandika kanyhi kalatasi yinji mhalinje ye ligolo-golo

27 Baho u mutemi Yehoyakimu, yali yeza kukondya kuyiticimiza mu moto, i kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo, yono ane Yelemiya namulonjelezaga Baluku, nayo yakwandika, Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
28 “Gwe Yelemiya, alu wusole kanyhi kalatasi yinji yono yipalinjile ye ligolo-golo, wandice kanyhi zinghani zose zono wali wakondya kuzandika mu kalatasi nongozi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo.
29 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanhuma mbite nghamulonjele mutemi Yehoyakimu nimutye nhavi, ‘Alu Yehoyakimu, vyono wakondya kuyiticimiza mu moto ayilya kalatasi mhalinje ye ligolo-golo, na kanyhi kuwa kumuwuza Yelemiya wukutya, ku sokoci kandika, nha vyono u mutemi wa Babiloni kolo kuza yayihodanje yisi yiyi, yawawulaje ne wanhu wayo wose, hamonga ne zimitugo zayo.
30 Ale nane luno, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghukulonjela nha vyono, agwe mutemi Yehoyakimu, holowa hasina kanyhi hamba monga wa muli wanago, mono kolotema mu wutemi wa Dawudi. Sunga no muvimba wako, si wolozikwa yaye, langa wolotagwa du mwi'hala caka wutowe ne lizuwa, mwi'pango lye misi, na mu cilo namo, wuputwe ne mbeho nghali.
31 Nane nokulipiciza gwegwe ne wanago wose, hamonga ne wetumami wako wose, ku soko yo wubi wono mwitumile. Kanyhi yo nghani yo wizine, vyono gwegwe ne wanhu wa Yelusalemi ne wanhu wa Yuda, hasina hamba yumonga mono yaziceleleye zinghani zangu. Holeka nane luno, nomuletelenyi magazo mawaha nyenye wose, gono nali nakondya kugawaga vyono nomuletelenyi.’ ”

32 Ale, nane Yelemiya nghawa kuyisola kanyhi kalatasi yinji mhalinje ye ligolo-golo, nghawa kumupela Baluku u mwandici wangu. Nayo yakawa kuzandika zinghani zose nha vyono namulonjelezaga nene. Yakazandika zinghani zose du kanyhi, nha vyono zali zinha mu kalatasi nongozi, hamonga no wityatye wayo we zinghani zayo, nha vyono namulonjelezaga nene.


Yelemiya 37

Zedekiya yakulomba wutazi kuli Yelemiya

1 Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, yakamutemya Zedekiya u mwana wa Yosiya, yawe mutemi wa Yuda, yakahalila hanhu ha Yehoyakimu u mwana wa Yehoyakimu.
2 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, mwene Zedekiya ne wetumami wakwe, hamonga ne wanhu wose, si wazicelelela yaye zinghani zono u Muwaha Mulungu yanonjela.

3 Mutemi Zedekiya yakamutuma Yehukali u mwana wa Shelemiya, hamonga na Zefaniya mukohani, u mwana wa Maseya, waze wanonjele nimulombe Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu, ku soko ye nghungugo yetu.
4 Na mu mapango gago, nali nghali si nawopwa mu jela yaye, nali nghali nghujenda-jenda du ako na ako muli wanhu.
5 Ne wasilikale we Wababiloni nawo, wali wakondya kuyipilima Yelusalemi. Ninga wali weza kuhulika nha vyono wasilikale we Wamisili wakuza, nawo wakondya kuwa behi no mupaka wa Misili, hodu wene Wababiloni wakawa kuwusa nghambi yawo behi na Yelusalemi.

6 Hanyuma ya zizo, u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli yakanhuma
7 mbite nghamulonjele Zedekiya nimutye nhavi, “Alu 'gwe Zedekiya, i wasilikale we Cimisili, wakuza kukutaziliza, ninga hamba nhavivyo, wolowuya kanyhi kwawo.
8 Nawo i Wababiloni, wolokuza kanyhi walihome likaya lili, walisole, walipembele moto.
9 Holeka ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghukusugula, nha vyono wulece kwikonga, wuwe kutya nha vyono, i Wababiloni si wokuza kanyhi. Ninga wumanye vyono wolokuza du.
10 Hamba wutye wuwasumilize lipuga lyose lye wasilikale we Cibabiloni, wasigale wasilikale wono wena zinghomele, nawo wawe wagonile du mu mahema gawo, wumanye vyono, wanhu wawo wo wolokwinuka hanyuma, walipembele moto likaya lili lyongomelele lukulu.”

Yelemiya yakwibatwa no kudindilwa mu jela

11 I wasilikale we Cibabiloni, wayiwusa nghambi yawo behi na Yelusalemi, vyono i wasilikale we Cimisili wali wasejelela behi.
12 Ale nane baho, hodu nghawa kuwuka mu Yelusalemi, nghawa kubita ku yisi ya Benyamini, ho nghasole ligawo lye mhuga yangu.
13 Ninga nali neza kufika ha mulango wa Benyamini, baho nghamwagana musilikale mulindilizi, mono yacemwaga litagwa lyakwe Iliya, u mwana wa Shelemiya, kanyhi mwizukulu wa Hananiya. Ale yuyo Iliya, yakawa kunyimya baho, yakanyitya “Alu 'gwe! Wukubita kwihanza ne Wababiloni.”

14 Nane nghamupitula nghatya, “Aa! Ng'o! Ane si nghubita kwihanza ne Wababiloni yaye.” Ninga hamba nhavivyo, yuyo Iliya, si yenda yahulicize yaye, langa yakanyibata yakawa kunhegula kuli wetumami.
15 Nawo i wetumami wakanghalipilanga muno, wakalajiliza nhowangwe, no kutajilwa mu jela, mu kaya ya Yonazani, u mwandici. Hambi nha yo wanoza yiwe yo jela.
16 Wakawa kumbika mu cinyumba ca mwi'kombo. Nane nghekala mumo vilo vinji.

17 Ninga hanyuma yayo, u mutemi Zedekiya, yakatuma wanhu waze wasole, wanhegule kuli mwene kwi 'kulu. Na kuko kwi'kulu, u mutemi yakambuza cinyele yakutya, “Wule, ho yili nghani yo yose yono yilawile kuli Muwaha Mulungu?”

Nane nghamutya, “Heya ho yili. Agwe Zedekiya, kotajilwa mu miwoko yo mutemi wa Babiloni.”
18 Hanyuma nane nghamuwuza nghatya, “Alu mwene mutemi, wubici wono nghusutizye gwegwe, hamba i wetumami wako, hamba i wanhu wawa, wono wulecile sunga wunhajile mu jela?
19 Hamba wali hayi lu i wawizi wenyu wono wamuwililenyi wakutya vyono, mutemi wa Babiloni si kolokuza yakuhome yaye hamba kuyihoma yisi yiyi?
20 Nalu wuhulicize 'gwe go mutemi. Ane nghukunena, wulece kungomoleza kanyhi ku jela, ku kaya ya Yonazani. Hambi hono wungomoleze kuko, ane mbeka nofwila kuko.”

21 Hodu baho, u mutemi yakawa kulajiliza nha vyono mbopwe mwi'kumbo lya mwi'kulu. Nane nekala baho, akuno nghupegwa mukate monga du nhondo zose, wono walawaga ku wateleka mikate, sunga mikate yose nhondo yikamalika mwi'kaya.


Yelemiya 38

Yelemiya yakutajilwa mwi'kombo lyono lisina malenga

1 Nayo Shefatiya, u mwana wa Matani, na Gedaliya u mwana wa Pashuli, na Yehukali u mwana wa Shelemiya, hamonga na Pashuli, u mwana wa Malikiya, wahulika zono nawalonjelaga wanhu,
2 zono Muwaha Mulungu yazilonga yakutya, “U munhu wo wose mono kolosigala mwi'kaya lili, kolofwila mu wulugu, hamba mu nzala, hamba mu matamwa. Ninga munhu wo wose mono kolowuka mwi'kaya lili, yetegule mweneco kuli Wababiloni, ayo langa si kolowulagwa yaye, langa koloponya wumi wakwe.”
3 Nawalonjela kanyhi nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu yalonga yakatya, “Nolitajila likaya lili mu miwoko ye wasilikale we Cibabiloni nawo wolisola.”

4 Ale, nawo i wetumami wakawa kubita kumulonjela mutemi wakutya, “Gwe mutemi, u munhu yuyu, ciba yawulagwe ye. Ku soko u wulonje wakwe wuwu, wukuwanyenyula vilungo i wasilikale wa mwi'kaya, sunga ne wanhu wose wono wasigaye mwi'kaya. Ne zinghani zakwe zizi, si zikuwataza cinji wanhu, langa mwene yakusaka yawatamise du.”

5 Nayo mutemi Zedekiya yakawapitula yakawatya, “Ale, viswanu, mumutendelaje nha vyono mwendile nyenye nyo weneco. Ane si nosuma kumwimilenyi yaye.”
6 Ale, hobaho wakawa kuza kusola, wakawa kunhimya ne milejehe mwi'kombo lya Malikiya u mwana wo mutemi, lyono lyali lili mwi'kumbo lya mwi'kulu. Ninga lyali lisina malenga yaye, langa lyali lina nhope du. Nane nghawa kudodomela mu nhope yiyo.

7 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, Ebedimeleki u Muetiyopiya mupaswe, mono yetumaga mwi'kulu, yahulika nha vyono wanhajila mwi'kombo. Na mwi'daha lilyo u mutemi yali yekaye ha mulango wa Benyamini.
8 Nayo Ebedimeleki yakamubitila mutemi bahalya, yakamulonjela yakatya, “Gwe mutemi, zinghani zono wetumile i wanhu wawa, si nghani swanu yaye. Hambi nha wamutajila Yelemiya mwi'kombo, na ho hanhu mbeka hono kofwila ne nzala.
9 Kanyhi wumanyile nha vyono, sunga na mono 'kaya, musina ndigwa yaye.”
10 Ale, nayo mutemi yakawa kumulajiliza Ebedimeleki yawasole wanhu wadatu waze wambuse mwi'kombo lono nghali sinafwa.
11 Ale, Ebedimeleki yakawa kuwasola wanhu wawo, yakawa kubita nawo sunga ku ku cinyumba ce viwutwa ca mwi'kulu, yakasola masambi-sambi yakagawapa no mulejehe yakagasunja ne milejehe yakawa kumheleza mumo mwi'kombo.
12 Hanyuma yakanonjela yakatya mbicile masambi gago mu zinghwapa, milejehe ho yilece kunghubula. Nane nghanoza nhavivyo,
13 nawo wakawa kunghwega no kumbusa mwi'kombo. Na ku wulawa baho, nhondo wakawa kumbika mwi'kumbo.

Zedekiya yakulondola wulanjizwe kuli Yelemiya

14 Hanyuma kanyhi u mutemi Zedekiya, yakatuma wanhu waze wanyheme mbite kwakwe, lono yali yali ha mulango we kadatu we Nyumba yo Mulungu. Yakanonjela yakutya nhavi, “Alu Yelemiya, ane nghusaka nghuwuze nghani, nane nghukusaka wunonjele ze mbeka zose.”

15 Nane nghamupitula nghamutya, “Ninga mutemi, hono nghulonjele ze mbeka, agwe kombulaga. Na hono nghulajile wulanjize wo wose, agwe si kowucelelela yaye.”
16 Ninga mwene mutemi Zedekiya, yakanyihuwiza cinyele yakutya, “Ane nghwilahiza kuli Mulungu mumi, u Mulungu mono kaciwumifyaga, vyono ane si nokuwulaga yaye. Hamba si nokutajila yaye mu miwoko ye wanhu wono wakukulondola wakuwulaje.”

17 Hodu, nane nghawa kumulonjela zono yalonjile u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mudaha vyose, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, nghamutya, “Hono witegule go mweneco kuli wetumami wo mutemi wa Babiloni, koponya wumi wako, sunga ne likaya lili hamba si lyoticimizwa no moto yaye. Agwe hamonga na we mhuga yako wose mopona.
18 Ninga hono wijelele du wulece kwitegula go mweneco, ale, wumanye nha vyono likaya lili, lyotajilwa mu miwoko ye Wababiloni, nawo woliticimiza no moto, nagwe go mweneco, hamba si kopona yaye.”

19 Ninga mutemi yakamhitula yakatya, “Gwe Yelemiya, ane nghuwogopa i wanyawusi wayetu, wono wakondya kwilunza ne Wababiloni. Lunji yisumice ninjizwe mu miwoko yawo, nawo ho wamhele magazo.”
20 Ninga nane nghamutya, “Ng'o mutemi, si kosuma kwinjizwa mu miwoko yawo yaye. Ninga ane nghukunena wuyihulice nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu. Na zose zowa swanu du hali 'gwe, kokwiponya no wumi wako.
21 Ninga ane ku lubavu lwangu u Muwaha Mulungu kakondya kundajila zono zokwijelela, hono wuleme kwilavya no kubita go mweneco.
22 Na mu wugubulilwe wuwo, ane niwawona wacekulu wanji wono walekwa mwi'kulu lya mu yisi ya Yuda, wakutegulwa kuli wetumami wo mutemi wa Babiloni. Alu wuhulicize nghulonjele zono wali kulonga lono wali mu nzila, wali kutya nhavi,

‘I wendece muno wo mutemi,

alu wamukonga, nawo kanyhi wamusuma.

Ne migulu yakwe luno yakopela mu nhope,

ne wendece wakwe wakondya kumuleka.’ ”

23 Hanyuma kanyhi nghajendelela nghatya, “I wagolece zako wose, hamonga ne wana, wose wolosolwa wategulwe ku Babiloni, nagwe go mweneco hamba si kolokwiponya yaye hali wene. Nagwe kolosolwa no mutemi wa Babiloni wuli muwopwe. Ne likaya lili lyoloticimizwa no moto.”

24 Nayo Zedekiya yakapitula yakatya, “Nane nghukunena, 'gwe Yelemiya, wulece kumumanyiza munhu yunji zinghani zizi. Hambi nha kowa weyambuza wumi wako go mweneco.
25 Na hono i wetumami wahulice du, nha vyono nali kuduwana nagwe, ale, wokuza du wakuwuze cono cali kuduwana cicici. Nawo wokwihuwiza nha vyono si wokuwulaga yaye, hono wuwalonjele zinghani zose.
26 Holeka nagwe nhondo wuwalonjele nha vyono, agwe wali kuza kulomba kuli'ne, ho ndece kukugomoleza kanyhi ku jela ho wukafwile kuko.”
27 Na mbeka i wetumami wose wakawa kuza no kumbuza, ninga nane nghawalonjela zizilya du, zono mutemi yanonjela vyono zo nizilonje. Na ku wulawa baho, nhondo wakawa wasina kanyhi yinji yono wonoza. Vyono hali hasina hamba monga mono yazihulika zono caduwana no mutemi.
28 Nane nghawa kujendelela kwikala du mwi'kumbo lya mwi'kulu, sunga mwi'zuwa lyono Yelusalemi yasolelwa.


Yelemiya 39

U wugwe wa Yelusalemi

1 Na mu mwezi we likumi, wa mu mwaka we licenda, lono Zedekiya yali yatemile mu Yuda, Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, yakawa kuza ku Yelusalemi, hamonga ne lipuga lyakwe lyose lye wasilikale, wakawa kuyihoma Yelusalemi.
2 Na mwi'zuwa lye licenda, lya mu mwezi we kane, wa mu mwaka we likumi na monga, wa mu wutemi wa Zedekiya, baho ho majinji ge likaya gakumulanjilwa.

3 (Na lono Yelusalemi yali yakondya kutekwa, i wetumami wawaha wose wo mutemi wa Babiloni, wakawa kubita, wakawa kutandala ha mulango wa haciligati. Ne wetumami wawo, wali wali hamonga ne wanhu awano. Neligali Salezeli, na Samugali Nebo u muwaha we wapaswe na Selisekimu, hamonga na Neligali Salezeli yunji kanyhi.)

4 Nayo mutemi Zedekiya hamonga ne wasilikale wakwe wose, wali weza kuziwona zono zikuwaziza, hobaho wakajela kwiponya, watize mwi'kaya, mwi'pango lye cilo. Hodu wakawa kutuluka cilo cose, wakakolela ku nzila ye busitani yo wutemi, wakawa kubita, wakakolela ku mulango wono wali wina majinji mejete, wakatiziza ku lubavu lwa kwi'lolo lya Yoludani.
5 Ninga nawo i wasilikale we Cibabiloni, wakawatyatya du, sunga wakawagana behi na Yeliko. Wakawa kumusola u mutemi Zedekiya, wakawa kumutegula kuli mutemi Nebukadineza, kwi 'kaya lya Libula, mu yisi ya Hamati, kono yali mwi'pango lilyo. Nayo Nebukadineza, yakawa kumuling'aniza Zedekiya yawulagwe.
6 Ninga Nebukadineza, yakamula kuwawulaga i wana wa Zedekiya, baho ha Libula, yakawawulajila ha meso du ge sogo yawo. Yakawawulaga hamonga ne wetumami wawaha wose wa Yuda, mwi'pango lilyo.
7 Hanyuma ya zizo, yakawa kumusola Zedekiya, yakawa kumukopozola meso gakwe gose, yakawa kumuwopa ne manyolo, yakawa kumutegula ku Babiloni.
8 Nawo i wasilikale we Cibabiloni, kono nyuma, wakawa kulikongoga likulu, hamonga ne zikaya ze wanhu wose, wakawa kugakumulanga ne majinji ge likaya lya Yelusalemi.
9 Hanyuma ya zizo nayo Nebuzaladani, mono yali yo musilikale muwaha, nayo yakawa kuwasola i watekwa, mu wanhu wono wali wakali wasigaye mwi'kaya, yakawategula ku Babiloni, hamonga ne wanhu wono wali watizizi, wono wali wasigaye wono welunza nayo.
10 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, yawasigaza wanji mumo mu yisi ya Yuda, ko muno i wanhu wono wali waciwa muno, wono wali wasina hamba cinji, na mwi'zuwa lilyo du yakawa kuwagawila i migunda ye mizabibu ne migunda yo kulima.

Yelemiya yakulecelwa

11 Lulo, u mutemi Nebukadineza, yakamulajiliza Nebuzaladani, u muwaha we wasilikale, yakamutya,
12 “Alu, wubite wukamusole Yelemiya, wumukamise viswanu, wulece hamba kumunozeza nghani 'mbi yo yose, langa wumunozezaje du nha vyono mweneco kowa kusaka wumunozeze.”
13 Ale baho, yuyo Nebuzaladani, hamonga ne wetumami wayagwe wanji, nha cinji wa Nebushazibani, na Neligali Salezeli, na wetumami wanji wo mutemi wa Babiloni,
14 wakawa kuza kusola 'ne, mwi'kumbo lya mwi'kulu, wakawa kumbika mu wulanjilizi wa Gedaliya, u mwana wa Ahikamu, kanyhi yo mwizukulu wa Shafani, mono yandima sunga yakafisa ko ku kaya mholile, nghawa kwikala hamonga ne wanhu wangu.

U witumbilo wa Ebedimeleki

15 Nane Yelemiya, lono nali nghali ndindiywe mwi'kumbo lya mwi'kulu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela
16 vyono mbite nghamulonjele Ebedimeleki u Muetiyopiya, nha vyono yalonjile u Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli. Nghawa kumubitila Ebedimeleki nghawa kumulonjela nghatya vyono, u Muwaha Mulungu katya nhavi, “Lanje! Ane nolilipiciza likaya lili nha vyono nalonga, hamba si nolimotya yaye. Na kolo zizi zikwijela, nagwe go mweneco koloziwona kwe meso gako.
17 Ninga ane, no Muwaha Mulungu nolokuponya, hamba si kolotajilwa na mu miwoko ye wanhu wono wukuwogopa yaye.
18 Nolokukamisa, nagwe hamba si kolowulagwa yaye. Kolokwiponya wumi wako, vyono wali wunyitumbiye nene. Vyo nghutya ane, no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 40

Yelemiya yakwikalana na Gedaliya

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakalongana nane kanyhi mwi'pango lono Nebuzaladani u muwaha we wasilikale, yali yakondya kundecela kuko ku Lama. Mu madaha gono nali nasolwa nategulwa kuko ku Lama, akuno mbopigwe ne manyolo, hamonga ne wanhu wanji wa ku Yelusalemi na Yuda, wono nawo wasolwa nha wafungwa, no kutegulwa ku Babiloni.
2 Yuyo u muwaha we wasilikale, yakasola yakanhegula ha cibembo, yakanonjela yakatya nhavi, “U Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wako, yakondya kuyogofya yisi yiyi ne mabi gaga.
3 Nalu yakondya kwituma no kufisiliza zose zono yalonga. Na zizi zose ziwagana, ku soko i wanhu wako wetuma wubi hali Muwaha Mulungu, no kumubedegala mwene.
4 Ninga ane luno, nghukuwopolela zuwali i manyolo gono wuwopigwe mu ciwino cako, nghulece wuli mwelecele. Nagwe wuhagule go mweneco, hono wuwe kusaka cibitane nane ku Babiloni, ale cibite, nane nokukamisa viswanu. Na hono wuwe si wendile cibitane nane, ale hodu, hambi yisi yose yiyi, nha yili mu mawoko gako, nagwe kosuma kuhagula no kubita wukekale hanhu ho hose hono kokwenda gwegwe go mweneco.”

5 Na lono nali nghali si namupitula, mwene Nebuzaladani yakanyitya kanyhi, “Alu wubite kuli Gedaliya u mwana wa Ahikamu, kanyhi yo mwizukulu wa Shafani, u munhu mono u mutemi wa ku Babiloni, yamuwika yawe yo mulongola mu makaya ga Yuda. Wubite wukekalane nayo, muwe hamonga ne wanhu. Hamba hono wende gwegwe, kosuma kuhagula hanhu ho hose du, hono kosaka gwegwe kwikala.” Hanyuma ya zizo, hodu yakawa kumhela zindigwa, hamonga ne vipegwa, yakawa kunghombola, nane nghawa kubita.
6 Nane nghawa kubita kuli Gedaliya ku Mizipa, nghawa kwikalana nayo, cikawa hamonga ne wanhu, wono wasigala mumo mu'si.

Gedaliya mulongola we yisi ya Yuda

(2Wat 25:22-24)

7 Ninga hali ho wali wetumami wanji, hamonga ne wasilikale wanji we Ciyuda, wono si wali wendile kwiyilavya kuli Wababiloni. Holeka wali weza kuhulika nha vyono u mutemi wa Babiloni yamunozize Gedaliya yawe yo mulongola we yisi ya Yuda, kanyhi nha vyono kamunoza yawe yo mulanjilizi wa muli wanhu waciwa wose wono wasigaye, wono si wasolwa no kutegulwa ku Babiloni.
8 Hobaho, Isimayeli mwana wa Netaniya, na Yohanani u mwana wa Kaleya, na Selaya u mwana wa Tanhumeti, ne wana wa Efayi, munya Netofa, na Yezaniya munya Maka, hamonga wose, wakawa kumubitila Gedaliya ku Mizipa, hamonga ne wanhu wawo.
9 Nayo Gedaliya yakawalonjela yakatya, “Ane nghumwilahizenyi nha vyono, anye musina soko yo yose yo kogopa kwilavya no kuwetumamila Wababiloni. Anye, mwikalaje du mu yisi yiyi, mujendelele du kumwitumamila u mutemi wa Babiloni, na zose zowa swanu du muli'nye.
10 Langa ane nojendelela kwikala bahano du, ha Mizipa, ho niwe mulonji wenyu, kolo Wababiloni wakuciziza. Ninga anye, mosuma kunoza divayi nyinji no kuyiwika ciwutwa, hamonga ne mikalo, ne mafuta. Mujendelele kwikala du mu vitumbi vyenyu vyono mwikaye.”
11 Nawo i Waisilayeli wose wono wekalaga ku Mowabu, na ku Amoni, wali weza kuhulika nha vyono u mutemi wa Babiloni kakondya kuwakombola Waisilayeli wanji wasigale, wekale mu yisi ya Yuda, kanyhi na vyono kamunoza Gedaliya yawe yo mulongola wawo.
12 Hodu, wose wakawa kwihanguliza, mu wulawa mu ma'si gono wali weyagaliye wakawa kuwuya ku yisi ya Yuda. Wakawa kumuziza Gedaliya, kuko ku Mizipa. Wakakonghola divayi nyinji muno hamonga ne mikalo ye libahu minji muno muno.

Gedaliya yakuwulagwa

(2Wat 25:25-26)

13 Na hanyuma ya zizo, Yohanani, hamonga ne wawaha wose we wasilikale, wono si wavumila kwilavya no kuwetumamila Wababiloni, hobaho wakawa kumuziza Gedaliya kuko ku Mizipa,
14 wakawa kumutya, “Alu mwene agwe Gedaliya, hongo si wumanyile nha vyono, u mutemi Balisi we yisi ya Amoni, kamutuma Isimayeli yaze yakuwulaje?” Ninga mwene Gedaliya hamba si yehuwila ng'o.
15 Hamonga na zizo, Yohanani yakamulonjela cinyele yakamutya, “Gwe Gedaliya nghukulomba wunghombole, mbite nghamuwulaje Isimayeli, kanyhi hasina hamba munhu monga mono komanya vyono nani mono yamuwulaje. Alu sokoci yene yono yoleka mwene Isimayeli yakombolwe yaze yakuwulaje 'gwe? Ku soko hono nghani yiyi yitumwe, yowambuza i Wayahudi wose wono wakondya kwikongholela hali'gwe, weyagale kanyhi ako na ako. Ne nghani yiyo, yowambuza i wanhu wose wono wasigaye mu yisi ya Yuda, watuje magayo mawaha muno.”

16 Ninga mwene Gedaliya yakapitula yakatya, “Agwe Yohanani, wulecele lukulu kwituma nghani yiyo! Hambi zose zono wukumulonga Isimayeli, nha si za mbeka yaye.”


Yelemiya 41

1 Na mu mwezi wo mupunghati wa mu mwaka wuwo du, Isimayeli u mwana wa Netaniya, kanyhi yo mwizukulu wa Elishama, na wo lulelo lwe citemitemi, kanyhi yo muyagwe ne wetumami wawaha wo mutemi, yakawa kubita ku Mizipa yakamulanje Gedaliya, yena ne wayagwe wanhu yikumi. Nawo lono wali watenga zindigwa, wakulya hamonga na Gedaliya,
2 hobaho Isimayeli hamonga ne wanhu wawalya yikumi, wakawa kuyikwega mimaje yawo mu zinjasulu, wakawa kumuwulaga Gedaliya.
3 Yuyo Isimayeli, yawawulaga i Waisilayeli wose wono wekalaga hamonga na Gedaliya kuko ku Mizipa, hamonga ne wasilikale wose we Cibabiloni wono wejela kuko.

4 Na mwi'zuwa lye kejete, lono nghani yiyi yo wuwulagwe wa Gedaliya yali yikali si namanyika yaye,
5 wakawa kuza wanhu makumi munana, wakulawa ku Shekemu, na ku Shilo, na ku Samaliya. Ne wanhu wawo, wali wacetile zindevu zawo, kanyhi wakayidemulanga myenda yawo, akuno wetemanjile mu miwili yawo. Wakawa wadinghuye ne vilavywa vyawo vyo wusaje hamonga no wuvumba, wakuleta ho wavilavye mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.
6 Nayo Isimayeli yakawa kutuluka ku wulawa ku Mizipa yabite yakawahinjilize, nayo yajendaga mu nzila akuno yakulila. Yali yeza kuwaficila, yakawatya, “Ale, muze mukamuwone Gedaliya.”
7 Nawo baho baho du wali weza kwinjila mwi'kaya, hodu Isimayeli hamonga ne wanhu wakwe wakawa kuwazumhila, no kuwawulaga wose, wakawa kuyigumila mwi'kombo miwili yawo.

8 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, muli wanhu wawa makumi munana, mwali mowali wanji wanhu yikumi, wono wamunena Isimayeli wakumutya, “Gwe wayiko, cikukunena wulece kuciwulaga se! Hambi nha cina viwutwa vyetu vyono civihundice kuko kwi wimbi, nha cinji, ngano, na shayili, ne mafuta, hamonga no wuci.” Hodu nayo Isimayeli yakawa kuwaleka.
9 Ne likombo lilyo lyono Isimayeli yayigumila miwili ye wanhu wono yawawulaga, lyali yikombo yigazi muno muno. Lyo yikombo lyono u mutemi Asa yalihimba, mu vipango vyono yahomwagwa na Baasha u mutemi wa Isilayeli. Mwene Isimayeli, yakalimemya likombo lilyo ne mivimba.
10 Ne wanhu wose wono wasigala kuko ku Mizipa, mwene Isimayeli yakawasola nha wafungwa, i wana we cicekulu wo mutemi, ne wanhu wose, wono Nebuzaladani u muwaha we wasilikale, yawawika mu wulanjilizi wa Gedaliya. Yakawa kutuluka nawo, yakawategula ku yisi ya Amoni.

11 Nayo Yohanani, hamonga ne lipuga lyose lye wawaha we wasilikale, wono wali wali hamonga nayo, wakazihulika zo wubi wono yetumile Isimayeli.
12 Nawo wakawa kutuluka, hamonga ne wanhu wawo, wakawa kumulonda, wakamwagana behi ne malenga mawaha ga Gibeyoni.
13 Nawo i wanhu wono Isimayeli yali yawawopile, wali weza kumuwona Yohanani, yena ne lipuga lye wawaha we wasilikale, wakasangalala muno muno.
14 Hobaho wakawa kupituka no kuwamanyhilila.
15 Ninga mwene Isimayeli hamonga ne wanhu wakwe we makumi munana, wakamutiza Yohanani, wakatijila ku yisi ya Amoni.

16 Nayo Yohanani hamonga ne wawaha we wasilikale, wakawa kuwasola wanhu wose wono wali wasolwa na Isimayeli nha wafungwa ku Mizipa, lono yamuwulajila Gedaliya ne wasilikale wakwe, hamonga ne wanhu we cicekulu wose, ne wana, hamonga ne walume wose i wapaswe.
17 Na vyono wawogopaga muno i Wababiloni, na vyono Isimayeli yali yakondya kumuwulaga Gedaliya, u munhu mono u mutemi wa Babiloni yali yamuwika yawe mulongola we 'si,
18 hodu, Yohanani hamonga ne wayagwe wose, wakatiziza ku Misili, ho wawe kutali ne Wababiloni. Na lono wabitaga ku Misili, wakamula kusimya nghambi yawo ha Cimuhamu, yono yali yili behi na Betelehemu.


Yelemiya 42

I wanhu wakumulomba Yelemiya, yawalombele kuli Mulungu

1 Baho i walongola wose we wasilikale, hamonga na Yohanani u mwana wa Kaleya, na Yezaniya u mwana wa Hoshaya, hamonga ne wanhu wose wawaha ne wadodo, wakanyiziza,
2 wakanonjela wakutya, “Gwe Yelemiya, cikukunena wucinozeze zono cikukuhila! Ase cikukulomba, wucilombele ase wose mono cisigaye. Hambi nha cali cili wenji, ninga luno casigala cili wanhu wacefu du, nha vyono nagwe wukuciwona.
3 Wumulombe u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu, yacilajile nzila yono yinojeye ciyikole, ne nghani yono yocikwila ku witume.”

4 Nane nghawapitula nghawatya, “Ale, viswanu, ane nomulomba u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu, nha vyono musacile nyenye. Na kanyhi nane nomuganulilenyi zose zono mwene kolonga, hamba si nomuhundicenyi nghani yinji yaye.”

5 Nawo wakamhitula wakatya, “U Muwaha Mulungu yo kowa muhomelezi wetu we mbeka, kanyhi mwihuwilwa kuli 'se, hono cilece kugebata malajilizo gono u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu, kokupela wuciletele.
6 Hamba gawe gakucendeza, hamba ng'o, ninga ase comuhulika du u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu, mono cikukunena agwe wucilombele kuli mwene. Hambi nha cimanyile nha vyono hono cimuhulice mwene, nghani zetu zose zowa swanu muno.”

U Muwaha Mulungu yakuwupitula wulombe wa Yelemiya

7 Vyaza kupuluta vilo yikumi, nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu yikawa kunyiziza.
8 Nane nghawa kumucema Yohanani, hamonga ne walongola wose we wanhu wono wali wali hamonga nayo, hamonga ne wanhu wose.
9 Nane nghawalonjela nghatya, “U Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, mono anye munhumile kuli mwene, ho nhegule wulombe wenyu kuli mwene, yakutya nhavi,
10 ‘Hono muwe mbeka mwendile kwikala mono yisi yiyi, ale, nane nomuzenjenyi, hamba si nomumwagulenyi yaye. Kanyhi nomuhadicenyi, hamba si nomukulenyi yaye. Sunga no wugazice wose wono nene nghamuletelenyi, alu wukumhela wukalale wuwaha muno.
11 Holeka luno, mulecele lukulu kumogopa kanyhi u mutemi wa Babiloni. Hambi 'ne nha nili hamonga nanye, nane nomuponyenyi mu miwoko yakwe.
12 Hambi 'ne, nha nili lusungu, nane nomunoza mwene yawe no lusungu kuli'nye, nayo komulecenyi muwuye ku kaya. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.’ ”

13 Ninga hono mutye, “Ase si cikwenda kwikala mu'si yiyi.” Mulece kumuhulika u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wenyu.
14 Na hono mutye, “Aa, ng'o! Ninga ase cobita ku'si ya Misili kono sicowuwona wulugu, hamba kuhulika lizi lyo wulugu, hamba kutuga nzala, canjile ndigwa. Nase cokwikala kuko.”
15 Hono mulonje nhavivyo, nayo u Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yakutya nhavi, “Nye nyo wo musigalila wa Yuda, hono muwe mwakondya kudumula nghani nha vyono mokwikala ku Misili.
16 Ale, no wulugu wono mukuwogopa, womusumenyi, ne nzala yono mwina mahwele nayo, yiyo nayo yomuzizenyi du, nanye wose molofwila kuko ku Misili.
17 Nawo wose wono wakondya kudumula nghani, nha vyono wobita wakekale kuko ku Misili, ale, wolofwila mu wulugu, hamba mu nzala, hamba mu matamwa. Na hasina hamba monga wa muli wene, mono kolopona yaye, hamba kwiponya yaye mu magazo gono nowaletela.”

18 U Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli yakutya nhavi, “Ninga mulanjilile! Hono mwijelele du mubite kuko ku Misili, ale, nane nomukolelwenyi likuwo lyangu hawaha, nha vyono nawakolelwa likuwo i wanhu wa Yelusalemi. Nanye molowa yiwifyo no wizine hali wanhu wose, nawo wolomubedegalenyi, no kulitumamila litagwa lyenyu nha wulijitwe. Nanye hamba si molohawona hanhu baha kanyhi.”

19 Nghawalonjela kanyhi nghatya, “Ninga mumanye vyono u Muwaha Mulungu, yakondya kumulonjelenyi, nha vyono, anye nyo wanhu mono musigazwe mu yisi ya Yuda, mulece kubita ku Misili yaye. Holeka ku soko yiyo, nane nghumusugulenyi, vyono, alu,
20 mweza kwituma nghani 'mbi muno muno. Mumanye nha vyono nyenye nyo weneco, mukasaka ane nimulombelenyi kuli Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wenyu, kanyhi mukehuwiza nha vyono, mokwituma zose nha vyono mwene komulajilizenyi.
21 Nane luno nakondya kumulonjelenyi. Ninga anye hamba si mukusacila lukulu kuzituma zose zono u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wenyu, yanhuma nize nimulonjelenyi.
22 Holeka ku soko yiyo, mumanye nha vyono, anye wose molofwila mu wulugu, hamba mu nzala, hamba mu matamwa, ku yisi yiyo yono mukusaka mubite mukekale.”


Yelemiya 43

Yelemiya yakutegulwa ku Misili

1 Nane nghawa kukondya, kuwalonjela wanhu zinghani zose zono u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wawo, yanhuma nize niwalonjele.
2 Nane nali neza kondya kuzilonga zizo zose, hobaho Azaliya u mwana wa Hoshaya, na Yohanani, u mwana wa Kaleya, hamonga ne wanhu wose i wedunhumuzi, wakawa kunonganga wakutya, “Gwe Yelemiya, awo wulambi wako du. U Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu, hamba si yakulajilize yaye wuze wucilonjele, cilece kubita kwikala ku Misili ng'o.
3 Langa Baluku u mwana wa Neliya, yuyo yo yakukonjile agwe wucigaluce, ho cisolwe no kutajilwa mu miwoko ye Wababiloni, waciwulaje, hamba lunji wacisole wacitegule ku Babiloni.”
4 Holeka ku soko yiyo, i wanhu wose, hamonga na Yohanani, ne wawaha wose we wasilikale, hasina hamba yumonga mono yalihulika lilajilizo lyo Muwaha Mulungu, ho wekale mu yisi ya Yuda.
5 Ninga mwene Yohanani hamonga ne wawaha wose we wasilikale, wakawa kuwasola wanhu wose, wono wali wakali wasigaye mu yisi ya Yuda, wakawa kuwategula ku Misili, hamonga ne wanhu wose, wono wali wawuya, ku wulawa mu makungugo kono wali weyagaliye.
6 We cilume na we cicekulu ne wana, hamonga ne wana we cicekulu wo mutemi. Wakawasola wanhu wose du wono wali walekwa na Nebuzaladani, u muwaha we wasilikale mu miwoko ya Gedaliya, wakasoleleza na nene hamonga na Baluku.
7 Hamba si walihulika lilajilizo lyo Muwaha Mulungu yaye, wene wakabita du ku yisi ya Misili sunga kwi'kaya lya Tapenesi.

8 Na kuko kwi 'kaya lya Tapenesi, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
9 “Gwe Yelemiya, alu wusole mabwe mawaha, wugavumvumice mu swakala, ha mulango we likaya lyo wutemi lya mwi'kaya lili. Na lono wukuzinoza zizi, wuzinozezaje ha meso ge Waisilayeli, nawo waziwone zose zono wuzinozize.
10 Hanyuma wuwalonjele nha vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu we mihanga, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, nomutuma u mwitumami wangu Nebukadineza, u mutemi wa Babiloni, yaze hanhu bahano. Nayo koliwika ligoda lyakwe lyo wutemi, mucanyha ye mabwe gago gono wugazenjeleze, yakome ne lihema lyakwe lyo lukumyo mucanyha yago.
11 Nayo kolo yakuza, koloyihoma yisi ya Misili. Ne wanhu wono wawicilwa kufwa no wutamwa, awo wolofwa ko wutamwa. Nawo wono wawicilwa kusolwa no kutegulwa kutali nha wafungwa, awo nawo wolosolwa wategulwe kutali nha wafungwa. Nawo wono wawicilwa wawulajilwe ku wulugu, ale, nawo wolowulajilwa ku wulugu.
12 Nane nologapembela moto i makumbicilo gose ge milungu ye Wamisili. Nayo u mutemi wa Babiloni, koloyisola milungu yawo, yayipembele mu moto, hamba lunji yaze yabite nayo ku Babiloni. Kanyhi koloyimeha muno yisi ya Misili, yayilece nzelu swee, nha vyono u mudimi kamehaga mhani mu myenda yakwe, kayinoza yiwa yisina mhani.
13 Kanyhi kologatulanga sunga ne mabwe ge cikumbucizo, gono gali kuko ku Heliyopoli mu yisi ya Misili, yazipembele moto ne zinyumba ze milungu ye Wamisili.”


Yelemiya 44

I nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu kuli Waisilayeli, ku Misili

1 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela kanyhi za kuli i Waisilayeli wono wekalaga mu Misili, na mu makaya gose ga Migidoli na mu Tapenesi, na mu Mempisi, sunga na mu zisi zo lubavu lwa kutakama lwe 'si.
2 Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yakalonga yakatya, “Anye nyo weneco mwewonelela i mabi gose gono nagaleta mu Yelusalemi, na mu makaya gose ga mu yisi ya Yuda. Na vyono gekala matongo sunga nalu. Hasina hamba munhu yumonga kanyhi mono yekaye mumo,
3 vyono i wanhu wayo wetuma zimbi muno, wakanghoza 'ne likuwo. Hambi nha wayilavizyaga makumbiko milungu yinji, no kuyitumamila milungu yono wene si wayihembaga ku wulawa na katali, hamba nyenye, hamba ne walezi wenyu, si wayihembela lukulu.
4 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, nane vyo nghajendelela du, kumutumilenyi wetumami wangu wawizi, wono wazaga kumulonjelenyi vyono, mulecele lukulu kwituma makani gago ge cikomaza, gono si nigendile yaye.
5 Ninga 'nye si mwasacila lukulu kuzihulika hamba kuzicelelela yaye. Hamba si mwasaka ho mugalece gago metumo genyu mabi go kuyikumbika milungu yinji.
6 Holeka nane nghawa kulimwaga likuwo lyangu mu makaya ge yisi ya Yuda, sunga na mu zinzila za Yelusalemi, nghawa kugapembela moto. Gakawa kulekwa matongo go kogofya muno ku wulangwe, nha vyono gakwijela sunga nalu.

7 “Holeka nane no Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, ane no Mulungu wa Isilayeli, nghumuwuzenyi, nghutya nhavi, alu nhawulenyi mukwituma i zimbi zono zinha zizi. Hamba nhawule lo mukuhila kwiyiletela nyo weneco wongomele, anye mo we cilume, na we cicekulu, ne wana, sunga ne wana wagalika, ho halece kuwa kanyhi na munhu wo wose wa muli nyenye, mono kolosigala yaye?
8 Alu nhawulenyi mukunghalaza, no kunghoza likuwo so wuhemba vihwani, na so wukumbika milungu yinji, mu yisi yiyi ya Misili, mono mwikaye? Hamba mukwituma zizi ho mwiyongomeze nyo weneco, ne makungugo gose ga mono'nze gamubedegalenyi, no kwitumamila litagwa lyenyu nha wulijitwe ne cevu lo?
9 Hamba mwasemwa lo i zimbi zono zetumwa ne walezi wenyu, ne watemi, mu makaya ga mu yisi ya Yuda, na mu zinzila za mu Yelusalemi, ne walezi wenyu, ne watemi wa Yuda hamonga ne wagolece zawo, hamonga na nyenye ne wagolece zenyu lo?
10 Ninga sunga nalu baha, anye mukali si mukulajila nhumbula yo winenenyhezi yaye. Hamba si mukusaka kumhela cogopo yaye, hamba kuwa ne mikalile yo kutyatya ze malajilizo gangu gose, gono namupelenyi nyenye hamonga ne walezi wenyu.

11 “Holeka aluno nane, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, Mulungu wa Isilayeli, nomulecenyi, niyongomeze yisi yose ya Yuda.
12 Awo nawo i wanhu wa Yuda, wono wasigaye, wono nawo wejelela, wakawa kubita kwikala mu yisi ya Misili, nane nolanjilila sunga niwongomeze wose. Nawo wose, i wawaha ne wadodo wofwila wose kuko du ku Misili, wofwa so nzala, hamba wofwila mu wulugu. Nawo wolowa cinhu ca kogofya muno, sunga ne wanhu wololitumamila litagwa lyawo nha wulijitwe ne cevu.
13 Nane nolowalipiciza wanhu wose wono wekaye mu Misili, nha vyono nayilipiciza Yelusalemi ko wulugu, ne nzala, hamonga ne matamwa.
14 Na holowa hasina munhu wowose we yisi ya Yuda, muli wawo wose wono wasigala no kubita kwikala ku Misili, mono kolopona hamba kuwumipa. Hamba hasina munhu wo wose mono kolowuya kanyhi ku yisi ya Yuda, kono wakusulumila, ho wabite wakekale kuko kanyhi. Hasina hamba monga mono kolowuya kuko, langa wolowuya wacefu du wono wolopona.”
15 Ale, baho, i wanhu wose we cilume, wono wakamanya nha vyono, wagolece zawo wayilavizya makumbiko milungu yinji, hamonga ne wacekulu wono wali wali bahalya, hamonga ne Waisilayeli wose wono wekalaga ku lubavu lwa kutakama lwe yisi ya Misili, wawa lipuga lyose, wakanonjela wakatya,
16 “Gwe Yelemiya, ase cayilemela lukulu nghani yono wacilonjela, mwi'tagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu.
17 Langa ase cojendelela du kwituma zinghani zose, nha vyono calonga. Cojendelela du kumulavizya makumbiko, mulungu wetu we cicekulu wa ku wulanga, no kumujidila vilavywa vyetu vye divayi, nha vyono canozaga ase, ne walezi wetu, ne watemi wetu, hamonga ne walongola wetu, mu makaya ga mu yisi ya Yuda, na mu zinzila za mu Yelusalemi. Na lono canozaga nhavivyo, cikatuga ndigwa nyinji muno, kanyhi cikamoteswa muno, cikawa cisina hamba wugaye wo wose yaye.
18 Ninga ku wulawa lono cali ceza kuleka kumulavizya makumbiko, u mulungu wetu we cicekulu wa ku wulanga, no kuleka kumujidila i vilavywa vyetu vye divayi, hobaho cikawa kucepucilwa vinhu vyose. Ne wanhu wetu, wakawa kufwa, ko wulugu ne nzala.”

19 I wacekulu nawo wakatya, “Nase lono canozaga mikate, no kumulavizya makumbiko u mulungu wetu we cicekulu wa ku wulanga, hamonga no kumujidila vilavywa vyetu vye divayi, cali cilumbanize ne walume zetu, muli zizo zose zono cetumaga.”

20 Nane baho nghawapitula i walume wose, hamonga ne wacekulu muli zizo, nghawatya,
21 “Ninga mumanye, nha vyono u Muwaha Mulungu, hamba si yagasemwa yaye, i makumbiko gono nyenye, ne walezi wenyu, ne watemi wenyu, ne walongola wenyu, hamonga ne wanhu wose du wa mu'si, mwalavyaga mu makaya ga mu yisi ya Yuda, hamonga na mu zinzila za mu Yelusalemi, mwene yagamanyile, hamba si yasemigwe yaye.
22 Kanyhi mulanje nha vyono, ne yisi yenyu yakondya kuwa matongo, hasina hamba munhu mono yekaye mumo. Hambi nha ceza cinhu ca kogofya muno. Sunga ne wanhu weza kulitumamila litagwa lyayo nha wulijitwe. Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu, nha si kosuma kujendelela kuwukangalila u wubi wenyu, hamonga ne mabi genyu gono mukwituma.
23 Holeka sunga ne magazo gaga, gamuzizenyi, ku soko ye vilavywa vye makumbiko genyu, gono mwali kuyilavizya milungu yinji, no kumusutizya u Muwaha Mulungu, na kanyhi vyono si mukusaka kugatyatya ne malajilizo gakwe.”

24 Kanyhi nghawalonjela i wanhu wose, hamonga ne wacekulu, zono u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yawalonjelaga i wanhu wa Yuda, wono wekaye mu yisi ya Misili.
25 Nha vyono yatya nhavi, “Anye hamonga ne wagolece zenyu, mwehuwiza kuli mulungu we cicekulu wa ku wulanga. Mwehuwiza nha vyono, momulavizya makumbiko, hamonga no kumujidila vilavywa vye divayi, nanye mbeka mukawukamisa muno wihuwizo wenyu. Ale, du viswanu! Mujendelele kuwukamisa wuwo wihuwizo wenyu!
26 Ninga luno, muwuhulicizaje u wilahize wangu nane, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, u wilahize wono nghuwilahiziza mu vilungo vye litagwa lyangu, kuli'nye nyo Waisilayeli wose, mono mwikaye mu yisi ya Misili. Nha cinji, ku wulawa lulu, si nghusacila lukulu kanyhi, yunji muli nyenye yetumamile litagwa lyangu mu wilahize, yawe kutya nha vyono, ‘Ane nghwilahiziza muli Muwaha Mulungu mumi!’
27 Nane nolanjilila ta, hono motuga wumoteswe. Ninga hamba si momoteswa ng'o, langa mokongomezwa wose du. Wose molofwa du, wanji wolofwa ko wulugu, na wanji wolofwa so nzala, hasina hamba monga wa muli'nye, mono kolosigala yaye.
28 Langa wacefu du wa muli nyenye, wo wolopona, wawuye ku yisi ya Yuda, ku wulawa kuko ku Misili. Nawo wawo wono wolopona, wo wolomanya nha vyono, nghani ya nani yono mbeka yifisilizwa, hono nghani yangu yo yifisilizwe, hamba nghani yawo.
29 Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghumukangazizenyi nha vyono, mbeka nomulipicizizenyi mono yisi yiyi. Sunga no wihuwizo wangu wo kumongomezenyi wowa wa mbeka.
30 Nane nomutajila Hofula, u mutemi wa Misili, mu miwoko ye walugu wakwe, wono wakusaka wamuwulaje, nha vyono namutajila Zedekiya u mutemi wa Yuda, mu miwoko ya Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, mono yali mulugu wakwe, mono yasakaga yamuwulaje.”


Yelemiya 45

U wihuwizo wo Mulungu kuli Baluku

1 Na mu mwaka we kane wo wutemi wa Yehoyakimu u mwana wa Yosiya, lono yali yatemile mu yisi ya Yuda. Baluku yakawa kuzandika zinghani zose zono namulonjelezaga. Nghamulonjeleza,
2 zose zono u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yazilonga yakutya, “Gwe Baluku,
3 agwe wukutya ti, Woyi he! Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu nha yandeka! Nalu kanyonjelezeza wukalale ne magayo. Sunga nane luno neza kusakala so wugule wangu, hamba si nghuwona wuswanu yaye.”

4 “Ninga ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghuzimwagula zono nazizenga, kanyhi nghuzikulanga na zono nazihadika. Nane nozinoza zizi, mono yisi yose.
5 Alu agwe Baluku, wukwisacila 'gwe zinghani zimbaha? Ng'o ye, wulece kunoza nhavivyo. Hambi 'ne nha noleta wongomezwe wuwaha muli wanhu wose, langa agwe go mweneco wumi wako wopona, ho hose hono kobita. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 46

Wusumwe we yisi ya Misili, ha Kalikemishi

1 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela za we makungugo,
2 yakanziza yisi ya Misili. Zinghani ze lipuga lye wasilikale wa Neko, u mutemi wa Misili, wono Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, yawasuma ha Kalikemishi, behi ne likolongo lya Efulata, mu mwaka we kane wa Yehoyakimu lono yali mutemi we yisi ya Yuda.

3 “I wawaha we Wamisili wakazoza wakutya,

‘Ale, mwiwikanizaje ne zingula zenyu,

mubite ku wulugu!

4 Mutandice zifwalasi zenyu Mwime micilili,

muvwale ne zikofiya zenyu ze cuma!

Munole ne migoha yenyu!

Musoleleze hamonga ne vitwalo vyenyu!’ ”

5 Kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yakuwuza yakutya,

“Alu, yo nghanici kanyhi yiyi yono nghuyiwona?”

“Lanje ta! Weza kuwuya ne nyuma so woga.

Ne wasilikale wawo weza kuwingwa,

wakondya kusumwa so woga,

nalu wakutiza wamanyhile muno muno.

Hamba si wakwilenga kanyhi kunyuma.

6 Ninga wawo wono wakutiza, hamba si wotiga yaye,

ne wasilikale hamba si wokwiponya yaye.

Nako ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, he kolongo lya Efulata,

wokobala, wogwa.

7 Hamba nani lo mono kokwinyasula nhe likolongo lya Nile,

hamba kotya nhe likolongo lyono lilapuce malenga galyo?

8 Yo yiyo yisi ya Misili,

yo yikwinyasuliza hacanyha nhe likolongo lya Nile,

nhe likolongo lyono lilapuce malenga galyo ko ku nyenje.

Nayo yisi ya Misili yikalonga yikatya,

‘Ane nokwiyinula sunga niyigubice yinze yose.

Nogongomeza makaya gose,

hamonga ne wanhu wayo wono wekaye mu makaya gago.

9 Ale, muzilonjele zifwalasi zituluce,

nago i magali go wulugu gakwegwe!

Muwategulaje wasilikale,

ne wanhu wa Kushi na Libiya,

wadinghuye ne zingula zawo,

nawo i wanhu wanghula ku wuguma no 'pinde,

wakulawilila ku Lidiya.’ ”

10 I zuwali, lyo lizuwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

we mihanga, lyono kokwilipiciziza.

Izuwali, kokwilipiciza lilipicizo mu walugu wakwe.

No mumaje wakwe wowalya sunga wigute,

woying'wa ne sakami yawo, sunga wuce.

Hambi izuwali, u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

nha yakuwalavya walugu wakwe makumbiko,

he kolongo lya Efulata, ku lubavu lwa kusumuka.

11 Nawo i wanhu wa Misili,

wakubita ku yisi ya Gileyadi kulondola miti!

Kumbe ne miti yenyu yiyo,

hamba si yikwiliye cinji hamba si yosuma kumuponyenyi yaye.

12 Nago makungugo nha gazihulika ze cevu cenyu,

ne wanhu wose wakondya kuhulika vyono mukulila.

Ne wasilikale wakwikobaza munhu na muyagwe,

nawo wawa kugwizana hasi wose.

U wuze wa Nebukadineza

13 Nayo Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni,

lono yaziza ho yayihome Misili,

u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,

14 “Ale, muyanzaje nghani yiyi, mu makaya gose ga Misili,

na mu Migidodi, na mu Mempisi sunga na mu Tapenesi,

‘Muwalonjele mutye wewikanize, weyidime weneco.

Hambi i vinhu vyenyu vyose,

nha vyokongomezezwa mu wulugu.

15 Alo nhawulenyi kanyhi u mulungu wenyu Apisi we vilungo,

yakondya kugwa?

U Muwaha Mulungu yo yamubamiliza hasi!’

16 Sunga ne wasilikale wenyu,

nha wakondya kwikobala, nalu wagwa hasi,

nalu wakulongana weneco wose wakwiyitya,

‘Nye, alenyi musunhe!

Alenyi ciwuye ku zikaya ku wanhu wetu,

ho ciwutize u mumaje wo mulugu!’

17 “Ale, mumupele Falawo u mutemi wa Misili litagwa yipya,

Yacemwe ‘Nghabuso,

Mono yakondya kucitaga cilejeho cakwe.’

18 Ane yo nene u Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga,

yo nene mutemi kanyhi yo nene Mulungu mumi.

Nha vyono yinha minala ye litunda lya Taboli,

yigasulutiye matunda kucanyha,

na vyono litunda lya Kalimeli,

nalyo lijeye mucanyha ye lilenga,

Ale, ne vilungo vya mono yakukuhoma,

vyo vyowa vinha.

19 Holeka mwiwikanizaje ku wusolwe nha wafungwa,

anye nyo wanhu wa Misili!

Hambi Mempisi nha yowa nha nyika, howa matongo,

howa hanhu hono hamba yunji si kusuma kwikala mumo.

20 Misili yihwanile ndama swanu muno,

yono yakondya kuhomwa no wuhodanji

wono wukulawa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka.

21 Sunga ne wasilikale wakwe wo kulipwa, watimbile du,

wenha ndama si wakusuma kwina no kwihoma,

langa wose wawuya ne nyuma no kutiza.

Hambi lizuwa lyawo lyo wongomezwe,

nha lyakondya kuza, lidaha lyo wuhodanjice wawo.

22 Alu i Wamisili weza kumangha caka,

wakulila nha nzoka, lono i walugu wakuwasejelela,

wakuwatemanga ne zinhemo,

nha vyono wanhu watemangaga mabici,

23 no kumala mbago mbaha.

Ne wanhu wawo, ho wenji wi wakusumika na ku wuwalwe,

ne wasilikale wawo wenji nhe zinzije.

24 Ninga i wanhu wa Misili, wakozwa cevu,

hambi nha wakondya kusumwa ne wanhu

wa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka.

Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

25 U Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga, u Mulungu wa isilayeli, yakutya nhavi, “Noyilipiciza yisi ya Amoni, u mulungu wa Tabesi, ne yisi ya Misili, hamonga ne milungu yayo, ne watemi wayo. Nomusola u mutemi wa Misili, hamonga ne wanhu wose wono wamwitumbiye,
26 niwawice mu miwoko ya wono wakuhila kuwawulaga, nha cinji, kuli Nebukadineza, u mutemi wa Babiloni, ne wasilikale wakwe. Ninga hanyuma yayo, i wanhu wolowuya wekale mu yisi ya Misili kanyhi, nha vyono wanoza mu vilo vyono vipulute. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

U Muwaha Mulungu kolowaponya wanhu wakwe

27 “Nye nyo wanhu wangu, mulece kogopa ye,

anye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli, mulece kogofywa.

Hambi 'ne, nha nomuponyenyi ku wulawa kuko ku yisi yo kutali,

ku wulawa ku yisi yiyo yono mwikaye nha muli wafungwa.

Molowuya du ku yisi yenyu mukekale mupolile,

nanye molowa mucehile du,

holowa hasina yunji wo wose mono kolomogofyenyi kanyhi yaye.

28 Ane nolomuzizenyi nimuponyenyi.

Nane nologongomeza i makungugo gose,

ku wulawa hanhu hose, hono namwiyagazizenyi.

Ninga anye, si nolomongomezenyi yaye.

Hamba nhavivyo,

si nolomulecenyi du ndece kumulipicizenyi yaye,

langa nolomulipicizenyi ko wulipicizwe we ling'ani vyo nghutya,

ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Yelemiya 47

I nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu kuli ze yisi ye Wafilisiti

1 Na lono u mutemi wa Misili yali yakali si nayihoma Gaza, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela zinghani za Gaza
2 yakatya,

“Lanje, malenga gakuza gakulawa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka,

nago gowa yikolongo lyono limemile,

nago golomema no kuyigubika yisi,

na vyose vyono vili mumo mu makaya,

hamonga ne wanhu wono wekaye mumo.

Ne wanhu wololondola wutazi,

ne wanhu wose wa mono'nze, wololila ko wusungu wuwaha.

3 Wolohulika nyhinyinho zo wukanyaje we mabangho ge zifwalasi,

hamonga ne misindo yo lumangho lwe magali go wulugu,

no wulindime we maguludumu gayo.

Mwi'zuwa lilyo, i wasogo zo munhu,

hamba si wolowuya ne nyuma wawasole wana wawo ng'o,

hambi i miwoko yawo nha yolowa yidejepe.

4 Ninga lipango lyakondya kuza lyo kuyongomeza yisi ye Wafilisiti,

kuyiwusiza lukulu u wutazi wose wono wusigaye,

wono wukulawa ku yisi ya Tulo na Sidoni.

Ane no Muwaha Mulungu,

nowongomezeza lukulu Wafilisiti wose wono wekaye mu zimhwani za Kilete.

5 Wukalale wuwaha wakondya kuwaziza i wanhu wa Gaza,

ninga i wanhu wa Ashikeloni, wanyamaye twii.

Alo i wanhu wono wasigaye mu yisi ye Wafilisiti,

wojendelela kulila sunga nili lo?

6 Mukulila mukutya, ‘Woyi hee!

U mumaje wo Muwaha Mulungu!

Alu kojendelela kutemanga sunga nili?

Wuwuye mu njasulu yako, wikale mumo wucehe!’

7 Alu u mumaje ho woceha nhawule,

kumbe nane ho niwupelile mulimo wo kwituma?

Hambi nha nakondya kuwulajiliza wuyihome Ashikeloni,

hamonga ne wanhu wose wono wekaye mu mhwani ze lilenga.”


Yelemiya 48

U wongomezwe we yisi ya Mowabu

1 Azi zo nghani zono u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

yakuyilonjela yisi ya Mowabu yakutya,

“Woyi hee kuli wanhu wa Nebo,

likaya lyawo nha lyakondya kuhodanjika!

Nayo Kiliyatayimu yatekwa,

ne magomawakali gayo ge vilungo gakumulwa,

ne wanhu wayo wakozwa cevu.

2 No lukumyo lwa Mowabu, lwakondya kuwuka.

Nawo i walugu wakondya kuyiteka Heshiboni,

nalu wakwiwikaniza,

ho yalyongomeze likungugo lya Mowabu.

Nago i makaya ga Madimeni gonyamazwa twii.

Hambi ilipuga lye wasilikale nha lyokuziza.

3 Nawo i wanhu wa ku Holonayimu wakulila muno wakutya,

‘Wuwulaji! Wongomezwe!’

4 “Alu Mowabu yakondya kongomezwa,

wuhulice, sunga ne wana wakulila.

5 Kanyhi wuhulice sunga ne cililo mu nzila,

ce mazi gawo go wukalale,

cibitile sunga ko ku Luhiti, i makaya ge magayo,

sunga ko ku Holonayimu.

6 Akuno wakutya, ‘Ale, mumanyhe mbela mbela,

mutize, mwiponye wumi wenyu!’

‘Ale, mumanyhe mwihindile du, nhe zinyhenje za mu nyika!’

7 “Hambi agwe Mowabu,
